Motivation: 现有AI天气模型在预测热带气旋路径方面表现优异，但严重低估其强度，亟需改进强度预测能力。

Method: 基于开源的AIFS-Single模型，提出一种简单的后处理校正方法AIFS-TC，并利用大语言模型（Claude Fable 5）通过自然语言指令自主完成系统的设计与构建。

Result: AIFS-TC在12小时至7天的预报时效内，对最大风速和最低中心气压的预测性能达到业务化先进水平，且对快速增强事件也具有良好表现。

Conclusion: 结合开源AI模型与低成本后处理，并借助大语言模型实现自动化开发，有望加速生命安全相关的早期预警系统在各领域的研究与应用。

Abstract: AI weather models are in the process of revolutionising weather forecasting. While these models have been shown to achieve superior performance to physics-based NWP in forecasting tropical cyclone (TC) tracks, they dramatically underestimate intensity. Here we present AIFS-TC, a simple correction to the AIFS-Single model that is competitive with the operational state-of-the-art for forecasting maximum wind speed and minimum central pressure at lead times of 12 h to seven days. This performance also holds for rapid intensification events. Notably, the entire system was autonomously designed and built by a large language model (Claude Fable 5) in a few hours, directed through a small number of natural-language prompts by a single domain scientist. That the operational frontier can be reached with an open-source AI forecast model (AIFS-Single) and relatively simple, cheap post-processing is significant for TC science, and points to agentic coding as a route to rapid exploration and progress in life-saving early-warning systems in other domains.

</details>


### [78] [Wave-induced erosion and notch development at the Fimbul Ice Shelf front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09965)
*Wenjun Lu,Lotte Wendt,Behnam Ghadimi,Shovon Jubair,Dominique Mouaze,Marianne Font,Rémi Lambert,Harvey Goodwin,Geir Moholdt,Raed Lubbad,Sveinung Løset*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 研究南极冰架前缘水线侵蚀与卫星观测退缩之间的关系，扩展了White (1980)侵蚀模型，结合波谱和破波效应，模拟显示水线侵蚀约110米，但卫星观测到的退缩约为264-266米，模型结果仅为观测值的约42%，差异可能源于未考虑破波后湍流和机械崩解过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解波浪驱动的热侵蚀对南极冰架前缘稳定性的影响，揭示隐藏的水线侵蚀如何导致冰架前缘崩塌和浮冰脱落，明确模型预测与卫星观测退缩之间的差距及其成因。

Method: 在White (1980)侵蚀模型基础上引入分量波谱处理不规则海浪，并采用考虑冰下足部地形的破波感知波形；利用每小时ERA5波浪数据驱动模型，在Fimbul冰架进行模拟；使用Sentinel-2和Sentinel-1卫星数据通过 plateau-break 方法和手动数字化提取前缘退缩进行对比验证。

Result: 模型预测2024年1月7日至2月27日间累计水线侵蚀约110米；卫星观测显示同期前缘退缩达264–266米，模型结果仅为观测值的约42%；在基准假设下（alpha = 1, Delta T_wi = 1°C），模型显著低估退缩量。

Conclusion: 当前模型未包含破波后湍流和机械崩塌过程，可能是低估前缘退缩的主要原因；近冰层热力驱动参数缺乏观测构成主要不确定性；仅靠前缘位置观测无法区分水力与机械贡献，需进一步现场测量与多源遥感结合以改进模型。

Abstract: Ocean waves erode the waterline of Antarctic ice-shelf fronts and carve a thermo-erosional notch. The notch is hidden from satellites, yet it preconditions front collapse and footloose calving, so the link between notch growth and observable front retreat matters for how wave-exposed ice shelves lose mass. We study this link at the Fimbul Ice Shelf, East Antarctica, in January-February 2024. The widely used White (1980) erosion formulation is extended in two directions: a component-wise spectral treatment of irregular seas, and a breaking-aware wave profile that accounts for shoaling over the submerged ice foot revealed by remotely operated vehicle (ROV) profiling. Forced with hourly ERA5 waves, the extended model predicts about 110 m of cumulative waterline erosion over the matched 7 January-27 February window. Satellite observations show considerably more retreat: about 264 m from an S1-guided Sentinel-2 plateau-break method, and 266 m from manually digitised Sentinel-1 fronts over a slightly longer window. Under the baseline assumptions (alpha = 1, Delta T_wi = 1 degree C), the model therefore falls short of the observed retreat by a factor of about 2.4. Part of this residual may be hydrodynamic, since post-breaking turbulence is not represented; part may be mechanical, because collapse and footloose calving can convert notch erosion into larger observable retreat. Unmeasured near-ice thermal driving remains a first-order uncertainty, and front-position observations alone cannot separate these contributions.

</details>


### [79] [Projected climate memory and inherited warm-tail risk in accelerated European summer warming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09966)
*Mauricio Herrera-Marín,Alex Godoy-Faúndez,Diego Rivera*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种经验性的简化动力学框架，用于分解欧洲夏季气温变化中的慢态记忆、可预测成分和同期创新成分，发现地中海状态的记忆显著增加了极端高温事件的风险预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解和预测欧洲夏季气温的变化及其极端事件，研究人员试图区分长期趋势、缓慢变化的状态记忆以及同期变率的影响。

Method: 基于投影算子理论，采用有限因果滤波器、岭正则化预测和逻辑风险模型，对1950-2024年ERA5数据衍生的28个IPCC AR6欧洲子区域夏季指标进行分解，并在2006-2024年期间验证。

Result: 地中海状态的记忆相比仅趋势和ARX基准模型能更好预测夏季平均温度；在简约逻辑模型中，高累积地中海记忆使极端高温事件的概率在1至5年时间尺度上提高约8-11个百分点；区域自举置信区间始终为正，但置换检验的证据强度中等。

Conclusion: 年度预测记忆虽非普适的短期预测指标，但作为一个物理解释明确的继承性风险加载变量，可用于识别易发生极端高温的年份和地区。

Abstract: European summer warming reflects interactions among background change, persistent ocean--land--circulation states, and same-season variability. We develop an empirical reduced-dynamics framework that decomposes regional summer indicators into inherited slow-state memory, its predictable component, and contemporaneous innovation. Projection-operator theory motivates the decomposition, implemented with finite causal filters, ridge-regularised prediction, and logistic risk models. Using ERA5-derived summer indicators for 28 IPCC AR6 European sub-regions over 1950--2024, with validation on 2006--2024, we find that Mediterranean-state memory improves mean summer-temperature prediction relative to trend-only and ARX baselines. The gain over ARX is modest, while moving-average, exponentially weighted, and tempered filters contain similar annual information, indicating that the data identify useful slow-state memory more robustly than a unique kernel shape. Predictable-state reconstruction is ridge-sensitive and therefore treated diagnostically rather than as a forecasting model. The strongest result concerns warm-tail risk. In parsimonious logistic models, high accumulated Mediterranean memory raises predicted upper-tail event probability by about 8--11 percentage points for annual maximum summer temperature, warm-day frequency, and warm-spell duration at 1-, 3-, and 5-year horizons. Regional bootstrap intervals remain positive for all targets and horizons. Circular-shift placebos yield one-sided probabilities of approximately 0.05--0.14 and do not survive strict family-wise correction across nine tests, so the evidence is moderate rather than decisive. Overall, annual projected memory is not a universal short-horizon predictor, but a physically interpretable inherited risk-loading variable identifying years and regions predisposed to warm-tail outcomes.

</details>


### [80] [Annually Modulated Pacific Subtropical Highs Pace the ENSO Cycle: A Hierarchical Framework from Earth Rotation to Atmosphere-Ocean Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09970)
*Yafei Wang*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了地球自转速率的亚年周期振荡通过影响东太平洋副热带高压带，进而调控厄尔尼诺事件的发生与多样性，提出了一种超前14至17个月预测ENSO相位的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解厄尔尼诺-南方涛动（ENSO）的触发与终止机制仍不充分，本文旨在揭示其前期关键动力过程，特别是非ENSO相关的地球自转变化如何影响ENSO演化。

Method: 通过分析东太平洋副热带高压带异常与赤道纬向风的关系，结合地球自转速率（日长变化）的亚年周期信号，利用交叉相关和偏相关方法识别其对ENSO的前兆作用，并构建预测指数。

Result: 发现日长变化的0.5至1年频带与ENSO无显著相关（相关系数<0.05），但能提前驱动副热带高压异常，进而控制赤道风场和海温演变；该信号在事件年前一年7月已完备，可提前14–17个月确定ENSO相位；成熟期ENSO通过形成Mirror-SIGMA环流模式反作用于大气，中断前兆链，完成闭环。

Conclusion: ENSO的启动受地球自转引起的亚年周期大气质量重新分布调控，经由东太平洋副热带高压带传导，构成可预测的前兆路径，而ENSO成熟后通过大尺度环流重组关闭该路径，形成自我终结机制。

Abstract: The El Nino-Southern Oscillation drives Earth's dominant interannual climate signal, yet what initiates an event and what ends it remain incompletely understood. Here we show that anomalies of the eastern Pacific subtropical high (EPSH) belts, marched through the year by the annual cycle, precede and govern the equatorial zonal wind near the dateline, which sets subsequent sea surface temperature evolution. The Northern Hemisphere ridge triggers events in boreal spring, the Southern Hemisphere ridge sustains them in austral spring, and their synchrony decides ENSO diversity. Preceding the ridges lies a sub-annual oscillation in Earth's rotation rate (length of day, 0.5 to 1 yr band) that redistributes atmospheric mass poleward three times once its phase aligns with the annual cycle, in August, in December, and in the following May, each displacement carrying the response further east and poleward until the significant centers overlie the ridges themselves. Crucially, this band is deaf to ENSO: its cross-correlation with NINO3 never exceeds 0.05 at any lag, whereas the classical ENSO-driven rotation response resides in the 2 to 10 yr band at 0.40. The band therefore reads forward, and partial correlation identifies the EPSH as the conduit: the direct path from rotation to ocean vanishes once the ridges are held fixed. Complete by the July preceding the event year, the index fixes the phase of the coming event fourteen to seventeen months ahead, though not its amplitude. At maturity the event reorganizes the Hadley and Walker circulations into the Mirror-SIGMA pattern, disrupting the upstream chain and closing the cycle.

</details>


### [81] [Rescene: band-limited stochastic forcing turns a frozen neural weather operator into a climate emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09971)
*Minjong Cheon*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: Rescene是一个围绕冻结的视觉变换器操作符的小型参数包装器，通过结合确定性慢时钟和生成式头部，实现了长期稳定且无漂移的天气预报，同时恢复了日变率并保持了小尺度现象的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习天气预报模型在超出训练范围后会出现不稳定、漂移或失去季节周期的问题，重新训练成本高昂。因此，研究旨在探索如何从一个完全冻结的主干模型中恢复出稳定的长期预测能力。

Method: 提出Rescene模型，包含一个0.33M参数的确定性“慢时钟”模块，将预测引导至与日期相关的气候态，以及一个0.06M参数的生成式头部，每一步添加频谱塑造的随机扰动；整体仅0.4M参数，包裹在一个1.5度、6小时步长的冻结视觉变换器上，基于ERA5数据开发。

Result: 确定性部分可稳定运行数十年但将日变率压缩到40%；加入生成头后，日变率恢复至观测的126%（Z500）和130%（MSLP），模式相关系数达0.89和0.92，阻塞频率恢复82%，集合校准良好（7–90天离散-技巧比0.78–0.97），100年积分无明显漂移（+0.008±0.014K/世纪）；尽管扰动限于k≤20，但更高波数的能量由冻结操作符主导（k≥40时能量供给为扰动的28倍，格点尺度增长率比行星尺度高247倍）。

Conclusion: Rescene证明即使主干模型完全冻结，也能通过轻量级结构设计实现长期稳定、高保真、无漂移的天气-气候预报，为构建可持续的地球系统模型提供了新路径。

Abstract: Over the past few years, the rapid development of machine learning (ML) models for weather forecasting has produced deterministic models whose medium-range skill matches or exceeds that of the European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts (ECMWF)'s high-resolution forecast (HRES). However, when these models are integrated freely beyond the horizon they were trained for, they blow up, drift, or lose their seasonal cycle, and retraining them for stability is expensive. We therefore ask what can be recovered from a strictly frozen backbone. We present Rescene, a 0.4 M-parameter wrapper around a frozen 1.5 degree, 6-hourly vision-transformer operator, developed using ERA5 reanalysis data and comprising a deterministic "slow clock" (0.33 M) that blends the forecast toward a lead-aware day-of-year climatology and a generative head (0.06 M) that adds a spectrally shaped stochastic perturbation at every step. The performance evaluation demonstrates that the deterministic wrapper alone is stable for decades but collapses daily variability to 40% of ERA5. Adding the generative head restores 126% (Z500) and 130% (MSLP) of the observed daily variability with pattern correlations of 0.89 and 0.92, recovers 82% of the observed blocking frequency, keeps the ensemble calibrated (spread-skill ratio 0.78-0.97 from day 7 to day 90), and integrates for 100 years with no detectable drift (+0.008 +/- 0.014 K per century). Moreover, because the perturbation is band-limited to total wavenumber $k \le 20$, the small scales are never forced, yet realistic $k \ge 20$ power is sustained: a direct decomposition of the 6-hourly energy budget shows that the frozen operator supplies 28 times more energy than the perturbation at $k \ge 40$, with a fractional growth rate 247 times larger at the grid scale than at planetary scales.

</details>


### [82] [Do AI weather models miss extremes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09972)
*Marvin Vincent Gabler,Roberto Molinaro,Niall Siegenheim,Henry Martin,Mark Frey,Niels Poulsen,Philipp Seitz,Olivier Lam*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: AI天气模型在极端条件下的表现并不普遍劣于传统模型，特定AI模型在风速、温度、太阳能和降水预测中表现出优于物理模型的技能，表明极端天气预报性能不足是特定模型问题而非AI模型整体缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在验证第一代AI天气模型是否在极端条件下普遍表现不佳，挑战当前对AI模型在极端天气预测中能力不足的普遍认知。

Method: 通过对11种物理和AI气象预报系统进行验证，使用欧洲地面观测站数据（包括风速、温度、太阳辐射和降水）评估其在不同气候条件下的平均绝对误差（MAE），并与ECMWF IFS在ERA5气候态下的表现进行对比分析。

Result: AI模型在极端条件下的表现并未普遍落后：某些AI模型在风速（如Jua EPT-2.1 Europa）、温度（Jua EPT-2 HRRR）、太阳能（Jua EPT-2.1 Helios）和降水（Jua EPT-2 Reasoning）预测中优于传统模型；而部分物理模型（如NOAA GFS）在高温尾部表现更差。所有模型均表现出向观测分布中心偏移的共同条件偏差。

Conclusion: AI天气模型在极端天气预测中的相对技能不足并非其类别性缺陷，而是特定模型的设计或训练问题；改进应聚焦于减少共有的条件偏差，而非单纯提升AI模型尾部表现。

Abstract: First-generation AI weather models are often reported to underperform at extremes, mostly in reanalysis-based evaluations of deterministic regression systems. We verify eleven physical and AI forecast systems against European synoptic, solar, and rain-gauge stations over ten months for 10 m wind, 2 m temperature, hourly shortwave accumulation, and hourly precipitation, scoring mean absolute error (MAE) against ECMWF IFS in ERA5 1991-2020 climatological regimes. Among these systems, AI models do not show a uniform relative-skill deficit in the tails. Jua EPT-2.1 Europa leads all-conditions wind (+8.4%), while Jua EPT-2 HRRR leads temperature overall (+12.1%) and in the heat regime (+19.6 +/- 2.2%). EPT-2.1 Europa and DWD ICON Global lead at gale-force wind. Jua EPT-2.1 Helios leads solar overall (+10.2 +/- 1.7%), in overcast conditions (+16.4 +/- 3.4%), and in the clear-sky tail (+24.8 +/- 5.4%). For precipitation, three Jua models gain 14-15% at moderate intensity and 9-11% at P75-P95; EPT-2 Reasoning remains ahead above P95 (+1.7 +/- 0.5%). Failures are model-specific: ECMWF AIFS loses 4.9 +/- 2.0% in the heat tail, while NOAA GFS loses 22.8 +/- 2.0% there. Every model, including numerical weather prediction systems, shows a shared conditional bias toward the centre of the observed distribution, with an inter-model spread several times smaller than the shared signal. Missing relative skill at extremes is therefore not a property of AI weather models as a class, but of particular AI and physical models.

</details>


### [83] [Atmospheric Conditions, Electric Fields, and Thunderstorm Ground Enhancements at Aragats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09973)
*A. Chilingarian,N. Manukyan,Q. Piliposyan*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于2012至2025年在Aragats高海拔站的观测数据，分析了雷暴地面增强（TGE）事件的发生条件及气候变化趋势，发现TGE的发生受雷云电场和云层距离共同影响，且当地变暖趋势不能仅由太阳辐射变化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究雷暴地面增强现象的发生机制以及区域气候环境变化的原因，特别是在高海拔地区气象、电场与粒子通量之间的相互作用。

Method: 利用Aragats站3200米海拔的一分钟分辨率观测数据，从610次TGE事件中筛选出284次高质量事件，分析其与气象条件、近地电场、云底高度及太阳辐射的关系，并评估气温和辐射的长期趋势。

Result: 强近地电场单独不足以导致频繁TGE；5月因云底低且电场强，TGE发生率高，显示云-探测器距离与TGE密切相关；年均气温呈显著上升趋势，而白天太阳辐射呈下降趋势。

Conclusion: TGE的发生由雷云带电强度和云与探测器之间距离共同决定；近年来Aragats地区的升温不能归因于短波太阳辐射增加，暗示其他驱动因素如温室效应可能起主导作用。

Abstract: The Aragats High-Altitude Research Station (3200 m a.s.l.) combines meteorological, atmospheric electric, and particle flux measurements, providing a unique setting for investigating thunderstorm ground enhancements (TGEs) and recent environmental variability. We analyze one-minute observations from 2012 to 2025, first focusing on the meteorological and electric-field conditions controlling TGEs and then on trends in air temperature and daytime solar radiation. From an archive of 610 TGEs, a quality-controlled subset of 284 events with particle flux enhancements larger than 10 percent was selected. Comparisons of May, August, and October show that strong near surface electric fields alone are insufficient for frequent TGEs. May combines strong-field conditions with low cloud bases and shows a pronounced inverse relation between TGE occurrence and cloud-base distance. Thus, TGE occurrence is jointly controlled by thundercloud electrification and cloud-to-detector distance, while the recent warming tendency cannot be attributed to increased measured short-wave radiation. The annual mean air temperature shows a statistically significant positive trend, whereas daytime solar radiation shows a significant negative trend. Thus, warming at Aragats cannot be explained solely by variations in solar radiation.

</details>


### [84] [A black-box-model-enhanced interaction method for water-wave scattering by large group of arbitrary-shaped ice floes in Arctic route planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09975)
*Chongwei Zhang,Hongli Yang,Peng Wu,Peng Lu,Dezhi Ning*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种增强交互（EI）方法和波分量检测（WCD）模型，用于高效预测北极航路中大量冰 floes 间的波浪场，具有高效率和实用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以处理复杂三维冰 floes 的衍射传递矩阵（DTM），限制了北极航线中波浪场的高效预测，因此需要一种通用且可实施的新方法。

Method: 提出了基于黑箱模型的波分量检测（WCD）方法来构建衍射转移矩阵（DTM），并在交互理论框架内发展了增强交互（EI）方法，结合动态规划实现航路优化。

Result: EI方法可在普通计算机上用不到1.5小时完成1800个冰floe（144万个边界元）的波场计算；应用于1561个冰floe时，船舶遭遇的平均波幅可降低至入射波幅的一半。

Conclusion: EI方法高效、通用，能准确预测复杂冰floe群中的波浪场，并可用于优化北极航行路线以利用冰floe的遮蔽效应。

Abstract: This study develops an enhanced interaction (EI) method for efficient prediction of the water-wave field among a large group of ice floes in Arctic route planning. A novel black-box model, termed the wave component detection (WCD) method, is proposed for constructing the diffraction transfer matrix (DTM) within the framework of interaction theory. The DTM, which is conventionally mathematically intractable for three-dimensional ice floes with arbitrarily complex geometry, can now be determined using this readily implementable and universally applicable approach. Without loss of generality, four ice-floe shapes are taken as example models to demonstrate the capability of the EI method. Operation rules are recommended for the practical implementation of the EI method. The error range of the EI method is identified in scenarios with multiple ice floes of different sizes and distances.The super-high efficiency of the EI method is demonstrated in cases involving an ultra-large group of ice floes. It takes less than 1.5 hours to calculate wave amplitudes at 160,000 locations in the wave field of 1,800 ice floes (based on 1,440,000 boundary elements) on an ordinary personal computer with a 2017-released CPU. Based on the wave field predicted by the EI method, users can take advantage of the wave-sheltering effect of the ice floes to optimize routes. For demonstration, the dynamic programming strategy is used to recommend optimized navigation routes among 1561 ice floes of mixed shapes. The average wave amplitude the ship encounters can be reduced to about half of the incident wave amplitude.

</details>


### [85] [The iSTORM Instrument for Airborne Measurements of Gamma-Ray Emissions from Thunderstorms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09990)
*Daniel Shy,J. Eric Grove,Bernard Phlips,Alena Schell,Mary Johnson-Rambert,Mason Quick,David Corredor,Scott Podgorny,Roy Salinas,Mitch Davis*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: iSTORM是一个用于研究雷暴产生的伽马射线瞬变现象（如glows、TGFs和新发现的FGFs）的机载伽马射线谱仪，搭载于NASA ER-2飞机，在ALOFT任务中成功观测到多种高能辐射现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在雷暴环境中原位探测和研究伽马射线瞬变事件（如glows、TGFs和FGFs），填补大气高能物理过程的观测空白。

Method: 设计并构建了一个由32个CeBr3闪烁体和硅光电倍增管（SiPM）组成的伽马射线谱仪iSTORM，部署于NASA ER-2高空飞机上，实现约250 keV至5 MeV的探测能量范围和157 cm²的总几何面积。

Result: 作为ALOFT任务中的两个伽马射线仪器之一，iSTORM成功记录到了glows、TGFs以及新发现的闪烁伽马射线闪光（FGFs）。

Conclusion: iSTORM在飞行条件下具备良好的灵敏度和稳定性，能够有效探测雷暴相关的高能辐射现象，为理解雷暴中的粒子加速机制提供了关键观测数据。

Abstract: The in-Situ Thunderstorm Observer for Radiation Mechanisms (iSTORM) is a gamma-ray spectrometer to study gamma-ray transients originating from thunderstorms, such as glows and terrestrial gamma-ray flashes (TGFs). It is designed and built by the U.S. Naval Research Laboratory for deployment aboard a NASA ER-2 aircraft. Using an array of 32 one-inch-diameter $\mathrm{CeBr}_3$ scintillators read out with silicon photomultipliers (SiPMs), the instrument achieves an energy range of $\sim 250 \ \mathrm{keV}$ to $5 \ \mathrm{MeV}$ under flight conditions, with a total geometrical area of $157 \ \mathrm{cm^2}$. One of two gamma-ray instruments in the ALOFT campaign, iSTORM recorded glows, terrestrial gamma-ray flashes (TGFs), and the newly discovered flickering gamma-ray flashes (FGFs).

</details>


### [86] [Deep Learning-Based Statistical Downscaling of Sea Surface Temperature Using a Residual Corrective Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10022)
*Onkar Jadhav,Tim French,Ivica Janekovic,Nicole L. Jones,Matthew Rayson*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度学习的残差校正神经网络（RCNN）框架，用于对澳大利亚西海岸海表温度进行统计降尺度，有效提升了季节性气候预测中对细尺度特征（如涡旋和锋面）的捕捉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球气候模型缺乏足够分辨率来准确模拟沿海海洋对大气强迫的响应和细尺度海温变化，动力降尺度计算成本过高，难以广泛应用。

Method: 采用U-Net生成初始高分辨率海温估计，结合残差校正方法逐步优化预测结果，并引入定制损失函数以增强对极端事件的预测能力。目标数据来自区域海洋模型ROMS。

Result: 将ACCESS-S2的海温预测水平分辨率从25公里提升至2公里，成功识别出2011年海洋热浪中的细尺度异常，显著优于原始数据。

Conclusion: 所提出的RCNN框架在计算效率与预测精度之间取得良好平衡，适用于沿海影响评估和海洋生态系统研究。

Abstract: The large-scale oceanic and atmospheric forecasts provided by global climate models typically lack sufficient resolution to accurately capture the response of the coastal ocean to atmospheric forcing and coastal circulation that drive fine-scale SST variability. Dynamical downscaling is computationally prohibitive, when applied to extensive coastlines, predictive ensembles, or long time periods. Therefore, this work presents a statistical downscaling of sea surface temperature (SST) from the seasonal coupled ocean-atmosphere forecast system (ACCESS-S2) using machine learning techniques. This study proposes a novel deep learning framework that uses a U-Net to generate an initial high-resolution SST estimate, which is subsequently refined using a residual corrective approach. The target SST fields are derived from the Regional Ocean Modeling System (ROMS). This two step approach called Residual Corrective Neural Network (RCNN) progressively refines initial U-Net predictions by incorporating dynamically scaled residuals at each step, enabling accurate capture of broad patterns and fine-grained features such as eddies and fronts. We also introduce a custom loss-assisted RCNN variant to improve performance during extreme events, which may be absent from training data due to climate-driven shifts in SST extremes. The framework efficiently downscales SST along the west coast of Australia. A 2011 marine heatwave case study shows that the RCNN improves ACCESS-S2 SST predictions by increasing horizontal resolution from 25 km to 2 km, enabling identification of fine-scale anomalies unresolved in the ACCESS-S2 dataset. This balance between computational efficiency and accuracy supports applications in coastal impact assessment and marine ecosystem studies.

</details>


### [87] [Stochastic Emulation of a Fully Coupled Preindustrial E3SMv3 Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10277)
*Elynn Wu,James P. C. Duncan,Troy Arcomano,Jeremy McGibbon,Oliver Watt-Meyer,Christopher S. Bretherton,Naser Mahfouz,Claudia Tebaldi,Luke Van Roekel,Andrew Roberts,Wuyin Lin,Finn Rebassoo,Jean-Christophe Golaz,Peter M. Caldwell*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SamudrACE框架的E3SM v3随机耦合模拟器，结合了大气（ACE2S）和全深度海洋（Samudra）模拟器，通过概率目标训练，在保持气候均态高保真度的同时，有效再现了多时间尺度的内部变率，尤其在ENSO谱、海温异常和海冰变化方面表现优异，但在极端热带降水事件外推上仍有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更真实地模拟气候系统中的内部变率，特别是在多时间尺度上的表现，研究旨在开发一种能够替代确定性模型的随机耦合气候模拟器，以更好地表征大气对海洋的扰动作用。

Method: 基于SamudrACE框架，将确定性大气模拟器替换为随机版本ACE2S，并与全深度海洋模拟器Samudra耦合；使用105年的工业前控制模拟数据进行训练，并采用概率目标函数进行微调，使大气成为海洋的内部变率来源。

Result: 在独立的400年数据上评估显示，该模拟器能以远小于现有模式-观测差异的偏差重现E3SMv3的平均气候状态；相比确定性基线，其在ENSO功率谱、涡旋丰富的海温异常及边缘冰区海冰变率等方面显著提升了内部变率的维持能力；日降水可准确再现至99.99百分位，但对最罕见的热带极端降水仍低估。

Conclusion: 随机耦合模拟器能在长期变率模拟中实现高保真度，是气候模拟的一种有前景的替代方案，但对外推未见极端事件的能力仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: We present a stochastic coupled emulator of E3SM version 3, built on the SamudrACE framework, which couples an atmosphere emulator (ACE2) with a full-depth ocean emulator (Samudra). We replace the deterministic atmosphere emulator with its stochastic counterpart, ACE2S, and fine-tune the coupled system with a probabilistic objective, so that the atmosphere acts as a source of internal variability for the ocean. Trained on 105 years of a pre-industrial control simulation and evaluated on an independent 400 years, the emulator reproduces E3SMv3's mean climate state with biases much smaller than existing model-to-observation differences. Relative to a deterministic baseline, stochastic training maintains internal variability across timescales, most notably in the ENSO power spectrum, eddy-rich SST anomalies, and sea ice variability in the marginal ice zone. The emulator captures daily precipitation accurately up to the 99.99th percentile, but underestimates the rarest tropical extremes. These results show that stochastic coupled emulators can reproduce long-timescale variability with high fidelity, while extrapolation to unseen extremes remains a key challenge.

</details>


### [88] [Observational Evidence Revises Presumed Large Ozone Worsening from Nitrogen Oxides Cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10399)
*Xiang Weng,Xiao Lu,Jiawei Li,Grant Forster,Jessica Chapman,Beckie George,Yunbo Lu,Guowen He,Haofan Wang,Jingcheng Lai,Peer Nowack*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 通过结合机器学习与观测约束，研究揭示了中国城市氮氧化物（NOx）减排对夏季臭氧污染的积极影响，纠正了部分模型预测的臭氧恶化结果，表明持续NOx控制有助于缓解臭氧污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有空气质量模型在NOx减排对臭氧影响上的矛盾结论，明确大气化学机制并为污染控制政策提供可靠指导。

Method: 采用集成机器学习框架并结合观测数据约束，模拟30% NOx减排下的臭氧响应，并将该约束扩展至10-60%的减排范围以诊断臭氧敏感性变化。

Result: 在30% NOx减排情景下，大多数城市地区臭氧浓度下降，仅2015年7月出现小幅上升；长期来看，2015–2023年间NOx控制对臭氧缓解的作用不断增强，惠及人口比例上升。

Conclusion: 持续的NOx排放削减带来的臭氧缓解效益大于许多模型预测的结果，应继续推进NOx控制政策。

Abstract: Many air quality models indicate that rapid reductions in nitrogen oxides (NOx), without comparable controls on volatile organic compounds, have worsened summertime ozone pollution in urban China, producing a short-term strong ozone penalty. Other models, however, simulate the opposite response, suggesting that cutting down NOx has already helped mitigate ozone pollution. This contradiction obscures understanding of atmospheric chemistry and weakens guidance on control policy design. Here, we reconcile this disagreement and reveal the underestimated benefits of NOx emission reductions using a machine learning framework integrated with an observational constraint. We first constrain ozone responses under a 30% NOx reduction, comparable to the magnitude of NOx emission declines across major Chinese city clusters between 2015 and 2023. The constrained results indicate that ozone decreases prevail across urban China, with only small increases mainly in July 2015. This challenges the widespread ozone worsening that many models predict. We then extend the constraint across 10-60% NOx reductions, establishing its use for rapid ozone sensitivity diagnosis without exhaustive scenario modeling. This diagnosis shows that sustained NOx control increasingly favored ozone mitigation during 2015-2023, benefiting a growing share of China's population. These results underscore that continued NOx reductions can deliver larger ozone mitigation benefits than many models suggest.

</details>

### [89] [Complexity of Local Observation Consistency in Discrete-Event Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10111)
*Tomáš Masopust*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在部分观测下的分层和多智能体监督控制中，局部一致性条件（LOC和LROC）的计算复杂性，证明了对于非确定性系统二者均为PSpace完全问题，而对于确定性系统可在多项式时间内判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部一致性条件（LOC和LROC）在分层与多智能体控制中至关重要，但其验证复杂性尚未明确，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过形式化分析和复杂性归约方法，研究LOC和LROC在非确定性和确定性系统中的计算复杂性。

Result: 证明了LOC和LROC在非确定性系统下是PSpace-complete，在确定性系统下可在多项式时间内判定。

Conclusion: 本文明确了LOC和LROC的计算复杂性边界，为实际系统中抽象验证的可扩展性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Hierarchical and multi-agent supervisory control under partial observation relies on local consistency conditions between a plant and its abstraction: local observation consistency (LOC) for the projection abstractions of hierarchical control, and local relabeling observation consistency (LROC) for the relabeling abstractions of multi-agent systems. Both are companions to a global condition and both have been used without their verification complexity being settled. We show that both are PSpace-complete for nondeterministic plants and decidable in polynomial time for deterministic plants.

</details>


### [90] [A Pragmatic Guide to Building Conservative Discrete Abstractions of Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10254)
*Jordan Peper,Krish Kapadia,James Gast,Ethan Howes,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保守构造的、模块化的离散抽象构建流程，用于闭环动态系统的符号模型检验，通过四个步骤实现对连续状态系统的有效且可靠的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保持可扩展性的同时确保对连续状态系统进行形式化验证的可靠性，需要避免常见的抽象不充分或过度悲观等问题。

Method: 采用四步模块化工作流：(i) 状态空间划分与抽象函数设计；(ii) 基于轴对齐边界框、多面体或带PAC覆盖保证的采样方法构建保守转移关系；(iii) 使用认证删除和反例引导的抽象精化消除虚假转移；(iv) 采用“可能-必须”语义进行LTL规范的可靠提升。

Result: 在三个案例研究中实现了端到端验证，展示了不同设计选择对抽象结构、运行时间和验证结果的影响。

Conclusion: 该流程能够在保证抽象保守性的前提下有效支持复杂信息物理系统的性质验证，提升模型检验的实用性与可信度。

Abstract: Symbolic model checking is an effective approach for verifying semantically rich temporal-logic properties of cyber-physical systems, but it hinges on discretizing continuous-state dynamics into a finite-state abstraction. To transfer verification guarantees from the abstract model to the concrete CPS, the abstraction must conservatively approximate the concrete state space and behaviors. Hence, model-builders must maintain this soundness while balancing pessimism with tractability. However, they face several common pitfalls such as under-approximating the state space, under-approximating transitions, unsound pruning of "degenerate" behaviors, and improper specification lifting. This tutorial presents a pragmatic, conservative-by-construction workflow for building discrete abstractions of closed-loop dynamical systems. The workflow consists of four modular steps with interchangeable subroutines: (i) state-space partition and abstraction-function design, (ii) conservative transition construction via axis-aligned bounding boxes, polytopes, or sampling with PAC coverage certificates, (iii) mitigation of spurious transitions and self-loops using certified erasure and counterexample-guided abstraction refinement, and (iv) sound lifting of LTL specifications using may-must semantics. We demonstrate the end-to-end pipeline on three case studies and report how these design choices affect abstraction structure, runtime, and verification outcomes.

</details>


### [91] [Switching Observers for Linear Systems: Beyond Individual Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10298)
*Masoud S. Sakha,Rushikesh Kamalapurkar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LMI的切换观测器设计方法，用于具有两个输出通道但每次仅能获取一个输出的线性时不变系统，实现了估计误差收敛到零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统观测器要求每个输出通道都可检测的问题，在部分输出不可用的情况下仍实现状态估计。

Method: 通过线性矩阵不等式（LMI）离线设计与通道相关的Luenberger观测器增益，并利用切换机制构建观测器。

Result: 在输出切换时间间隔有界且整体系统可观测的条件下，所设计的切换观测器能使估计误差趋于零。

Conclusion: 该方法无需单个输出通道可检测，提供了无需实时计算的离线增益设计及误差收敛保证。

Abstract: This paper develops an LMI-based switching observer for linear time-invariant systems with two output channels, where only one output is available at any given time. We assume that the system is observable when both outputs are considered together, and that the time intervals between consecutive output switches are uniformly bounded. Under these assumptions, we design a set of channel-dependent Luenberger observer gains offline via linear matrix inequalities and show that the resulting switched observer drives the estimation error to zero. The main contribution is an offline gain design and convergence result for switched observers that does not require individual output channels to be detectable.

</details>


### [92] [Topological Feasibility Guarantees for Differentiable Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10332)
*Guangyu Wu,Ján Drgoňa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有确定性可行性保证的可微预测控制（DPC）方法，通过拓扑分析和基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的代理损失，在不依赖在线安全滤波器的情况下实现了离线策略学习的安全性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DPC方法缺乏严格的离线可行性保证，通常依赖概率性保障或在线安全滤波器，限制了其在安全关键系统中的应用。

Method: 利用DPC内置模型动态的特性，从拓扑和几何角度分析学习策略与状态空间的性质，提出一种结合控制屏障函数（CBF）的代理损失进行自监督离线训练的新策略。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法能从有限训练样本中推导出严格的确定性可行性保证，仿真结果显示约束违反程度随样本增加单调趋近于零。

Conclusion: DPC通过其基于模型的结构可提供传统黑箱方法（如强化学习）无法实现的形式化安全性证书，为基于学习的控制提供了新的可行性保证视角。

Abstract: Differentiable predictive control (DPC), a self-supervised learning approach for approximating explicit model predictive control (MPC) policies, offers significant computational advantages over online optimization-based MPC. However, feasibility guarantees, a core requirement for safe control, are currently provided either probabilistically or via online safety filters. The lack of rigorous feasibility guarantees for offline policy optimization remains an open problem. This paper establishes deterministic feasibility guarantees for DPC using a novel topological analysis of the induced reachable safe set, without requiring online safety filters. By exploiting the inherent model-based nature of DPC, in which differentiable system dynamics are embedded directly into the computational graph, we analyze the properties of the learned control policies and the corresponding system states from topological and geometric perspectives. Inspired by our theoretical analysis, we propose a novel self-supervised offline policy learning strategy that utilizes a proxy loss with Control Barrier Functions (CBFs). Crucially, these properties not only significantly improve policy training but also enable the derivation of strict, deterministic feasibility guarantees from a finite number of training samples. Extensive closed-loop simulations validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating that the empirical constraint violations monotonically decrease to zero as the training sample size increases. Ultimately, this work illustrates that DPC policy optimization yields formal safety certificates that are structurally unattainable with conventional black-box methods, e.g., reinforcement learning (RL) or supervised learning-based approximate MPC, thereby providing a new perspective on feasibility guarantees in learning-based control.

</details>


### [93] [From Privileged Control to Deployable Adaptation:Fusing Mechanism-Guided Task Reduction with Learned Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10453)
*Xitong Niu,Peifeng Hui,Zheyong Jiang,Yuan Gao,Chuanlin Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种机制引导的迁移方法，将特权多模式控制知识转化为可部署的自适应控制器，显著降低跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在训练-部署不对称环境下，传统模仿学习因无法区分不同特权模式下的专家行为而存在安全隐患，且固定观测器或名义控制器难以应对输入增益变化和大扰动并存的情况。

Method: 利用精确的采样数据恒等式消除专家控制律中的加性扰动，通过因果状态历史推断任务相关的逆输入增益；从专家动作和部署可见轨迹中重构潜在目标，实现无需真实系统参数标签的学习。

Result: 所提方法在未见的60秒测试中比调优观测器减少约69%的跟踪均方根误差，结构化学生控制器接近特权专家性能，而直接动作网络在闭环中失败。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可解释的设计视角，用于将特权多模式控制知识迁移到实际部署的自适应控制器，并给出了迁移有效的理论条件。

Abstract: Simultaneous input-gain variation and large additive disturbance create a control problem in which a fixed observer or nominal controller may be unable to reproduce the performance of a regime-aware design. We study a training--deployment asymmetry: during simulation or commissioning, an expert controller is allowed to use the known gain and disturbance, whereas the deployed controller can use only the reference and measured states. Directly imitating expert actions is generally unsafe because the same instantaneous student observation may correspond to different privileged regimes and hence different expert actions. We propose a mechanism-guided transfer route rather than a new neural architecture. An exact sampled-data identity removes the additive disturbance from the expert law and reduces learning to a task-relevant inverse input gain inferred from causal state history. The latent target is reconstructed from expert actions and deployment-visible trajectories, so the true plant parameter is not required as a student label. A common-quadratic certificate is derived for the actual augmented sampled recursion, followed by explicit residual, coverage, switching, noise, and saturation qualifications. A parameter-regime scan shows that the nominal observer's error grows sharply as $a$ decreases and that the next gain above the best non-failing tuning diverges for every tested $a<1$. Direct action networks also fail in closed loop despite moderate offline error, whereas the structured student remains close to the privileged expert and reduces tracking RMSE by about 69\% relative to the tuned observer in unseen 60-s trials. The contribution is an interpretable design perspective for turning privileged multi-regime control knowledge into a deployable adaptive controller, together with conditions under which the transfer is meaningful.

</details>


### [94] [Nuclear fusion for AI: A pathway to power data centers sustainably](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10454)
*Layla Araiinejad,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 核聚变有望为快速增长的AI驱动数据中心提供可扩展、低碳的电力来源，其高容量因子和可靠供电特性使其成为理想选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能、云计算和加密货币挖矿推动电力需求激增，数据中心用电占比将持续上升，给电网可靠性和脱碳目标带来压力，亟需寻找可持续的能源解决方案。

Method: 通过比较分析核电（尤其是核聚变）与数据中心负荷特征在容量因子、平准化度电成本、电网接入限制和部署路径等方面的技术经济匹配性，评估核聚变的适用性。

Result: 初步技术经济分析表明，某些先进核聚变概念（特别是磁约束系统）可能在超大规模数据中心应用中具备成本竞争力；与数据中心共址还可缓解输电瓶颈、提升韧性，并且核聚变更优的安全性和废物特性增强了其社会与政治可行性。

Conclusion: 核聚变是可持续支撑下一代计算基础设施的战略性路径，应在政策制定和产业部署规划中被优先考虑。

Abstract: This perspective examines whether nuclear fusion can provide a scalable, low-carbon power source for rapidly growing AI-driven data center demand. As large language models, cloud computing, and cryptocurrency mining accelerate electricity consumption growth, data centers are projected to account for a substantially larger share of U.S. and global electricity use in the coming decades, creating significant pressure on grid reliability and decarbonization goals. We evaluate the technical and economic alignment between data center load profiles and nuclear power, particularly fusion, through a comparative analysis of capacity factors, levelized cost of electricity, grid interconnection constraints, and deployment pathways. Unlike intermittent renewables, nuclear fission and fusion offer high-capacity-factor, firm baseload generation suited to AI training and inference workloads that require continuous, reliable power. Preliminary techno-economic analysis suggests that several Nth-of-a-kind fusion concepts, particularly magnetic confinement systems, may become cost-competitive with firmed renewable systems and advanced fission for hyperscale data center applications. Co-location of fusion plants with data centers further reduces transmission bottlenecks, improves resilience, and aligns with emerging hyperscaler procurement strategies. We also assess recent regulatory developments and argue that fusion's favorable safety profile and reduced waste burden improve its long-term social and political viability relative to fission. We conclude that fusion represents a strategically important pathway for sustainably powering next-generation computing infrastructure and should be prioritized in both policy and industrial deployment planning.

</details>


### [95] [Removal-Only Actuation in Age-Structured Branching Populations: Fundamental Limits of Equilibrium Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10641)
*Ouerdia Arezki,Ali Zemouche*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究了亚临界年龄结构分支种群在预防性移除控制下的衰减速率极限，发现其受繁殖特性决定的结构性限制，无法达到理论屏障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在准稳态下通过年龄依赖性移除控制种群衰减的潜力与局限，特别是在疾病暴发管理中的应用。

Method: 利用Yaglom极限描述存活条件下的平衡状态，建模预防性移除为增加死亡率但不改变生育律的控制器，分析其对Foster-Lyapunov漂移和衰减速率的影响。

Result: 证明了移除控制对衰减速率的提升受限于由繁殖决定的传输屏障Lbar，最大可达衰减速率为Lbar+ν*，其中ν*≤0为无子女死亡谱系的Malthusian参数，且该间隙有闭式解。

Conclusion: 此类移除策略无法突破结构性障碍，实际可实现的衰减速率由控制器形态而非强度决定，在无个体产生两个以上后代时间隙消失。

Abstract: A subcritical age-structured branching population dies out almost surely. Conditioned on survival, it converges to its Yaglom limit, a quasi-stationary equilibrium that we take as the operating point for control. We model preventive removal (culling) as an age-dependent actuator that raises the mortality rate and leaves the offspring law untouched, and we show that its authority over this equilibrium is bounded for structural reasons. Two facts drive the result. First, the input is matched to the killing rate but unmatched with respect to the Foster--Lyapunov drift, so the transmission barrier $\Lbar$ set by reproduction alone is invariant under such actuation. Second, and this does not follow from invariance alone,} the supremum of the reachable decay rates is $\Lbar+ν^\star$, where $ν^\star\le0$ is the Malthusian parameter of the lineage conditioned never to die childless; the gap $|ν^\star|$ is given in closed form and vanishes exactly when no individual has two or more offspring. Consequently no removal law of this class reaches the barrier, and along the admissibility boundary the achievable decay rate is governed by the shape of the actuator rather than by its size. We illustrate these results on a model calibrated to the 2001 Cumbrian foot-and-mouth outbreak.

</details>


### [96] [AIDC Microgrid Vulnerability Assessment Under Computing-Power Coordinated Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10645)
*Ze Yu,Hongwei Zhen,Chao Shen,Mingyang Sun*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文首次探讨了针对低碳AI数据中心的算力协同攻击，提出了一种考虑供需双侧不确定性的微电网脆弱性评估框架，并通过阻抗分析方法识别出易受攻击的时间窗口和攻击向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型服务的快速发展，AI数据中心规模不断扩大，带来电力资源紧张和碳排放增加的问题；可再生能源的引入虽有助于减碳，但也带来了供能侧与算力需求侧双重波动下的跨域稳定性挑战。

Method: 提出一种不确定性感知的AI数据中心微电网脆弱性评估框架，结合置信加权实现与长期攻击可达域分析，利用阻抗筛选方法将源-荷变化映射到稳定裕度退化，识别脆弱攻击时段与攻击向量。

Result: 案例研究表明，算力协同攻击可导致逆变器频率偏移超过额定值20%，引发单点攻击无法达到的失稳状态；所提框架能从长期运行轨迹中提取稀疏但高置信度的脆弱时段。

Conclusion: 算力与能源耦合系统面临新型协同攻击风险，本文提出的评估框架有效识别了低频、高危的攻击路径，为未来安全防护设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language model (LLM) services is accelerating the expansion of AI data centers (AIDCs), intensifying concerns over power system resource adequacy and rising carbon emissions. The integration of renewable energy provides a pathway toward addressing these pressures, but it also introduces new cross-domain stability challenges to low-carbon AIDCs. For example, variability in renewable generation affects reliability on the supply side, whereas fluctuations in AIDC workloads affect reliability on the demand side, jointly creating interconnected stability risks in AIDC microgrids. To address this problem, this paper is the first to explore computing-power coordinated attacks against low-carbon AIDCs. First, we propose an uncertainty-aware AIDC microgrid vulnerability assessment framework to capture two interacting attack surfaces: inverter control parameter tampering attacks, and AI-induced demand manipulation attacks. Then, accounting for renewable-side forecast uncertainty and AIDC-side demand response uncertainty, we introduce confidence-weighted realizations and construct a long-term attack reachable domain analysis. Furthermore, an impedance-based screening method is utilised to map generation and load variations to erosion of stability margin, thereby identifying vulnerable attack time windows and attack vectors. In addition, case studies show that computing-power coordinated attacks induce sustained inverter frequency excursions exceeding 20% of the nominal value and reach instability conditions unattainable by single attacks. The results also demonstrate that the proposed framework can extract sparse, high-confidence vulnerable periods from long-term operating trajectories.

</details>


### [97] [Optimal Stopping of Self-Refining Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10729)
*Kim Hammar,Tansu Alpcan,Emil C. Lupu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将基础模型自优化过程形式化为最优停止问题的新方法，通过实验验证了其在编码基准上比以往策略更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高基础模型输出的质量并控制成本，研究者希望找到最佳的迭代优化次数。

Method: 将自优化过程建模为最优停止问题，并利用随机近似方法计算最优停止策略。

Result: 在编码基准上的实验表明，所提出的停止策略相比之前的工作显著更具成本效益。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效平衡反馈带来的性能提升与计算成本，实现了更高效的模型自优化。

Abstract: Foundation models can improve their outputs through a self-refinement process driven by external feedback. In this process, the model is embedded in an iterative loop where it generates outputs, receives feedback from verifiers, and refines its responses through in-context learning. Following a novel approach, we formalize this process as an optimal stopping problem where the number of refinement iterations is decided based on expected improvement relative to cost. We derive optimal stopping policies and show that they can be efficiently computed through stochastic approximation. To evaluate our approach experimentally, we apply it to a coding benchmark for foundation models. The empirical results show that our stopping policies are significantly more cost-efficient than stopping policies proposed in prior work.

</details>


### [98] [KOOPMAN-Luenberger Observer Design for Nonlinear Systems with Application to the Monitoring of a Latent Thermal Energy Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10746)
*Mustapha Habib,Dario Aguiar,Esther Kieseritzky,Tilman Barz,Qian Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于KOOPMAN算子的线性状态观测器（KOOPMAN-LSO）框架，用于在测量稀疏且状态不可达的情况下实现非线性系统的高精度状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性动态系统在测量稀疏和内部状态无法直接获取时的状态估计难题，寻求一种兼具精度与计算效率的解决方案。

Method: 利用物理信息引导的基函数将非线性动力学提升至高维可观测空间，通过eDMDc方法识别带控制的线性预测模型，并在提升空间中构建基于LQR对偶法设计增益的离散时间Luenberger观测器。

Result: 在基于相变材料的潜热储能系统实验中，仅使用有限输出测量即可准确重构不可测状态，出口温度RMSE低至0.0819°C，内部PCM温度误差普遍低于1.0°C。

Conclusion: KOOPMAN-LSO框架成功结合了KOOPMAN提升的表达能力与线性观测器设计的简洁高效，为实际非线性系统提供了一种系统化的状态估计方法。

Abstract: State estimation for nonlinear dynamical systems remains a fundamental challenge, particularly when measurements are sparse and internal states are inaccessible. This work presents a KOOPMAN-based Linear State Observer (KOOPMAN-LSO) design framework that enables linear observer synthesis for nonlinear systems through KOOPMAN operator theory. The nonlinear dynamics are lifted into a higher-dimensional observable space using physics-informed basis functions, where a linear predictor with control is identified via extended dynamic mode decomposition with control (eDMDc). A discrete-time Luenberger observer is then constructed in the lifted space, and the observer gain is obtained through a dual linear - quadratic regulator (LQR) formulation to ensure stable and tunable estimation error dynamics. The proposed framework combines the representational capability of KOOPMAN lifting with the simplicity and computational efficiency of linear observer design, providing a systematic approach for nonlinear state estimation under limited sensing. Its effectiveness is demonstrated on a latent thermal energy storage (LTES) system based on phase-change materials (PCM), where internal temperature states are not directly measurable. Experimental results under varying operating conditions show accurate reconstruction of unmeasured states from limited output measurements, illustrating the potential of KOOPMAN-LSO design for practical nonlinear systems. The proposed approach achieves high-fidelity reconstruction with an RMSE as low as 0.0819 °C for the LTES outlet temperature and generally below 1.0 °C for observable internal PCM temperatures.

</details>


### [99] [Control of hybrid wind-wave energy systems using reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10754)
*Zechuan Lin,Kemeng Chen,Maosen Fan,Xiaofan Li,Xi Xiao,John V. Ringwood*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习（RL）的混合风浪能（HWWE）系统控制框架，通过高保真仿真环境直接学习实时控制策略，在波浪能捕获和浮式平台运动抑制之间实现了显著的帕累托性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低海上可再生能源成本，将波浪能转换器（WECs）与浮式海上风力机（FOWTs）集成形成HWWE系统具有潜力，但其复杂动力学特性给控制设计带来挑战，需探索高效控制方法。

Method: 建立包含15 MW风力机、VolturnUS半潜式平台和三个环形WEC的HWWE数值模型，并将其作为强化学习训练环境，采用多目标优化方法从帕累托前沿角度评估控制性能。

Result: 所提出的RL控制器相比传统控制策略，在相同平台运动水平下波浪能捕获提高75%以上，或在相同能量捕获下平台运动减少近50%。

Conclusion: 强化学习能够有效提升HWWE系统的综合性能，显著扩展其可实现的性能边界，为复杂海洋能源系统的智能控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Integrating wave energy converters (WECs) with floating offshore wind turbines (FOWTs), to form hybrid wind-wave energy (HWWE) systems, is a promising approach to achieve further cost reduction for offshore renewable energy. In such systems, the control of the integrated WECs plays an important role, with the potential to generate additional wave energy while simultaneously suppressing floating platform motion. However, HWWE systems are characterized by complex dynamics, making accurate modelling only viable through numerical simulation, and posing significant challenges for control design. This paper proposes a reinforcement learning (RL) control framework for HWWE systems, in which the real-time control policy is learned directly through interactions with high-fidelity simulation. A numerical model is established for a HWWE system consisting of an IEA 15 MW wind turbine, a VolturnUS semi-submersible platform, and three torus-type WECs, which is then employed as the RL training environment. Control performance is evaluated in terms of both wave energy generation and platform motion reduction, two competing objectives, from a Pareto perspective. It is shown that the proposed RL controller achieves substantial Pareto improvements over conventional control strategies, e.g., over 75\% higher wave energy capture at the same platform motion level, or nearly 50\% lower motion at the same energy capture level, thereby significantly extending the attainable performance boundary of HWWE systems.

</details>


### [100] [Variable Step Time Delay Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10770)
*Yonsoo Kim,Hancheol Cho*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为变步长时间延迟控制（VTDC）的主动非周期鲁棒控制框架，通过将反馈控制与控制更新调度联合设计，实现了无需持续监控触发条件的自适应控制更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常将控制器与触发机制分离设计，导致资源利用不高效或性能受限。本文旨在通过联合优化控制与调度，提升系统在不确定环境下的鲁棒性与实时性。

Method: 受Runge-Kutta积分中自适应步长调节启发，VTDC利用局部控制误差与实际时间步长之间的可建模关系，基于滑模流形的时间延迟控制构建系统时间延迟误差（TDE），并推导出TDE随控制区间二次增长的边界特性，从而得到一个闭式反馈调度律，直接代数确定下一次控制更新时刻。

Result: VTDC能够自动调节控制更新间隔以维持TDE在期望范围内；所提方法避免了Zeno行为，保证了时间步长与步长比的有界性，并使滑动变量一致最终有界；仿真验证了其在航天器姿态控制中的可行性与低计算开销。

Conclusion: VTDC为非周期控制提供了一种结构驱动的新型设计范式，通过将调度律嵌入控制器结构本身，实现了高性能、低资源消耗的闭环控制，具有良好的工程应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents Variable-step Time Delay Control (VTDC), a proactive aperiodic robust control framework that jointly addresses feedback control and control-update scheduling, rather than treating the controller and triggering mechanism as separate components. Inspired by adaptive stepsize regulation in Runge-Kutta integration, VTDC exploits the fact that the local control error can be structured to scale with the realized timestep. Using sliding-manifold-based Time Delay Control, the resulting System Time Delay Error (TDE), which reflects local model and uncertainty variations, is shown to be quadratically bounded by the control interval. This relation enables a closed-form feedback law that automatically enlarges or reduces the subsequent interval to regulate the TDE magnitude. Because the scheduling law is constructed directly from the controller structure, the next update time is determined algebraically without continuous trigger monitoring, future-state prediction, or iterative search. The resulting variable-step closed loop is shown to admit bounded timesteps and step ratios, exclude Zeno behavior, and render the sliding variable uniformly ultimately bounded. Spacecraft attitude-control simulations further demonstrate the practical feasibility and low computational burden of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [101] [Cross-modal topology decodes battery faults from sparse voltage snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10825)
*Jinwen Li,Yunhong Che,Simona Onori,Weihan Li,Xiaosong Hu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DeFault的跨模态诊断框架，通过将一维电压序列映射到多维相空间拓扑结构中，实现了对电动汽车电池故障的高精度识别，无需新增传感器即可在现有车队中实现可扩展的电池安全监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池安全性是制约电动汽车大规模普及的主要瓶颈，而现有的诊断方法受限于稀疏、低频的电压测量数据，难以区分重叠的故障模式。

Method: 引入DeFault框架，利用双向交叉注意力机制，将一维电压快照展开为多维相空间拓扑结构，从而解耦复合故障模式，实现物理一致性过滤。

Result: 在来自99辆运行中电动车的1640万条数据上验证，仅使用500秒（<100mV）电压快照，对四种故障类型的平均准确率达0.96，F1分数达0.84。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，无需新增传感器，即可通过拓扑展开方法实现对老旧车队电池的高保真、可解释电化学诊断，为电池安全问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Battery safety remains the primary bottleneck for mass electric vehicle (EV) adoption, yet field monitoring is hamstrung by a fundamental asymmetry: complex electrochemical faults must be diagnosed via sparse, low-frequency voltage measurements. Existing methods struggle to resolve the signal ambiguity between overlapping fault modes without hardware upgrades. Here, we demonstrate that these distinct fault fingerprints are not lost, but topologically folded within voltage snapshots. We introduce DeFault, a cross-modal diagnostic framework that mathematically unfolds one-dimensional voltage sequences into multi-dimensional phase-space topologies. DeFault employs a bidirectional cross-attention mechanism that acts as an autonomous, physics-aligned filter, explicitly decoding compounded fault modes that remain fundamentally invisible to sequence-based methods. Validated on a field dataset of 16.4 million data records from 99 in-service EVs, our method achieves an average accuracy of 0.96 and an F1 score of 0.84 for four fault types using only 500-second snapshots (spanning <100 mV). This work proves that high-fidelity, interpretable electrochemical diagnosis is achievable on legacy fleets without new sensors, providing a scalable solution for the battery safety crisis.

</details>


### [102] [Robust Safety Filtering for Input-Constrained Underactuated Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10872)
*Muhamad Rausyan Fikri*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对输入受限、欠驱动线性系统在未知干扰下的鲁棒安全过滤框架，结合H-∞控制与高阶控制屏障函数，确保系统安全性并保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了应对输入受限和未知干扰下欠驱动系统的安全控制挑战，提升系统在复杂约束下的鲁棒性与可行性。

Method: 基于零和微分博弈设计H-∞基线输入，结合干扰观测器提供估计及其误差界，利用该信息构建鲁棒高阶控制屏障函数，并通过有限时域H-∞性能平衡调整输入偏差。

Result: 在受扰动的线性化两轮平衡机器人上验证了方法有效性，展示了位置与姿态约束如何竞争有限的轮矩输入，实现了安全控制与性能的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效保证输入约束下系统的前向不变性和安全性，适用于标量输入系统的点对点可行控制，并具有明确的可行性裕度评估。

Abstract: We present a robust safety-filtering framework for input-constrained underactuated linear systems subject to unknown disturbances. A baseline H-$\infty$ input is derived from a zero-sum differential game, while a disturbance observer supplies an estimate and a transient error bound. The baseline input is adjusted using the disturbance estimate, while the estimate and its error bound are used to define robust high-order control barrier function constraints; forward invariance holds as long as the admissible-input set remains nonempty. For scalar-input systems, pointwise feasibility is determined from an exact input interval, and the interval width defines the feasibility margin. A finite-horizon H-$\infty$ performance balance accounts for the accumulated deviation of the applied input from the baseline H-$\infty$ policy. Simulations on a linearized two-wheeled balancing robot show how position and body-pitch constraints compete for the same bounded wheel-torque input.

</details>


### [103] [Multi-Rate Distributed Unscented Kalman Filtering Under Collective Detectability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10921)
*Mohammad Ali Abooshahab,Morten Hovd*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需中心融合的多速率分布式无迹卡尔曼滤波器，通过信息扩散实现稳定且高效的非线性网络状态估计，优于传统协方差平均一致性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性网络中无中心融合、局部不可观测以及多速率采样下的滤波稳定性问题，设计一种无需全局协调的分布式滤波算法。

Method: 采用基于窗口化集体可检测性条件的多速率分布式无迹卡尔曼滤波，各节点在本地运行连续-离散UKF，并在一跳邻域内交换估计信息；使用信息矩阵扩散与加权融合，并引入H∞变体和正则化信息边界保证稳定性。

Result: 在集体不可观子空间平凡且线性化误差满足一致性约束时，信息加权融合均值具有指数有界均方误差；算法在多速率（3:2）下仍保持稳定，而协方差平均一致性和扩散均值发散；信息扩散通信量仅为五轮协方差平均的五分之一且精度更高。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需收缩-混合条件即可实现有界误差，通过局部信息扩散和正则化设计，有效解决了分布式非线性滤波中的可观测性不足与多速率挑战。

Abstract: We develop a multi-rate distributed unscented Kalman filter for nonlinear networks without a fusion center. Local continuous-discrete UKFs run on a common base grid, update at their own sampling instants, and exchange estimates within one-hop neighborhoods. Under a windowed collective-detectability condition, diffusion of information matrices gives uniformly bounded quotient covariances although no individual node need be observable. When the collectively invisible subspace is trivial and the statistical-linearization discrepancies satisfy an explicit coherence bound, the information weighted fused mean yields exponentially bounded mean-square errors without a contraction-mixing condition. This contrasts with the arithmetic diffusiojn mean, which requires one. A distributed $H_{\infty}$ variant uses a fixed attenuation penalty. A finite-prefix feasibility check and the one-hop detectability Gramian yield a uniform regularized-information margin - an explicit local-contraction-mixing condition then yields mean-square error boundedness. The developed methods are tested on a stochastic nonlinear benchmark and a three-inertia network in which every node misses at least one mode. Information diffusion is more accurate than five-round covariance-averaging consensus at one fifth of its communication. It remains stable under 3:2 multi-rate sampling, where the covariance-averaging consensus and the diffusion mean both diverge, and it keeps the quotient covariances bounded.

</details>


### [104] [HPC-Enabled Generator Importance Assessment for RTO-Scale Resource Adequacy Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14064)
*Eve Tsybina,Nicholson Koukpaizan,Joshua Hambrick,Samim Konjicija,Slaven Peles*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高性能计算框架，用于快速评估发电机组对电网的重要性，并按燃料类型、运行成本或电网影响进行排序，显著提高了电力系统规划的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着老化设备退役和负荷增长超过新发电设施建设，现代电力系统面临压力，亟需高效方法评估发电机组退役对系统储备、燃料供应链和公共健康的影响。

Method: 开发了一种基于高性能计算（HPC）的框架，能够在几分钟内完成对单个发电机组重要性的评估与排序。

Result: 该框架大幅缩短了传统上耗时的计算过程，使规划者能够快速评估更广泛的发电组合情景。

Conclusion: 所提出的HPC框架实现了发电机组影响的快速评估，提升了电力系统规划的灵活性和响应能力。

Abstract: Modern power systems are increasingly under stress as aging assets approach retirement and load growth outpaces new generation construction. The severity of this challenge varies by region: in the EU, the transmission grid can partially compensate for local generation shortfalls, while in the US, generation tends to be more localized, making retirements harder to offset. Retirement of generation has consequences for system reserves, fuel supply chain, and public health. We present an high-performance computing (HPC) framework for rapidly assessing the grid importance of individual generating units and ranking them by primary fuel type, operating cost, or grid impact. Historically, such studies were computationally intensive and therefore conducted infrequently. This work demonstrates that such assessments can be completed in minutes, enabling planners to evaluate a much broader range of generation portfolio scenarios than was previously possible.

</details>


### [105] [Techno-Economic Boundary Analysis of Small Modular Reactor Cogeneration for Hyperscale Data Center IT and Cooling Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10999)
*Honglin Li,Buxin She,Jie Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在德克萨斯州ERCOT地区将小型模块化核反应堆与200兆瓦数据中心共址的经济可行性，通过联合优化反应堆调度、蒸汽提取、吸收式制冷和电网交换，分析了资本成本、市场条件、政策激励和冷却效率对系统竞争力的影响。结果显示，在2023年中等反应堆资本成本下，核电配置成本比电网供电高出49-62%，但nth-of-a-kind反应堆可降低成本77-89%；碳价为53-64美元/吨CO₂时可在小时级出口补贴下弥补差距。吸收式制冷年供冷占比38%，年增成本920万美元，其经济性取决于设备安装成本与效率。研究划定了共址核能热电联产在何种条件下可与电网购电竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着超大规模数据中心对电力需求快速增长，传统电网难以满足其高利用率需求，促使人们重新关注将其与小型模块化反应堆（SMR）共址的可能性。同时，利用核反应堆余热驱动吸收式制冷以满足数据中心大量冷却需求，可提升能源利用效率并减少碳排放。然而，电力销售与热能利用两种收入流尚未在多种现实条件耦合下进行综合定价分析，亟需系统评估其经济可行性及关键影响因素。

Method: 研究采用小时级联合优化模型，对一个200 MWe的数据中心在ERCOT区域的核反应堆调度、蒸汽提取、吸收式制冷运行及电网电力交换进行建模分析。模型覆盖109种情景，涵盖资本成本、市场价格、政策激励（如45Y生产税收抵免）、融资条件和冷却效率等多种变量，评估不同配置下的经济表现。

Result: 在2023年中等反应堆资本成本下，核能配置的成本比仅依赖电网供电高出49-62%，即使有45Y税收抵免也是如此；当资本成本降至约5000美元/kWe时，系统具备经济可行性；nth-of-a-kind反应堆使成本降低77-89%（2023年），但在2024年低价市场中仍介于平价至高出34%；碳价达53-64美元/吨CO₂时可在小时级出口补贴下弥补成本差距；吸收式制冷响应电价变化，满足全年38%的冷却负荷，相比仅发电配置每年增加920万美元成本；该成本差额在吸收式制冷安装成本为60美元/kWc（基准效率）或570美元/kWc（老旧效率）时可被消除，低于当前商用价格（450–1200美元/kWc）。

Conclusion: 共址的小型模块化反应堆与吸收式制冷耦合系统在特定资本成本、碳价政策和设备成本条件下具备与电网购电竞争的潜力。nth-of-a-kind反应堆和合理的碳定价是缩小成本差距的关键；吸收式制冷的经济性高度依赖于其安装成本和运行效率。研究明确了推动此类低碳集成能源系统商业可行所需的技术进步、政策支持和市场机制。

Abstract: Hyperscale data centers are adding firm, high-utilization demand faster than grids can serve it, renewing interest in colocating them with small modular reactors. Such a plant could earn revenue in two ways, selling low-carbon power and diverting steam to absorption chillers that serve a cooling load accounting for 20-40% of facility electricity use, but neither revenue stream has been priced across the conditions that must coincide. Here we co-optimize reactor dispatch, steam extraction, absorption cooling and grid exchange hourly for a 200 MW$_\mathrm{e}$ data center in the Electric Reliability Council of Texas (ERCOT) region, across 109 runs spanning capital, market, policy, financing and cooling efficiency. At 2023 mid-range reactor capital, the nuclear configurations cost 49-62% more than grid supply even with the Section 45Y production tax credit. The viable region opens near \$5,000 kW$_\mathrm{e}^{-1}$, and nth-of-a-kind capital makes them 77-89% cheaper in 2023, though between parity and 34% more expensive in the low-price 2024 market. A carbon price of \$53-64 tCO$_2^{-1}$ closes the mid-range gap under hourly export crediting. Absorption cooling is dispatched in response to hourly electricity prices and supplies 38% of annual cooling, at an added cost of \$9.2 million yr$^{-1}$ relative to the reactor-only plant; that gap closes at an installed absorption cost of \$60 kW$_\mathrm{c}^{-1}$ at baseline efficiency and \$570 kW$_\mathrm{c}^{-1}$ on a legacy-efficiency campus, against surveyed commercial prices of \$450-1,200 kW$_\mathrm{c}^{-1}$. Together these results delineate the capital, market and policy conditions under which colocated reactor cogeneration is competitive with grid procurement, and the range over which each condition moves the outcome.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [A full software stack for epidemic disease management: Unlocking the joint potential of software technology and supercomputing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09933)
*Jonas Gilg,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Mariama Jaiteh,David Kerkmann,Niklas Medinger,Shahbaz Memon,Anna Wendler,Moritz Zeumer,Henrik Zunker,Maximilian Betz,Ralf Hannemann-Tamas,Jonas Immanuel Heinicke,Julian Litz,Achim Basermann,Cas Cremers,Manuel Dahmen,Andreas Gerndt,Jens Henrik Göbbert,Björn Hagemeier,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Berit Lange,Katharina Nöh,Sarah Straßburger,Michael Meyer-Hermann,Martin J. Kühn*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在应对传染病大流行时，如何通过自动化工作流和集成的软件模块提升响应速度，并强调需要符合FAIR原则的可扩展计算基础设施以实现高效、安全的建模与数据管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的传染病建模和响应流程仍依赖大量人工操作，导致响应延迟，难以应对快速演变的疫情，亟需更高效的自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出构建一个支持异构硬件、动态数据集成和高绩效计算（HPC）的现代化软件基础设施，并遵循FAIR原则以确保可重用性和可持续性。

Result: 该基础设施能显著缩短响应时间，提升建模效率，支持安全的数据管理和透明的结果展示，适用于未来疫情的快速部署。

Conclusion: 为提高大流行病准备能力，必须整合先进的科学软件与可扩展的计算基础设施，推动自动化、合规且透明的数字防疫体系发展。

Abstract: Infectious diseases remain a major threat to human societies. During the recent COVID-19 pandemic, mathematical modeling and extensive computer simulations proved highly effective in supporting public health experts and decision makers.
  Despite these advances, the full potential of modern modeling approaches and digital technologies has not yet been realized. Many critical tasks -- including expert consultations, model execution, scenario analyses, report preparation, and result communication -- still relied heavily on manual, human-driven processes with each manual interaction introducing avoidable delays and limiting responsiveness during rapidly evolving outbreaks.
  Pandemic preparedness should opt for automated workflows and seamlessly integrated software modules that can improve pandemic mitigation capabilities by substantially reducing response times. For this step, we require robust and flexible computational infrastructure capable of supporting heterogeneous hardware and continuously evolving infectious-disease models. In addition, data sources need to be dynamically integrated. Managing such demands needs infrastructure that supports automated high-performance computing (HPC) workflows. Beyond computational performance, software infrastructure must ensure secure user and data management to comply with data-protection regulations and provide clear, transparent presentation of results to both decision makers and the public.
  Meeting the aforementioned challenges requires tight integration of state-of-the-art scientific software with modern, scalable infrastructure that can leverage supercomputing resources when necessary. For rapid deployment in future epidemic or pandemic scenarios, adherence to the FAIR principles for research software is critical to ensure reusability and sustainability.

</details>


### [107] [HyperShape: Hyperelasticity Across Diverse Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09938)
*Leo Widmer,Sidaty El Hadramy,Stéphane Cotin,Philippe Claude Cattin*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: HyperShape是一个可扩展的框架，用于生成合成形状及其对应的超弹性模拟数据，旨在系统评估神经算子在不同分布和复杂度下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超弹性问题的神经算子基准测试依赖于简单或少量几何形状，难以严格评估模型在不同几何和边界条件下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出HyperShape框架，生成具有可控形状变化的2D和3D超弹性数据集，支持在分布内、分布外和从合成到真实的迁移场景中进行系统性评估，并测试多种先进神经算子的表现。

Result: 实验发现神经算子在简单形状上表现良好，但随着形状复杂性、几何多样性和边界条件变化增加，性能显著下降，且需要大量训练数据；性能退化与几何复杂度呈一致且可预测的关系。

Conclusion: HyperShape为超弹性代理模型提供了一个开放、可扩展的基准测试平台，能够随领域发展不断集成新的几何、材料模型和加载条件，推动神经算子在复杂变形建模中的进一步研究。

Abstract: Hyperelastic deformations are highly sensitive to domain geometry and boundary conditions, making generalization across both a critical capability for neural operators applied to these problems. However, existing benchmarks for neural operators on hyperelasticity rely on simple or few geometries, which makes it difficult to assess this capability rigorously. To address this gap, we introduce HyperShape, an extensible framework designed to generate synthetic shapes and their corresponding hyperelastic simulation data, producing a suite of 2D and 3D datasets with adjustable complexity and controllable shape variations. This design enables systematic assessment of generalization across in-distribution, out-of-distribution, and synthetic-to-real transfer settings. Using this framework, we evaluated the performance of several state-of-the-art neural operators over diverse shape distributions. Our findings reveal that neural operators perform well on simple shapes but struggle as shape complexity, geometric diversity, and boundary condition variability increase, requiring large amounts of training data in such regimes. Performance degrades consistently and predictably with geometric complexity highlighting the need for further model development. As an open and extensible benchmark, HyperShape is designed to grow alongside the field: new geometries, material models, loading conditions, and evaluation settings can be easily incorporated to validate hyperelastic surrogate models.

</details>


### [108] [The Researcher's Guide to HPC Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09953)
*C. Nicole Avans,Evan Drake Suggs,Riley Shipley,Dennis Jump,Anthony Skjellum*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文旨在降低理解高性能计算（HPC）系统中网络技术的门槛，提供学习资源和参考文献，涵盖通信各层级、网络编程API、控制平面技术、物理互连和链路层设备，并以El Capitan超级计算机架构为例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于超级计算机和数据中心网络的硬件与软件技术在课程和教材中较少涉及，本文希望填补这一教育空白，帮助读者更好地理解和研究这些关键技术。

Method: 通过系统性地介绍网络技术的各个层面，并结合El Capitan超级计算机的实际架构案例，辅以大量参考资料，帮助读者深入学习。

Result: 提供了一个全面的学习起点，涵盖了HPC网络中的关键技术和组件，并为进一步研究提供了丰富的参考文献。

Conclusion: 本文有效降低了学习HPC网络技术的门槛，适合作为研究人员和学生进入该领域的入门指南。

Abstract: Networks are critical infrastructure in nearly every computing system, but they are particularly important in High Performance Computing (HPC) systems, where users tackle problems at massive scale. Unfortunately, many of the hardware and software technologies that comprise supercomputer and datacenter networks are not widely taught in classes, nor are they extensively covered by textbooks or other educational documents outside of their primary sources. The purpose of this document is to provide a starting point to learn about these technologies by lowering the barrier to understanding and providing an abundance of references for further research. Covered topics include all levels of communication and network programming APIs, network control plane technologies, physical interconnects, and link-layer devices which are then brought to life in discussion of the architecture of El Capitan, a leadership class supercomputer created by Lawrence Livermore National Laboratory.

</details>


### [109] [pyRMV: Reusable, Cross-model Validation for Computational Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09956)
*Hugo Dictus,Eduard Subert,Armando Romani,Henry Markram*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的计算模型验证方法，并开发了针对小鼠初级视觉皮层模型的Python验证工具库，推动计算科学中模型验证的标准化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决计算科学中模型验证缺乏标准化的问题，通过将模型视为系统属性预测生成器而非目标系统替代品，提高验证方法的通用性和可扩展性。

Method: 设计一种模型无关的验证框架，开发包含多种验证工具的Python库，适用于不同类型的模型，并讨论该方法的局限性及改进途径。

Result: 实现了对小鼠初级视觉皮层模型的通用验证方案，验证工具具有良好的可移植性和易用性，为跨模型比较和标准化验证提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 该方法是推动计算模型验证标准化的重要一步，有助于提升计算神经科学领域模型的可信度和可重复性。

Abstract: We present a model-agnostic approach to the validation of computational models, and a python library containing a suite of validations for mouse primary visual cortex models. By viewing the model not as a stand-in for the target system, but instead as a generator of predicted system properties, this approach allows validations to easily generalize to many different models, while also making them comparatively simple to write. Several limitations of this approach are outlined alongside possible means of addressing them. This presents an important step in addressing what we consider the most important problem in computational science: the standardization of model validation.

</details>


### [110] [Optimizing Parameterized Physics-Informed Neural Networks to Solve Multilayered Static Linear Elastic PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09981)
*Joseph Lim,Hanbo Song,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络（P2INN）的框架，用于多层材料变形控制设计，显著降低传统有限元方法的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元方法在设计多层材料时计算成本过高，限制了设计空间探索和优化。

Method: 构建包含FEM基准、P2INN位移场模型和FEM参考评估的框架，通过Navier-Cauchy残差施加物理约束，并采用分层PDE分解、界面连续性惩罚和合规性感知缩放等训练策略。

Result: 单层纯物理驱动配置下平均体积MAE为1.56%，最差情况为2.87%；三层配置下平均体积MAE为2.53%，最差情况为4.66%，达到初步设计探索的目标精度。

Conclusion: P2INN模型轻量且前向推理快速，可作为传统FEM的替代方案，大幅减少计算开销，加速防护结构等领域的逆向设计与优化。

Abstract: Designing multilayered materials for controlled deformation is heavily bottlenecked by the immense computational costs and time of traditional, industrial-grade finite element methods (FEM). This excessive expense severely limits design space exploration and gradient-based optimization. To streamline the workflow, we propose a framework for parameterized physics-informed neural networks (P2INNs). The framework encompasses three core components: I. a FEM baseline with a Hex8 element and trilinear basis functions, II. a P2INN displacement field model across variable material stiffness and layer thicknesses, and III. FEM-referenced evaluation. The PINN enforces static linear elasticity via Navier-Cauchy residuals with training strategies that prioritize physics, including layerwise PDE decomposition, interface continuity penalty, and compliance aware scaling. For one-layer, pure physics-driven configurations, the PINN achieves a mean volume MAE of 1.56% and worst-case volume MAE of 2.87% against the FEM benchmark. For three-layer configurations with controlled supervised training, the PINN achieves a mean volume MAE of 2.53% and worst-case volume MAE of 4.66%, meeting the near-5% worst-case volume-MAE target for preliminary design-space exploration. The trained P2INN model remains a lightweight model with a simple forward pass for future calculations, creating an alternative to traditional FEM. By drastically reducing the need for expensive FEM evaluations, this approach could accelerate the inverse design and optimization of advanced layered architectures for protective structures in various load-heavy or potentially collision-heavy fields.

</details>


### [111] [OpenPM: Auditable Point-in-Time Evaluation for LLM Portfolio-Management Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09988)
*Xinying Cai,Minghao Guo,Jiahe Liu,Jiaojiao Han,Bangwei Guo,Yitao Long,Yuxuan Chen,Bohan Wu,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Raymond Li*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenPM，一个可审计的、基于时间点的评估框架，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在投资组合管理中的表现，防止回望偏差和风险失控，并通过参考代理“分层分配器”展示了构造器、分析员质量与交易成本对收益的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM交易代理研究结果可能因前瞻泄漏、过于乐观的执行假设以及未严格执行的风险指令而被高估，因此需要一个更严谨、可审计的评估框架来真实反映其性能。

Method: 提出OpenPM框架，要求所有输入数据必须在决策时点可用，将自然语言风险指令转化为类型化约束并强制执行；构建名为“分层分配器”的参考代理，包含打分的分析员、生成权重的构造器和确保可行性的确定性批评者，并通过固定分析员证据回放不同构造器以隔离其效果。

Result: 在短期窗口案例中，更强的构造器仅在相同池中相对于等权策略表现出微弱且依赖模型的增益；分析员质量比构造器选择更重要，换手率是主要成本来源；所有回报均为无市场冲击的静态窗口上的上界，并非经验证的超额收益。

Conclusion: 当前LLM交易代理的实际价值有限，性能受数据质量和交易成本主导，严格的时序验证和风险约束执行对公正评估至关重要，未来研究应关注分析质量提升与成本控制。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to read markets, assess risk, and allocate capital. However, reported results for LLM trading agents can be inflated by look-ahead leakage, optimistic execution, and risk mandates that are described but not enforced. We present OpenPM, an auditable point-in-time evaluation framework for LLM portfolio-management agents. In OpenPM, an agent manages a \$1M long-only book over the S\&P 500 universe using market data at five-minute intervals. Every record visible to the agent must be available at the decision time. Natural-language risk mandates are converted into typed constraints and enforced on the executed portfolio. Each run produces audit artifacts, including a contamination certificate, a cost-sensitivity curve, and a constraint-adherence report. We also build a reference agent named the tiered allocator, where typed analysts score candidates, a constructor LLM proposes weights, and a deterministic critic guarantees feasibility. We isolate constructor behavior by capturing analyst evidence once and replaying it across constructor models. In our short-window case study, stronger constructors show modest and model-dependent gains over equal weighting on the same pool, but analyst quality matters more than constructor choice, and turnover is the main cost driver. All returns are upper bounds on a single frozen window without market impact, not validated alpha.

</details>


### [112] [VeriFin: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Verifying LLM-Generated Financial Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10213)
*Bethel Hall,Sachi Shome,William Eiers*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: VeriFin是一个用于金融领域数值问答的神经符号验证框架，通过将操作数与XBRL事实数据对齐、从问题或文件中推导计算逻辑，并利用Z3求解器进行验证，有效识别并修复大语言模型生成的错误财务声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成财务数据时常引用错误的报告期、单位、项目或公式，仅靠算术检查无法发现此类错误，因此需要一种能结合真实数据源和逻辑推理的验证方法。

Method: 提出VeriFin框架：1）将数值运算的操作数锚定到XBRL披露事实；2）从问题、文件链接库或指标定义中推导正确计算方式；3）使用Z3求解器验证声明正确性；4）利用不可满足核心定位矛盾点以支持修正。

Result: 在包含600个问题的XBRLFiling基准和FinanceBench上测试显示，VeriFin在所有验证方法共享的候选池中未接受任何错误声明，而基线方法接受了6至92个（XBRLFiling）和4至21个（FinanceBench）错误声明；且该结果在多个生成模型下均保持稳定；求解器反馈使修复成功率最高达69.9%。

Conclusion: 基于源头事实的符号化验证可为大语言模型生成的财务声明提供可靠、可审计的接受控制机制，显著提升金融问答系统的准确性与可信度。

Abstract: Large language models often produce plausible numerical claims from financial filings while using the wrong reporting period, unit, line item, or formula. Verifying such claims requires more than rechecking arithmetic: a verifier must ground the relevant facts, establish the authorized calculation, and determine whether the candidate value follows from both. We propose VeriFin, a neurosymbolic verification framework for numerical financial question answering that grounds operands in filed XBRL facts, derives calculations from the question, filing linkbases, or documented metric definitions, and checks claims using Z3. When a claim is inconsistent, solver-derived unsatisfiable cores identify the conflicting facts, formula, and candidate value, enabling targeted repair. We evaluate VeriFin on XBRLFiling, a new 600-question benchmark constructed from 10-K filings of 28 U.S. companies, and on FinanceBench. On fixed candidate pools shared by all verification methods, VeriFin accepts none of the incorrect claims, whereas the baselines accept 6 to 92 incorrect claims on XBRLFiling's 600 claims and 4 to 21 incorrect claims on FinanceBench's 67 claims. The zero false-accept result persists across multiple answer-generator models. Solver-derived feedback further improves repair, achieving up to 69.9% recovery among true catches. These results show that source-grounded symbolic verification can provide a reliable and auditable acceptance control for LLM-generated financial claims.

</details>


### [113] [The Kuramoto Neural Operator: Learning to Solve PDEs via Coupled Oscillator Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10234)
*Petr Badolia,Leonid Obukhov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于耦合振子系统连续极限思想的新型神经算子——Kuramoto Neural Operator (KNO)，用于求解在不同物理配置下重复出现的偏微分方程（PDE）。与传统方法固定基函数表示不同，KNO通过隐空间中相互作用的振子场演化来建模解算子，在多种PDE基准任务上表现优异，并通过消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。此外，研究发现预测误差与振子系统的同步程度密切相关，揭示了模型内在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子多采用固定基函数表示解算子，虽适合全局结构但难以捕捉物理空间中的局部相互作用。为此，作者从耦合振子系统的连续极限可描述广泛PDE的现象出发，探索一种更具物理启发性的替代建模路径。

Method: 提出Kuramoto Neural Operator (KNO)，将PDE解算子建模为隐空间中一组相互作用振子的动态演化过程，借鉴Kuramoto模型的思想，通过振子相位和耦合关系捕捉非线性动力学特征，并利用神经网络参数化其演化规则。

Result: 在多个多样化的PDE基准测试中，KNO实现了优于现有方法的预测性能；消融研究表明各架构组件均对性能有显著贡献；同时发现模型预测误差与隐振子系统的同步程度呈系统性变化关系。

Conclusion: KNO提供了一种新颖且物理意义明确的神经算子设计范式，通过振子动力学建模有效捕捉PDE的局部交互特性，在性能和可解释性方面均表现出优势，为算子学习开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Operator learning is a rapidly advancing area of computational science. It is particularly well suited to problems where a partial differential equation (PDE) must be solved repeatedly under varying physical configurations. Most existing architectures represent the solution operator in a fixed basis. While this assumption is well aligned with global structures, it is less suitable for phenomena governed by local interactions in physical space. We explore an alternative perspective motivated by the observation that the continuum limit of coupled oscillator systems can describe a broad class of PDEs. Building on this idea, we introduce the Kuramoto Neural Operator (KNO), which represents the solution through the evolution of a latent field of interacting oscillators. Across a diverse collection of PDE benchmarks, KNO achieves strong predictive performance, with improvements over competing approaches. Our experimental evaluation also includes an extensive ablation study that quantifies the contribution of each architectural component incorporated into KNO. Furthermore, we show that the model's prediction error is closely linked to the collective dynamics of the latent oscillators. It varies systematically with their degree of synchronization, providing insights into the underlying mechanisms.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Forecasting: Recasting Volatility Control as a Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10375)
*Hongji Pu,Leyang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: VolRouter是一种模块化框架，通过市场状态条件下的估计器-控制器对路由来实现波动率控制，在多个资产类别中显著提升了风险调整后收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波动率控制方法通常依赖固定波动率估计或预定义规则，难以适应变化的市场条件，本文旨在提出一种更灵活、自适应的控制框架。

Method: 将波动率控制建模为基于市场状态的估计器-控制器对路由问题，分三阶段进行：状态推断、切换审查和配对选择；支持基于规则、可学习或大语言模型的决策模块。

Result: 在S&P 500、多资产、比特币和USDT场景下测试，VolRouter在三个场景中取得最高夏普比率；在S&P 500中夏普比率从0.952提升至1.222，最大回撤和CVaR均下降；多资产和比特币也表现改善，USDT为边界情况。消融实验表明性能提升源于相对策略评估和选择性持续切换。

Conclusion: 波动率控制可被视为随市场状态变化的策略选择问题，VolRouter通过状态感知的路由机制有效提升了风险控制的适应性和绩效。

Abstract: Volatility control converts risk estimates into portfolio exposure, yet existing approaches often rely on a fixed volatility estimator or a pre-defined control rule that may not adapt to changing market conditions.
