We propose VolRouter, a modular framework that formulates volatility control as state-conditioned routing over estimator-controller pairs. VolRouter first summarizes market conditions into a control-relevant state profile and then performs routing through three stages: state inference, switch review, and pair selection. The Router can be implemented using rule-based, learnable, or LLM-based decision modules, while portfolio actions remain generated by predefined control policies. We evaluate VolRouter across S&P 500, Multi-Asset, Bitcoin, and USDT volatility-control settings. VolRouter achieves the highest Sharpe ratio in three of four settings. On S&P 500, it improves Sharpe from 0.952 for RV + Naive Scaling to 1.222 while reducing maximum drawdown from 15.10% to 12.58% and daily CVaR from 1.76% to 1.32%. On Multi-Asset, it improves Sharpe from 1.498 to 1.540 and reduces CVaR from 1.56% to 1.18%. Bitcoin shows similar improvements in risk-adjusted performance, while USDT provides a boundary case where simpler state-aware selectors remain competitive. Ablation and sensitivity analyses show that the improvement comes from relative policy evaluation and selective persistent switching rather than simply expanding the policy library. These results suggest that volatility control can be viewed as a policy-selection problem when risk management requirements vary across market states.

</details>


### [115] [FlowGRN+: Improving Gene Regulatory Network Inference by Spline Fitting and Manifold Projection in Conditional Flow Matching (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10407)
*Tsz Pan Tong,Jun Pang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: FlowGRN+ 是一种改进的基因调控网络推断方法，通过引入样条拟合并结合投影策略提升细胞动态轨迹的时间连贯性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如 FlowGRN 在重建细胞轨迹时存在时间不连贯和依赖人工干预的问题，限制了其可重复性和下游应用。

Method: FlowGRN+ 将样条拟合集成到条件流匹配（CFM）框架中以生成更稳定的参考轨迹，并引入投影方案将样条切线投影到数据流形的局部切空间以纠正过冲问题。

Result: 在 BEELINE 基准测试中，FlowGRN+ 展现出更平滑的轨迹重建效果和具有竞争力的 GRN 推断性能。

Conclusion: FlowGRN+ 提高了单细胞 RNA 测序数据下细胞轨迹重建和基因调控网络推断的稳定性与可重复性，其设计思路可为其他基于 CFM 的细胞动态模型提供借鉴。

Abstract: Gene regulatory networks (GRNs) are fundamental in understanding cellular dynamics and underlying mechanisms during development and disease. Although scRNA-seq technologies have enabled the collection of vast numbers of gene expression profiles at single-cell resolution, inferring GRNs from scRNA-seq data remains a significant challenge due to high dimensionality and dropout. Recently, FlowGRN has shown promising results in reconstructing cell trajectories and inferring GRNs by applying conditional flow matching (CFM) to learn the cell dynamics. However, FlowGRN still faces limitations in the temporal coherence of reconstructed dynamics and relies on human inspection, which hinders downstream applications and reproducibility. In this paper, we propose FlowGRN+, an improved version of FlowGRN that integrates spline fitting into the CFM framework to generate more stable reference trajectories for training, thereby improving the temporal coherence of the learned dynamics. To address overshooting in spline fitting, we further introduce a projection scheme that projects spline tangents onto the local tangent space of the data manifold. We evaluate FlowGRN+ on the BEELINE benchmark and show improved trajectory smoothness with a competitive GRN inference performance. FlowGRN+ provides a practical framework for reconstructing cell trajectories and inferring GRNs from scRNA-seq data, and the insights from this work may also be useful for other CFM-based models of cellular dynamics.

</details>


### [116] [Geometry-aware neural causal discovery for large-scale spatiotemporal systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10466)
*Haoyang Yan,Kaiqi Zhao,Weiping Wang,Yunpeng Wang,Xiaolei Ma*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: GeoDCD是一种基于几何感知的神经网络框架，用于大规模时空因果发现，利用空间坐标构建可学习层次结构，并通过Jacobian敏感性分析生成时变有向图，在多种复杂系统中表现出优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模时空系统中变量数量多、交互关系复杂且因果结构随状态变化，传统因果发现方法难以有效处理这些问题。

Method: 提出GeoDCD框架，利用空间坐标初始化可学习的层次结构，并通过训练非线性预测器后对其输入-输出雅可比矩阵进行敏感性分析，转化为动态有向因果图。

Result: 在Lorenz-96混沌系统上F1分数达0.99，结构汉明距离相对最优基线降低36.8%，运行时间近似线性增长，成功应用于10,512节点的海平面气压数据；在实际观测中识别出与环流一致的关键路径，揭示厄尔尼诺/拉尼娜相关的结构重组，并分离能源与交通系统的相互影响。

Conclusion: GeoDCD能够在无干预条件下实现高效的大规模时空因果发现，适用于机制假设生成，尤其适合复杂动态系统中的因果结构分析。

Abstract: Causal discovery at large spatiotemporal scale is difficult: variables are physically embedded, candidate interactions grow quadratically with system size, and causal structure changes with system state. We introduce GeoDCD, a geometry-aware neural framework that uses spatial coordinates to initialize a learnable hierarchy and converts a trained nonlinear predictor into time-varying directed graphs through input-output Jacobian sensitivity analysis. On chaotic Lorenz-96 dynamics, GeoDCD attains an F1 score of 0.99, reducing structural Hamming distance by 36.8% relative to the strongest neural baseline, and still leads flat baselines when coordinates are uninformative. Runtime scales approximately linearly over the evaluated range, enabling discovery on a 10,512-node sea-level-pressure grid. Applied to observations, GeoDCD identifies circulation-consistent gateways, resolves El Nino/La Nina-dependent reorganization, and separates energy-to-traffic from traffic-to-energy influence in coupled electric-vehicle and road systems. Edges are neural-Granger sensitivities rather than interventional effects, positioning GeoDCD for mechanistic hypothesis generation where interventions are unavailable.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Polyhedral Outer-Approximations for MISOCP: Geometry and Cutting Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10055)
*Yongzheng Dai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合整数二阶锥规划的外逼近求解方法，提出了一种更高效的割平面策略和渐进整数外逼近框架，有效减少了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高混合整数二阶锥规划（MISOCP）在外逼近求解过程中的效率，需要更有效地构建线性松弛模型，减少冗余割平面并降低计算成本。

Method: 通过分析新生成割平面相对于已有割平面的边际贡献，基于违反度和体积度量，提出兼顾分离深度与角度新颖性的割平面生成策略，并设计渐进整数外逼近（progressive-integrality OA）框架，从线性规划松弛逐步过渡到完全混合整数规划。

Result: 在CBLIB实例和大规模交流机组组合模型上的实验表明，所提出的割平面策略和渐进整数框架能显著减少外逼近所需的计算工作量。

Conclusion: 结合角度新颖性导向的割平面生成和渐进整数松弛策略，能够更高效地求解混合整数二阶锥规划问题，提升外逼近算法的整体性能。

Abstract: Mixed-integer second-order cone programs are commonly solved by polyhedral outer approximation (OA), which iteratively strengthens a linear relaxation of the conic feasible region through cutting planes. We study how such approximations can be constructed more efficiently. First, we analyze the marginal contribution of a newly generated cut relative to cuts already present in the relaxation. Using violation- and volume-based measures, we show that this contribution decreases at least as fast as linearly as the new supporting direction approaches an existing one. Motivated by this geometric analysis, we develop a cut-generation strategy that balances separation depth with angular novelty and admits closed-form constructions. Second, we introduce a progressive-integrality OA framework that proceeds from an LP relaxation through partially integral relaxations before reaching the full MILP, thereby using inexpensive early iterations to strengthen the approximation before later mixed-integer solves. Computational experiments on CBLIB instances and large-scale AC unit-commitment models demonstrate complementary benefits from the proposed cut strategy and progressive integrality, substantially reducing the computational effort of outer approximation.

</details>


### [118] [Thread Scaling of Hexaly on the TDVRPTW across Two Model Encodings. An Experimental Report: External-Function Serialization, Slice-Count Choice, and the Time-Sliced Thread Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10079)
*Florian Rascoussier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种Python编码方式在Time-Dependent Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows中的多线程扩展性能，发现原生求解器内计算时间片的方式比外部回调函数更有效利用多核资源，显著提升求解质量和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同建模方式对Hexaly中多线程扩展性能的影响，特别是在时间依赖路径规划问题中的表现差异。

Method: 对比两种Python编码方式：一种通过外部回调函数计算连续行驶时间，另一种在求解器内部使用时间片近似计算；并测量其在不同线程数下的CPU使用和求解表现。

Result: 外部函数接口仅使用单个评估工作线程，无法随线程请求扩展；而原生编码能有效利用多核，随着线程增加（1到16）几乎在所有测试中都提升了求解质量与稳定性，并减少了随机种子间的差异。

Conclusion: 建模方式对多线程扩展性有决定性影响，原生时间片编码虽需验证近似精度，但能最大化多核优势，是提升求解性能的关键。

Abstract: Thread scaling in Hexaly depends first on how the Time-Dependent Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows is modeled. We compare two Python encodings: one evaluates continuous travel-time functions through external callbacks, while the other approximates them with time slices evaluated natively by the solver. Independent CPU measurements show that the supported external-function interface uses a single evaluator worker regardless of the requested thread count. The native encoding, by contrast, makes effective use of the allocated cores. Across our selected benchmark panel, wider native searches produce better solutions both at the end of the run and throughout the search, while substantially reducing variation between seeds. Nearly every paired run improves from one to sixteen threads. The choice of encoding has an even larger impact than the thread count: native evaluation provides the strongest gains, at the cost of a travel-time approximation that must be sufficiently fine and independently checked. These descriptive results support multi-threading as a practical way to improve solution quality and stability when the model can exploit it, and show that the modeling approach is central to thread-scaling conclusions in time-dependent routing.

</details>


### [119] [A New Approach for Feedback Stabilization and its Application for Data-Driven Control of Polynomial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10158)
*Diego de S. Madeira,Joao Gabriel N. Silva,Wilkley B. Correia,Antonis Papachristodoulou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于耗散性理论的非线性系统状态反馈渐近稳定新框架，适用于含噪声测量的多项式系统的数据驱动控制，并通过SOS优化提供了两种迭代设计方法，克服了传统方法需初始化控制Lyapunov函数等限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受基于耗散性控制发展的启发，旨在解决非线性系统在噪声环境下数据驱动控制的设计难题，尤其是传统SOS方法中存在的初始化依赖等问题。

Method: 利用耗散性理论构建新的稳定性条件，结合SOS优化提出两种无需初始控制Lyapunov函数的迭代状态反馈控制设计方法。

Result: 所提方法成功实现了对多项式非线性系统的有效控制，数值例子验证了其适用性和优于传统方法的性能。

Conclusion: 新框架为含噪声的数据驱动控制提供了可靠解决方案，显著提升了控制器设计的自动化程度和实用性。

Abstract: Inspired by recent developments in dissipativity-based control, this work proposes new sufficient conditions for asymptotic stabilization of nonlinear systems using state feedback. We prove that this new framework is well suited for data-driven control of polynomial systems using noisy measurements, a topic that has lately attracted considerable attention. Two iterative procedures for data-based state feedback design are provided using the proposed framework, that use sum-of-squares (SOS) optimization. Typical limitations of conventional SOS methods based on alternating (D-K) procedures for controller design, such as the need to provide an initialization for a control-Lyapunov function (CLF), are overcome in this paper. Numerical examples demonstrate the applicability and the advantages of the new strategies.

</details>


### [120] [An Optimal Energy Production Problem with Energy Source Switching and Load Following Nuclear Power Plants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10197)
*Fabio Baschetti,Alessandro Gnoatto,Athena Picarelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在高比例可再生能源系统中，负荷跟随型核电站的最优运行策略，采用有限时域最优切换模型分析其在不同经济环境下的调度决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着波动性可再生能源占比上升，净负荷需求不确定性加大，亟需低碳灵活电源提供调节能力。研究旨在评估核电站作为可调度低碳资源的经济价值及其最优运行方式。

Method: 将核电出力决策建模为有限时域最优切换问题，考虑爬坡限制和模式转换成本；通过粘性解方法求解一组Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman拟变分不等式，并采用单调半拉格朗日格式进行数值计算。

Result: 得到了闭合经济与开放经济下核电运行的最优策略；发现短缺成本、切换成本、爬坡能力和市场接入条件显著影响负荷跟随行为；量化了可控低碳容量在高比例可再生能源系统中的经济价值。

Conclusion: 核电可通过灵活运行提升系统经济性，其灵活性应通过合理的补偿机制、平衡市场设计和互联政策予以激励和支持。

Abstract: The integration of weather-dependent renewable generation increases the volatility of residual demand and raises the value of dispatchable low-carbon flexibility. This paper studies the optimal operation of a load-following nuclear power plant owned by a producer that must balance stochastic residual demand while accounting for ramping limits and costly changes in operating regimes. Nuclear output can be increased, decreased, or kept constant, and the production decision is formulated as a finite-horizon optimal switching problem. We analyze both a closed-economy benchmark, where excess production cannot be sold and shortages require costly back-up generation, and an open-economy setting, where the producer can trade electricity at prices driven by aggregate market residual demand. The value functions are characterized as viscosity solutions of a system of Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman quasi-variational inequalities, and optimal policies are computed using a monotone semi-Lagrangian scheme. The numerical results show how shortage costs, switching costs, ramping capability, and market access shape optimal nuclear load following. The analysis highlights the economic value of controllable low-carbon capacity in renewable-intensive systems and provides implications for flexibility remuneration, balancing-market design, and interconnection policy.

</details>


### [121] [Towards Co-Designed Event-Triggered Extremum Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10246)
*Roberto Luo,Pedro Henrique Silva Coutinho,Victor Hugo Pereira Rodrigues,Tiago Roux Oliveira,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件触发的多变量极值搜索方法，通过联合设计控制器和触发机制，实现了对非线性映射在Hessian不确定性下的指数收敛，并减少了通信负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件触发极值搜索方法通常先固定控制器再设计触发机制，限制了性能提升，本文旨在通过协同设计优化整体系统性能。

Method: 采用基于李雅普诺夫函数和平均法的分析方法，将控制器与触发机制的联合设计建模为具有线性矩阵不等式约束的凸优化问题。

Result: 所提方法保证了闭环系统的指数稳定性与无Zeno行为，数值结果表明全增益矩阵优于对角增益矩阵，在衰减速率和触发阈值方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 利用Hessian耦合信息的全增益矩阵控制器在性能上显著优于传统对角结构，验证了协同设计框架的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: This paper studies event-triggered gradient-based multivariable extremum seeking for nonlinear maps with polytopic Hessian uncertainty. Unlike existing event-triggered extremum-seeking methods, which first fix the controller (typically diagonal) and then design the triggering mechanism, the proposed approach jointly synthesizes the controller and the triggering mechanism through a co-design framework that admits both diagonal and full controller gain matrices. The co-design problem is formulated as a convex optimization problem with linear matrix inequality constraints. Its solution guarantees exponential convergence of the average closed-loop system with a prescribed decay rate while maximizing the admissible triggering threshold to reduce communication. Lyapunov and averaging analyses establish exponential stability of the event-triggered system, and Zeno-freeness is proved to guarantee implementability. Numerical results illustrate that diagonal gains cannot achieve the same triggering thresholds and decay rates as the full controller gain matrices, highlighting the benefits of exploiting Hessian coupling information.

</details>


### [122] [Continuous-Time Covariance Steering with Common Free-Final Time: Finite-Horizon Solutions and Infinite-Horizon Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10267)
*Akan Selim,Fengjiao Liu,Siddhartha Ganguly,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间随机线性系统在给定初末协方差矩阵之间进行状态协方差引导的最优共同自由终时问题，提出了确定性重构方法，给出了最优性条件，并设计了基于信任域线搜索的算法，通过三个实例验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决随机微分方程约束下的自由终时随机最优控制问题，实现对系统状态协方差的有效引导。

Method: 通过对SDE约束的随机最优控制问题进行确定性重构，推导最优性必要条件和充分条件，分析有限视界最优解的渐近行为，并利用哈密顿量对终时的敏感性设计信任域线搜索算法。

Result: 建立了自由终时随机最优控制问题的确定性等价形式，得到了最优终时有限的充分条件，刻画了极限情形下的不变集与轨迹，并开发了可检测无穷视界情形的数值算法。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效求解协方差引导的自由终时最优控制问题，算法在多个应用场景中表现出良好性能，为随机系统的协方差控制提供了理论与计算支持。

Abstract: This article studies the optimal common free-final time problem for steering the state covariance of a continuous-time stochastic linear system between prescribed initial and terminal covariance matrices. We first establish a deterministic reformulation of the SDE-constrained free-final time stochastic optimal control problem (SOCP). For the ensuing SOCP, we provide necessary conditions for optimality and establish sufficient conditions for the optimal common final time to be finite. Subsequently, we characterize the asymptotic behavior of the finite-horizon optimal solutions as the final time tends to infinity, the invariant sets and trajectories associated with this limiting regime, and derive the sensitivity of the Hamiltonian with respect to the common final time. Finally, leveraging these sensitivities, we develop a trust-region line-search algorithm together with an infinite-horizon case detection method, and demonstrate its performance on three different problems: (a) an illustrative covariance-steering example, (b) a demonstration of spacecraft maneuver using covariance control, and (c) Gaussian mixture to Gaussian mixture steering.

</details>


### [123] [Kullback-Leibler Mirror-Prox for Measure-Valued Variational Inequalities and Mean-Field Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10293)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ibrahim Ekren,Lu Vy,Ziqing Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于相对熵的镜像- prox方法（KL Mirror-Prox）用于求解紧致状态空间上的静态平均场均衡，通过变分不等式形式化均衡条件，并在单调性假设下证明了收敛性与误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了高效计算非光滑、非凸及非势能结构下的平均场博弈均衡，需要一种适用于紧凑状态空间且能处理离散化误差的稳定算法。

Method: 将均衡条件建模为概率测度空间上的变分不等式，采用Kullback-Leibler散度作为proximal项，设计了熵正则化的外梯度算法（KL Mirror-Prox），并在有限状态离散化上实现指数权重更新。引入Tikhonov正则化以选择相对于参考测度熵最小的均衡。

Result: 在Lasry-Lions单调性和连续性条件下，证明了网格细化的遍历平均收敛性，并给出了Minty残差和近似均衡的有限迭代误差界；在强单调条件下获得了最后、最优和平均迭代点的度量收敛速率。

Conclusion: 所提KL Mirror-Prox方法有效求解了广泛类别的平均场均衡问题，兼具理论收敛保证与数值稳定性，适用于非光滑、非凸及非势能情形，并可通过正则化选择特定均衡。

Abstract: We study the computation of static mean-field equilibria on a compact state space by formulating the equilibrium condition as a variational inequality over probability measures. We propose an entropic variant of Korpelevich's extragradient algorithm---the Kullback--Leibler Mirror-Prox method---in which Euclidean projections are replaced by relative-entropy proximal steps. Each half-step is therefore an explicit exponential reweighting of the current measure, implemented on a finite state-space discretization. Under Lasry--Lions monotonicity and continuity assumptions, we prove convergence of mesh-refined ergodic averages and obtain finite-iteration Minty-residual and approximate-equilibrium bounds that jointly quantify iteration and discretization errors. Under strong monotonicity, we derive metric convergence rates for the last, best, and averaged iterates. We also develop a KL-type Tikhonov regularization that selects the equilibrium minimizing relative entropy with respect to a reference measure. The framework applies to potential and nonpotential cost operators and does not require differentiability or convexity of the cost in the individual state.

</details>


### [124] [Attainment Boundaries and Escape Rates in Asymptotically Conic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10341)
*Vinh Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了无界凸集上线性优化的可达性边界，利用凸共轭理论分析了渐近行为与最优值的逃逸方向，并在特定锥规划中得到了显式公式和正则化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无界凸集上优化问题的可达性条件及其边界行为，尤其是在目标函数未达到最小值时的渐进行为。

Method: 采用凸共轭理论、面渐近选择定理以及对特定结构（如二阶锥）的精细分析，研究目标扰动下的极小化解的逃逸方向与最优值渐近性。

Result: 建立了内向扰动下的面渐近选择定理，确定了解的逃逸方向、爆破尺度和最优值的主导渐近项；对径向渐近锥形集合获得完整的边界三分法和普适缩放律；对平移椭球二阶锥规划给出了显式的原对偶公式及精确的逃逸、条件数和正则化速率。

Conclusion: 即使在严格原始可行、零对偶间隙和对偶可达的情况下，原始问题仍可能无法达到最优解；提出了一种无需径向对称假设的多维临界集渐近分析框架。

Abstract: We study attainment boundaries for linear optimization over unbounded convex sets. For epigraphs of finite convex functions, convex conjugacy separates recession-cone copositivity, boundedness below, and attainment through three nested subsets of conjugate space. At a finite but unattained boundary value, we establish a facewise asymptotic selection theorem for inward perturbations of the objective. The theorem identifies the escaping directions of the perturbed minimizers, their precise blow-up scale, and the leading asymptotics of the optimal value; the critical set may be multidimensional, and no radial symmetry is assumed. For radial asymptotically conic epigraphs, we obtain a complete boundary trichotomy and universal scaling laws for objective tilting, hard truncation, and power regularization. For shifted ellipsoidal second-order cone programs, we derive explicit primal--dual formulas together with sharp escape, conditioning, and regularization rates. These models also admit an exact robust-optimization representation. At the attainment boundary, strict primal feasibility, zero duality gap, and dual attainment can coexist with failure of primal attainment.

</details>


### [125] [Submillisecond Sequential Convex Optimization for Powered Landing via Dynamics Condensation and xPIPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10395)
*Wenbo Li,Ziqi Xu,Dai Shen,Shengping Gong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于低延迟动力着陆的压缩序列凸逼近方法，通过精确块消元和简化计算结构实现了亚毫秒级的端到端求解速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对动力着陆中因质量变化、自由终端时间和二次气动阻力导致的高计算成本问题，需要降低在线求解局部凸子问题的耗时，尤其是长动力学等式链和内层迭代带来的开销。

Method: 采用精确块消元技术去除中间状态变量和区间方程，并引入低权重能量项与固定二次邻近正则化，使代理问题强凸且曲率可预测；内层求解器使用基于固定大小数组和3x3区间求解的外推比例-积分投影梯度法（xPIPG），并通过一-pass节点映射进一步降低计算成本。

Result: 在Intel Core i7-10875H上，单次规划的中位端到端求解时间为374微秒，P99为512微秒；所有100个初始状态扰动均通过验证，节点数在15–51范围内时中位时间仍低于0.7毫秒；独立高精度积分验证终端位置误差为0.183米。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个在给定模型、硬件和定时边界下实现亚毫秒级端到端求解的单条动力着陆轨迹序列凸优化方法，适用于对低延迟要求极高的实际应用。

Abstract: Powered landing with variable mass, free final time, and quadratic aerodynamic drag requires the repeated solution of local convex subproblems, whose main online cost lies in the long dynamics-equality chain and the inner iterations. This paper develops a condensed sequential convex approximation designed for low latency. Exact block elimination removes 217 intermediate-state components and 210 interval equations from a 31-node model, leaving 100 primal variables coupled by six terminal equalities. A low-weight energy term and fixed quadratic proximal regularization make the ideal surrogate strongly convex with predictable curvature. The inner solver is an extrapolated proportional--integral projected gradient (xPIPG) implemented with fixed-size arrays, $3\times3$ interval solves, and a fused one-pass node map. The one-pass map is a deliberate low-cost approximation, not the exact joint proximal operator. We therefore evaluate the timed code by nonlinear trajectory residuals and independent physical checks rather than by a claim of exact KKT convergence. The single-precision C implementation completes one plan in four outer updates and 336 xPIPG updates. On an Intel Core i7-10875H, the median end-to-end solve time is \SI{374}{\micro\second} and the P99 value is \SI{512}{\micro\second}. All 100 common initial-state perturbations pass validation, and the median remains below \SI{0.7}{\milli\second} for 15--51 nodes. An independent high-accuracy first-order-hold integration gives a terminal position error of \SI{0.183}{\meter}. Within the stated model, hardware, stopping rule, and timing boundary, this is, to the authors' knowledge, the first submillisecond end-to-end sequential-convex solve for a single powered-landing trajectory.

</details>


### [126] [A lower bound for stepsize-based acceleration of gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10418)
*Jianhao Ma,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在光滑凸优化中，仅通过预设的步长策略对梯度下降法进行加速的极限。作者提出了一个新的下界Ω(T^{-1.9319})，表明单纯依靠步长调度无法使梯度下降达到最优的O(T^{-2})收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有工作显示通过精心设计的步长策略可以提升梯度下降的收敛速度，但关于此类方法的下界仍不清楚，尤其是相较于经典的Ω(T^{-2})下界而言。因此，探索仅靠步长调度的理论极限具有重要意义。

Method: 通过构造特定的优化问题和分析梯度下降在预设非负步长序列下的行为，推导出最后一个迭代点的收敛速率的新下界。证明过程借助GPT-5.6 Sol Pro在作者指导下完成。

Result: 得到了梯度下降配合预定步长策略时的最后迭代收敛率的新下界Ω(T^{-1.9319})，说明其无法达到O(T^{-2})的最优速率。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖步长调度不能将梯度下降加速至最优收敛率，揭示了此类加速方法的理论局限性。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that, for smooth convex optimization, plain gradient descent can be accelerated from its textbook convergence rate of $O(T^{-1})$ (where $T$ denotes the number of iterations) to $O\big(T^{-\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})}\big)$ using carefully designed stepsize schedules alone, without resorting to momentum or other algorithmic modifications. Despite this progress, however, little was known about lower bounds for such methods beyond the classical $Ω(T^{-2})$ benchmark for general first-order methods. In this work, we present a new lower bound of $Ω(T^{-1.9319})$ for the last-iterate convergence rate of gradient descent with predetermined nonnegative stepsize schedules. This result provides rigorous evidence that stepsize schedules alone cannot accelerate plain GD to the optimal $O(T^{-2})$ convergence rate. The proof was developed by GPT-5.6 Sol Pro under the authors' guidance.

</details>


### [127] [Indefinite Stochastic Linear-Quadratic Optimal Control Problem for a Markov Regime-Switching Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10597)
*Na Li,Yilin Wei,Harry Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有马尔可夫体制转换的不定随机线性二次控制问题，提出了一种松弛补偿器方法，推导了无约束和有约束情况下的最优控制及相应的Riccati方程，并通过资产配置实例验证了理论的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将传统的正定随机LQ控制推广到不定情形，并处理带有马尔可夫体制切换的复杂随机系统控制问题。

Method: 基于SLQ问题的适定性，引入松弛补偿器，分析随机哈密顿系统并求解相应的Riccati方程，得到开环与闭环最优控制形式。

Result: 得到了无约束和非负约束下最优开环控制及其闭环反馈表达式，建立了对应的广义Riccati方程系统。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功扩展了SLQ控制的应用范围，数值模拟表明该框架在实际资产配置等复杂问题中具有良好的适用性和有效性。

Abstract: This paper investigates an indefinite stochastic linear-quadratic (SLQ) control problem with parameters subject to Markov regime-switching. Based on the well-posedness of the SLQ problem, we introduce a relaxed compensator that extends SLQ control problems from the positive definite case to the indefinite case. We analyze the corresponding stochastic Hamiltonian system for both unconstrained and constrained control cases under the indefinite framework and derive the corresponding optimal open-loop controls. We further investigate the associated Riccati equations for both unconstrained and constrained control cases and derive the closed-loop feedback forms of optimal controls. We illustrate the theoretical results with an equity-bond asset allocation problem under un-constrained and non-negative control constraints. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the theoretical framework and demonstrate its practical value in solving complex stochastic control problems with Markov regime-switching.

</details>


### [128] [Randomization and Performance Improvement for Integer Optimal Control with Total Variation Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10624)
*Robert Baraldi,Paul Manns,Lars Mösezahl,Marvin Severitt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了三种用于求解混合整数PDE约束优化问题的算法（SLIP、Patch-SLIP和新提出的Randomized-Patch-SLIP），通过在图像去噪和隐身等基准问题上的实验，评估了不同改进技术组合下的性能，并提供了开源代码包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于混合整数PDE约束优化问题在组合复杂性和模型评估方面均具有挑战性，且现有算法常受限于问题规模，因此需要系统地评估和改进现有算法性能。

Method: 数值分析SLIP和Patch-SLIP两种已有算法，并提出一种新的随机域块选择算法Randomized-Patch-SLIP，证明其收敛性；结合文献中的多种改进技术进行综合性能比较。

Result: 给出了不同算法与改进技术组合下的计算结果，在图像去噪和隐身问题上建立了基准测试，验证了各方法的性能表现。

Conclusion: 本文系统总结了当前最优的算法改进组合，为混合整数PDE约束优化问题提供了有效的求解策略和可复现的开源工具支持。

Abstract: Mixed-integer PDE-constrained optimization problems are computationally challenging due to both the combinatorial nature of integer programming as well as evaluation of the model. Many algorithms and subsequent performance improvements have been developed to solve these problems, but they are often limited by problem size. We numerically analyze two such algorithms: SLIP and Patch-SLIP, which solve trust-region subproblems over either the full or partial domain, respectively. We additionally propose and prove convergence of a randomized third algorithm, Randomized-Patch-SLIP, which solves trust-region subproblems over randomly selected patches of the domain. We compare performance of all three algorithms with various improvement techniques found throughout the literature; the purpose of this work is to document the best combinations of these improvements in conjunction with various solvers. We additionally establish benchmark problems in image denoising and cloaking. Computational results are reported on combinations of algorithms and improvements, and code used in the experiments is provided as a package.

</details>


### [129] [Robust Unit Commitment in District Heating Networks: Chance-Constrained and CVaR Optimization Under Demand Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10899)
*Annika Buchholz,Janina Zittel,Thorsten Koch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合机会约束规划和条件风险价值优化的方法，用于在热需求不确定性下优化区域供热网络的机组调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于热需求的高度不确定性，区域供热网络的运行规划面临挑战，本文旨在在不牺牲系统可靠性的前提下应对这种波动性。

Method: 将机会约束规划（CC）和条件风险价值（CVaR）优化方法引入混合整数机组组合问题，并利用贝叶斯模型生成具有预测分布的热需求时间序列作为输入。

Result: 在柏林实际供热网络数据和一组合成基准实例上评估了两种方法，在解的质量、风险暴露和计算开销方面进行了比较。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能有效处理热需求不确定性，在保证系统可靠性的同时实现优化调度，且CVaR和CC方法各有适用场景。

Abstract: While district heating networks are a key component of the energy transition, their operational planning is challenging due to substantial uncertainty in heat demand. We address this volatility without compromising system reliability. By adapting chance-constrained programming (CC) and conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) optimization to the mixed-integer unit-commitment problem for district-heating networks, we can compute optimal unit-commitment schedules under demand uncertainty. We generate heat demand time series using a Bayesian model, which explicitly quantifies forecasting uncertainty and yields predictive distributions as input to the network optimization. To handle the inherent uncertainty in heat demand, we apply both robust optimization approaches: chance-constrained programming limits the probability of unmet demand, while CVaR optimization penalizes severe shortfalls in the tail of the distribution. We evaluate the approaches both on real-world data from the Berlin district heating network and on a benchmark set of synthetic, realistically parameterized instances of varying sizes. The results compare the two uncertainty-handling methods with respect to solution quality, risk exposure, and computational effort.

</details>


### [130] [Robust Patrol in a Dispersed Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10935)
*Edward Mellor,Kevin Glazebrook,Kyle Lin,Rob Shone*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种巡逻问题，巡逻者在多个地理位置分散的地点之间移动以检测随时间到达的攻击者。当旅行时间可忽略时，提出了一种最优循环策略；当考虑旅行时间时，提出了两种基于实际巡逻实践的循环巡逻模式，并给出了检测攻击的期望时间公式及优化搜索时间参数的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了有效应对地理分散区域中的动态攻击，设计高效的巡逻策略以最小化攻击者未被发现的时间。

Method: 建立巡逻模型，分析无旅行时间和有旅行时间两种情况下的循环巡逻策略，推导检测时间期望公式，并提供优化参数的算法。

Result: 得到了无旅行时间情况下的最优循环策略，在考虑旅行时间时提出了两种实用的循环巡逻模式并能计算其性能指标，且设计了参数优化算法。

Conclusion: 最优巡逻策略高度依赖于具体问题的结构和参数，所提出的循环模式在多种场景下表现良好。

Abstract: We consider a patrol problem in which a patroller moves among several geographically dispersed locations to detect attackers that arrive over time. Once the patroller arrives at a location, they can spend any amount of time searching for attackers at that location---and detect an attacker there with some location-dependent instantaneous detection rate---before moving to a different location. The objective of the patroller is to minimize the expected time an attacker stays undetected at a location, regardless of where and when the attack occurs. In the special case where travel times are negligible, we elucidate an optimal cyclic policy in which the patroller allocates a fixed fraction of their effort to each location continuously. The patrol problem becomes significantly more challenging when travel times cannot be ignored. We introduce two types of cyclic patrol patterns based on common patrol practice for perimeter patrol and border patrol, respectively, and derive formulae for the expected time to detect an attack in both cases. We also provide an algorithm for finding the best search time parameters in both of these cases. We give several examples where these cycle types perform well and numerically demonstrate that the optimal patrol policy depends highly on the structure and parameters of each patrol problem.

</details>


### [131] [Threshold Structure of Optimal Policies in Restart POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10936)
*Konstantin Avrachenkov,Alexey Piunovskiy,Yi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类在一般Bore尔空间上的重启型部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Restart POMDP），通过引入充分统计量将问题转化为完全可观测的MDP，并在不同成本准则下证明了最优策略具有关于时间的阈值结构，进一步在状态空间有序和核函数单调条件下分析了阈值随状态的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于系统状态不可直接观测，控制器需在持续运行与重启观测之间权衡，旨在降低长期累积成本，因此需要设计具备结构化性质的最优策略以提高决策效率。

Method: 利用最后一次观测状态和距上次重启的时间作为充分统计量，将原POMDP转化为完全可观测的MDP；结合自然的成本劣化条件和随机单调性假设，采用动态规划方法分析不同成本准则下的最优策略结构，并通过vanishing discount方法处理平均成本情形。

Result: 证明了在折扣和无折扣总成本下，最优策略在经过时间上具有阈值结构；当状态空间部分有序且转移核满足随机单调性时，最优阈值随状态非增；对平均成本情形，在几何遍历性和瞬态收益控制条件下也建立了类似的阈值结果。

Conclusion: 重启型POMDP的最优策略具有清晰的阈值结构，该结构依赖于时间与状态的联合特性，为实际系统中的感知-决策平衡提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study a Restart POMDP (Partially Observable Markov Decision Process) on a general Borel state space, where the controller either lets the hidden state evolve unobserved or restarts the system and observes the new state. Exploiting a sufficient-statistic representation consisting of the last observed state and the elapsed time since restart, we reduce the problem to a fully observed MDP. Under a natural one-step cost deterioration condition, we prove that optimal policies have a threshold structure in the elapsed time for both the discounted and total undiscounted cost criteria. When the state space is partially ordered and the kernel is stochastically monotone, we further show that the optimal threshold is nonincreasing in the state. For the average cost criterion, under additional assumptions of geometric ergodicity and domination of the transient gain, we establish analogous threshold results via the vanishing discount approach, after showing the uniform boundedness of the optimal thresholds and relative value functions.

</details>


### [132] [Prescribed-performance position tracking for infinite-dimensional passive systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10940)
*Anthony Hastir,Timo Reis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于被动速度输出的无限维线性系统的位置控制问题，提出了一种带有动态增益调节机制的非线性递归漏斗控制器，确保了闭环系统的全局存在性、唯一性和预定性能，并证明了系统信号的有界性及最终增益的恒定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有分布或边界控制与观测的无限维线性被动系统，实现高精度的位置跟踪控制，尤其是在仅能获取被动速度输出的情况下，解决传统微分方法带来的噪声放大问题。

Method: 在系统节点框架下，设计一种非线性递归漏斗控制器，引入动态增益监管机制；利用跟踪误差及其导数（由被动输出直接获得）进行反馈，避免数值微分；当归一化递归误差接近预设阈值时，监管器自动增加阻尼增益。

Result: 证明了闭环轨迹的全局存在唯一性、规定的漏斗性能、导数跟踪误差和被动输出的全局有界性；在附加位置兼容严格耗散条件下，监督增益仅被有限次增加并最终保持恒定，从而保证增益、内部状态和控制输入的全局有界性以及时间上一致的递归误差漏斗裕度。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制器有效实现了无限维被动系统的位置控制，兼具鲁棒性与性能保证，适用于如带均匀分布阻尼的边界驱动欧拉-伯努利梁等机械系统。

Abstract: We study funnel control for linear infinite-dimensional passive systems whose controlled output is obtained by integrating the passive output. In mechanical applications, this corresponds to position control based on a measured passive velocity output. Within the system-node framework, we propose a nonlinear recursive funnel controller with a dynamic gain supervisor for systems with distributed or boundary control and observation. The supervisor automatically increases a damping gain whenever the normalized recursive error approaches a prescribed monitoring level. The controller uses the tracking error and its derivative, with the latter obtained directly from the passive output rather than by numerical differentiation. We prove global existence and uniqueness of the closed-loop trajectory, prescribed funnel performance, and global boundedness of the derivative tracking error and the passive output. Under an additional position-compatible strict dissipativity condition, the supervised gain is increased only finitely many times and eventually becomes constant. Consequently, the gain, the internal state, and the control input are globally bounded, and the recursive-error funnel margin is uniform in time. The results are illustrated by a boundary-actuated Euler-Bernoulli beam with uniformly positive distributed damping.

</details>


### [133] [DD-suite: A cross-platform package to build Decision Diagrams for optimization purposes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10957)
*Antonia F. Blanco,Margarita Castro,Rodrigo Toro Icarte*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: DD-suite是一个开源、跨平台的决策图（DD）软件包，支持离散优化问题的建模与求解，具有Python和C++接口，具备高效性能和良好可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策图代码多针对特定算法或应用，复用困难，限制了其广泛应用。

Method: 开发了名为DD-suite的开源软件包，支持构建精确、受限和松弛的决策图，提供统一建模接口、多种算法功能及可视化工具，并设计为可扩展的模块化架构。

Result: 实现了Python和C++版本，C++比Python快5-6倍，性能接近专用Rust框架ddo；提供了文档、示例和测试套件，验证了其高效性与可用性。

Conclusion: DD-suite是一个高效、可扩展且易于使用的决策图构建工具，有助于推动决策图在离散优化中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: Decision diagrams (DDs) have become a powerful tool for discrete optimization, supporting a wide range of algorithms that span cut-generation procedures, decomposition methods, and specialized branch-and-bound searches. Despite this growth, their adoption remains limited, partly because most existing DD code is tailored to a specific algorithm or application and is therefore hard to reuse. We introduce DD-suite, a cross-platform, open-source software package for building and manipulating DDs for discrete optimization. DD-suite is available in both Python and C++ through a shared modeling interface, and lets users construct exact, restricted, and relaxed DDs for any discrete optimization problem expressed in recursive form. The package implements the DD reduction procedure, shortest-path routines for obtaining primal and dual bounds, a visualization tool, and an extensive automated test suite. Furthermore, it includes extensive documentation, a support webpage, and ready-to-use examples for four combinatorial problems. Rather than a closed solver, DD-suite is designed as an extensible building block: users can add new construction mechanisms or run custom algorithms on top of the resulting diagram, as we illustrate with a DD-based cutting plane procedure embedded in a state-of-the-art mixed-integer programming solver. Our numerical experiments show that the C++ implementation is 5-6 times faster than the Python one while producing identical diagrams, and remains within a small constant factor of ddo, a specialized Rust framework, confirming that DD-suite combines an accessible, extensible codebase with competitive performance.

</details>


### [134] [Gromov-Wasserstein Quantization and Clustering: Structure, Rates, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11016)
*Florian Beier,Stephan Eckstein*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) 量化问题，提出了一种与k-均值算法类似的Lloyd算法来近似求解，并展示了其在3D形状和神经网络结构剪枝等建模中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类方法如k-均值依赖于固定空间内的Wasserstein距离进行量化，无法捕捉空间几何结构的变化。本文旨在通过引入Gromov-Wasserstein量化，实现对数据分布及其所在空间几何结构的同时聚类，拓展聚类的应用范围。

Method: 提出Gromov-Wasserstein量化框架，证明解的存在性，并基于理论特性设计类似Lloyd的迭代算法进行数值求解。同时分析欧氏几何下GW量化的收敛速率，并与标准Wasserstein量化速率进行比较。

Result: 证明了GW量化问题解的存在性，给出了可用于设计算法的特征刻画；计算了典型欧氏几何下的量化速率，并建立了与Wasserstein速率的关系；数值实验表明该方法在3D形状分析和神经网络剪枝等任务中具有良好的建模能力和近似性能。

Conclusion: Gromov-Wasserstein量化为聚类提供了新的视角，不仅能聚类点数据，还能聚类其背后的几何结构，所提出的算法有效且具备理论支持，展现出比传统方法更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental class of data analysis techniques with the most important representatives being centroid-based methods like $k$-means. Such methods are strongly connected to quantization problems, which aim to approximate general probability measures with discrete ones. For example, $k$-means corresponds to quantization with respect to the Wasserstein distance. While Wasserstein quantization clusters points within a fixed space, this paper studies Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) quantization, which additionally aims at clustering the ambient geometry of the space. We show existence of solutions to the GW quantization problem and give a characterization that justifies an analogue to the $k$-means algorithm (Lloyd's algorithm) to approximate them numerically. We further calculate the quantization rate for usual Euclidean geometries that are used in the GW context, and relate it to standard Wasserstein quantization rates. Finally, numerical experiments show that GW quantization opens up many modeling possibilities beyond normal clustering methods (e.g., for geodesic distances of 3D shapes or structured pruning of neural networks) and that the introduced algorithm leads to useful numerical solutions with approximation quality often in line with theoretically optimal rates.

</details>


### [135] [Integrated Surgical Scheduling with Weekend Bed Occupancy Management: A Column Generation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11018)
*Sara Cambiaghi,Saverio Basso,Davide Duma,Matteo Salani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合优化框架，用于解决在周末床位容量受限情况下的主外科调度与外科病例分配问题，通过列生成算法有效求解大规模实例，并分析了床位可用性、手术室利用率和患者优先级之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于周末床位资源减少，医院面临手术安排与床位分配之间的协调难题，影响患者接入、运营效率和医护人员工作负担，因此需要一个综合优化方法来平衡这些因素。

Method: 建立了一个多目标混合整数线性规划模型，并采用列生成算法（CG），其中子问题建模为资源约束最短路径问题，以高效求解大规模实例。该框架支持块状、开放及改进块状排班策略。

Result: 列生成方法在求解质量和计算时间上均优于直接求解MILP模型，尤其适用于大规模问题；实验揭示了周末床位占用、手术室利用率与患者优先级之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够帮助医院在资源受限情况下更好地平衡患者接入、运营效率和员工 workload，为手术调度决策提供了有效的管理启示。

Abstract: This paper addresses the integrated Master Surgical Scheduling Problem and Surgical Case Assignment Problem under weekend bed capacity constraints. We propose a joint optimization framework that simultaneously determines the assignment of specialties and surgeons to Operating Room (OR) blocks, the selection and assignment of patients to these blocks, and the sequencing of surgeries, while accounting for reduced bed availability during weekends. The model is formulated as a multi-criteria Mixed-Integer Linear Program (MILP) that prioritizes the total priority-weighted amount of scheduled patients, minimizes excess weekend bed occupancy, and maximizes OR utilization. To solve large instances, we develop a Column Generation (CG) algorithm in which the pricing subproblem is formulated as a Resource-Constrained Shortest Path Problem. The framework accommodates block, open, and modified block scheduling policies. Computational experiments show that the CG approach outperforms the direct MILP solution in terms of solution quality and computational time, particularly for larger instances. We further analyze the trade-offs between weekend bed availability, OR utilization, and total patient priority. Our results provide managerial insights into how hospitals can balance patient access, operational efficiency, and staff workload when resources are limited.

</details>


### [136] [Certificate-based Synthesis of Coordinated Droop Control for Heterogeneous Radial Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11141)
*Georgios Pantazis,Michelle Chong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的电压证书设计方法，用于改善下垂控制的低压馈线中的电压管理，通过引入虚拟协调和仿射前馈补偿的下垂架构，实现了更精确且具有空间信息的电压边界保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构低压馈线中，传统的基于全局最坏情况的电压证书过于保守，无法准确反映电压风险的实际分布，因此需要一种能够结合局部扰动、控制器参数和馈线灵敏度的设计方法。

Method: 推导出保持局部扰动边界、下垂斜率、逆变器限制和馈线相关灵敏度的确定性逐时段、逐节点电压包络，并引入具有虚拟协调和仿射前馈补偿的下垂控制架构，通过缩放变换将联合控制器与证书设计转化为线性规划问题。

Result: 所提方法能够在所有可接受扰动下保证电压和逆变器输出在认证范围内，同时在五个用户的住宅馈线和包含四个馈线的26个用户农村网络上验证了该方法的有效性，获得了更紧致且更具空间分辨能力的电压证书。

Conclusion: 本文证明了电压证书不仅可以用于评估控制器性能，还可用于指导控制器设计，所提出的控制架构结合证书设计能有效提升低压配电网的电压调节能力与安全性。

Abstract: Voltage certificates for droop-controlled low voltage feeders are often constructed from global worst-case quantities. In heterogeneous feeders, such bounds can hide where voltage risk arises and become increasingly conservative downstream as feeder sensitivities accumulate. This paper shows that voltage certificates can be used not only to assess a given controller, but also to design it. Specifically, we derive deterministic all-time, bus-wise voltage envelopes that retain local disturbance bounds, droop slopes, inverter limits, and feeder dependent sensitivities, while recovering the worst-case certificate as a special case. To overcome the deterioration of these bounds induced by the network topology, we introduce a droop architecture with virtual coordination and affine feedforward compensation that reshapes the effective voltage sensitivity. A scaling transformation converts the joint controller and certificate design into a linear program which ensures forward invariance and the satisfaction of reactive power reserve constraints. The controller uses only selected communication links and guarantees the certified voltage and inverter bounds for all admissible disturbances. Our certificates and methodology are evaluated on two radial low voltage network benchmarks: a five-customer residential feeder and a 26-customer rural network comprising four feeders. The studies demonstrate tighter and more spatially informative certificates, while respecting inverter limits.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Ollivier's Ricci Curvature on Complex-weighted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10132)
*Yu Tian,Eleanor Wiesler,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将Ollivier-Ricci曲率推广到复权图的新方法，适用于有向和复权重网络，揭示了其与磁拉普拉斯算子及局部环结构的关系，并展示了在有向网络社区检测中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散Ricci曲率方法主要局限于无向实权重网络，难以刻画有向或复权重关系（如社会、生物、量子网络），因此需要一种更通用的曲率定义。

Method: 提出了Ollivier-Ricci曲率在复权图上的原则性扩展，推导了其与磁拉普拉斯算子的关系，并给出基于局部环结构的上下界；发展了曲率估计的计算方法。

Result: 建立了新曲率概念的基本理论性质，能够在局部邻域中关联循环结构，并通过实验验证了其在有向网络社区检测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为复杂网络（包括有向和复权重情形）提供了统一的几何分析框架，拓展了曲率工具的应用范围，具有理论与实际价值。

Abstract: Understanding the geometry of complex networks is critical for effective modeling and analysis across domains. While discrete notions of Ricci curvature have emerged as powerful tools for characterizing both local and global network structure, existing formulations are largely confined to undirected networks with real-valued weights. This limits the use of curvature-based analysis of directional and complex-weighted relations that arise naturally in many applications, from social and biological systems to quantum and signal-processing networks. In this work, we introduce a principled extension of Ollivier's Ricci curvature to complex-weighted graphs, which encompasses directed graphs as a special case. We establish fundamental theoretical properties of this new notion, including relations to the magnetic Laplacian and combinatorial upper and lower bounds that relate curvature to cycle structure in local neighborhoods. We further develop computational methods for curvature estimation and demonstrate their utility in community detection on directed networks.

</details>


### [138] [FUBU-EPSTEIN: A Large-Scale Twitter Dataset on the Jeffrey Epstein Case and Its Global Public Discourse (2019-2023)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10210)
*Michael Kreil,Tristan Manfred Stöber,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FUBU-EPSTEIN数据集，一个包含5438万条推文的大规模多维度语料库，用于研究杰弗里·爱泼斯坦刑事案件在社交媒体上的舆论动态。数据集包含情感、阴谋论、错误信息立场等标注，并进行了去标识化处理以降低隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了支持对爱泼斯坦案相关社交媒体 discourse 的计算研究，特别是关注注意力波动、阴谋论传播和归因模式的长期分析。

Method: 持续近实时采集2019年8月至2023年4月间的Twitter数据，构建包含3703万边的社会联系图，并使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型进行多维标注；发布去文本化、去标识化的衍生版本以平衡研究价值与隐私保护。

Result: 发布了FUBU-EPSTEIN数据集，包含5438万条状态、多种分类与数值标注、粗略时间信息及4615万条内部状态关系；原始数据可通过伦理与法律监管下的合作研究申请获取。

Conclusion: 该数据集为研究重大公共事件的社交媒体动态提供了高价值资源，同时通过去标识化设计有效降低了隐私泄露和平台内容再分发的风险。

Abstract: The criminal case of Jeffrey Epstein has generated a complex, long-running global discourse on digital platforms, characterized by punctuated attention shocks, conspiracy theories, and blame attribution. To facilitate the computational study of these dynamics, we introduce the FUBU-EPSTEIN dataset, a large-scale, multi-dimensional research corpus of 54.38 million Twitter statuses authored by 7.11 million users and collected between August 2019 and April 2023. The source corpus was captured continuously in near-real time and enriched with a directed social contact graph of 37.03 million edge rows and annotations from Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct covering sentiment, conspiracy and misinformation stance, toxicity, moral emotion, and related dimensions. For public distribution, we created a textless, de-identified derivative that retains one row for every deduplicated status, categorical and numeric annotations, coarse temporal information, and 46.15 million internal status relationships. It excludes tweet text, original post and user identifiers, handles, profile fields, exact timestamps, and reverse mappings. The resulting release supports longitudinal content and diffusion analyses while reducing disclosure and platform-content redistribution risks. To request access to raw data for collaborative research under ethical and legal safeguards, contact fubu.dataset@gmail.com.

</details>


### [139] [A Graph Approach to the Academic Publishing Network: A Heterogeneous Model and Structural Screening over OpenAlex Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10774)
*Robert Šamárek,Radek Martinek*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图分析的学术出版诚信评估方法，利用OpenAlex数据构建异质多变量图模型，并通过社区检测和结构指标识别异常发表模式，避免了传统计量指标的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的科学计量学指标（如h指数、影响因子）忽略了拓扑上下文，难以捕捉不正当的协同发表行为，因此需要一种更全面、可解释的方法来评估学术出版完整性。

Method: 构建一个包含七种节点类型和七种边类型的异质图模型，基于引用和合著网络的投影，使用可解释的结构指标、社区检测以及三种异常发表模式探测器进行分析，且不采用二元分类，而是提供供人工评估的排序候选名单。

Result: 在VSB-奥斯特拉瓦技术大学的数据上，该方法成功识别出真实研究团队、跨学科桥梁及异常合作与引用模式；在有外部真值的期刊数据上，发现学科广度是唯一稳健的异常信号（AUC 0.70），且基于图的开放声望指标（PageRank）比基于计数的指标更抗引文操纵。

Conclusion: 图分析为学术出版生态系统的完整性监控提供了可扩展、透明且抗操纵的新工具，优于传统孤立指标，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The academic publishing ecosystem is a vast, heterogeneous network of works, authors, institutions, journals, and topics. Traditional scientometrics reduces it to isolated tabular indicators (h-index, Impact Factor) that ignore topological context and are not designed to capture coordinated illegitimate practices. Building on our companion review, which proposed graph analysis of publishing integrity, this paper implements that approach. We define a heterogeneous multivariate graph model over OpenAlex open data (seven node types, seven edge types) and a methodology based on projections (citation and co-authorship networks), interpretable structural metrics, community detection, and three screening detectors of anomalous publishing patterns. We deliberately avoid binary classification: detectors return ranked candidates with explicit structural evidence for human assessment. On the institutional corpus of VSB - Technical University of Ostrava (2020-2025) with its one-hop citation neighbourhood, community detection recovers real research groups, centralities identify cross-disciplinary bridges, and the screenings flag dense co-authorship cliques, locally closed citation loops, and thematically isolated venues. On a second, venue-centric corpus with external ground truth (journals delisted by Scopus and DOAJ) and size-matched controls, a naive case-control design yields seemingly strong but spurious detectors (a prominence confound), whereas after matching the only robust signal is the breadth of disciplinary scope (AUC 0.70); an open graph-based prestige measure (PageRank over the journal citation network) tracks a JIF proxy while being an order of magnitude more resistant to citation gaming than count-based indicators. We release the method as the open-source library apnet with a reproducible CLI workflow and a web interface; the analysis runs on commodity hardware in minutes.

</details>


### [140] [Shaping the notion of #wellbeing in the therapy culture context: an analysis through Instagram narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10793)
*Ainara Larrondo-Ureta,Simón Peña-Fernández,Jordi Morales-i-Gras*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究通过大数据分析方法，探讨了Instagram上#wellbeing标签下的文本与视觉主题、用户参与度及性别偏见问题，发现该话题内容以心理健康和情感福祉为主，且呈现明显的女性化倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别Instagram上#wellbeing标签下的主要主题与子主题，评估不同内容的用户参与度，并探究视觉内容中存在的性别偏见。

Method: 采用数据挖掘与文本分析方法处理9,844条帖子，通过层次聚类将帖子分类；使用非参数统计检验评估参与度；利用计算机视觉模型分析图像并基于视觉相似性进行聚类；通过目标检测模型分析图像中的性别表征。

Result: 发现#wellbeing的内容主要围绕心理健康、自我探索、灵性和积极心理学展开，相关帖子互动度最高；身体活动与营养主题占比较低。视觉分析显示内容呈现显著的女性化特征，男性代表不足。

Conclusion: Instagram上的#wellbeing话语体系偏向女性，强调心理与精神层面的福祉，物理健康相关内容处于次要地位，平台在健康传播中存在性别表征失衡问题。

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to (1) identify textual and visual themes and sub-themes associated with the #wellbeing hashtag on Instagram, (2) assess their varying levels of engagement and (3) investigate gender bias present in the analysed visual narratives. This study employs a range of big data analysis techniques to investigate various dimensions of wellbeing on Instagram. Initially, a sample of 9,844 posts was processed using data mining and text analysis methods to identify and categorise significant hashtags, which facilitated the classification of posts into thematic clusters through hierarchical clustering. Engagement was then assessed using non-parametric statistical tests. Additionally, computer vision models were used to analyse and classify visual narratives, grouping images into communities based on visual similarities. Finally, gender representation in the images was examined using object detection models. The study reveals that the discourse around #wellbeing on Instagram is predominantly feminised and focuses primarily on mental, psychological and spiritual aspects. Therapeutic and positive psychology narratives are the most prevalent and engaging, while physical activity and nutrition play a relatively secondary role. Two major macro-narratives emerge: firstly, mental health and emotional wellbeing, often featuring hashtags related to therapy, self-discovery, spirituality and motivational quotes; secondly, though less prominently, themes and visual narratives concerning physical activity and healthy habits, emphasising exercise and nutrition.

</details>


### [141] [MARS: A framework for modelling register-based social networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10946)
*Katherine Hamilton,Irina Epure,Frank Takes*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARS图框架，用于模拟基于登记数据的社会网络生成过程，并分析其统计特性，验证了该模型在荷兰大规模社会网络中的适用性，同时探讨了空间联系强度对网络闭合性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有随机图模型难以有效分析基于登记数据的社会网络结构，阻碍了对潜在社会系统的深入理解，因此需要一种能准确反映此类网络生成机制的新框架。

Method: 提出Multiplex Affiliation-based Random Spatially-embedded (MARS) 图框架，复现基于登记数据的社会网络构建方式，并推导其在一般和特殊情况下的基本统计性质；在一个具体实现模型中验证其对荷兰全国规模社会网络的拟合能力。

Result: MARS框架能够恢复出与荷兰实际登记社会网络相似的结构性质；分析发现空间联系强度越大（即个体活动范围越广），社会网络中的闭合性越低，表明空间自由度增加与社会凝聚力下降相关。

Conclusion: MARS框架为基于登记数据的社会网络提供了有效的建模工具，有助于揭示由制度性数据构建的复杂社会结构背后的机制，并支持进一步的社会系统分析。

Abstract: Register-based social networks have become of increasing interest in countries where formal government-curated microdata is available. Due to the non-trivial generative process of register-based networks, existing random graph models fail to facilitate effective structural analysis, hindering the discovery of meaningful insights in the underlying social system. In this paper we introduce the Multiplex Affiliation-based Random Spatially-embedded (MARS) graph framework, which replicates the construction method of register-based social networks. We derive fundamental statistical properties of MARS ensembles in general and special cases. To demonstrate the applicability of the framework, we implement a simple model under the MARS framework and show that it recovers similar properties to those exhibited by the population-scale register-based social network of the Netherlands. Furthermore, we analyse the effect of spatial tie strength on closure in the network and compare our results with existing empirical findings, showing that increased spatial freedom is correlated with decreased social cohesion.

</details>

### [142] [Orbital-selective oxygen holes in cuprate ladders beyond the Zhang-Rice paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10228)
*Chengyun Hua,Tianran Chen,Isaac C. Ownby,Colin L. Sarkis,Garrett Granoth,Masaaki Matsuda,Jiaqiang Yan,Ho Nyung Lee,Jeongkeun Song,Yuya Shinohara,Masatomo Uehara,Jun Akimitsu,Oleksandr Prokhnenko,Eugen Weschke,Takeshi Egami,D. Alan Tennant*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究发现Sr14Cu24O41梯状铜氧化物中的空穴主要局域在氧的非键pπ轨道上，而非形成传统的Zhang-Rice单态，挑战了传统铜-氧杂化模型的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究梯状铜氧化物中掺杂空穴的真实轨道特征，检验Zhang-Rice单态框架的适用性。

Method: 结合O K边偏振依赖共振软X射线散射与非弹性中子散射，分析轨道占据及晶格、磁激发行为。

Result: 发现空穴主要占据在横档氧的非键O 2pz(pπ)轨道，导致异常声子模式、缺失的非共线磁涨落和反常磁激子分裂。

Conclusion: 传统基于Cu-O杂化轨道的Zhang-Rice单态模型不足以描述该体系，需重新评估铜中心模型在铜基超导体中的充分性。

Abstract: The electronic structure of the spin-ladder cuprate Sr14Cu24O41 challenges the presumed universality of the Zhang-Rice singlet (ZRS) framework and models based exclusively on Cu-O hybridized orbitals. Combining polarization-dependent resonant soft X-ray scattering at the O K-edge with inelastic neutron scattering, we show that doped holes in the Cu2O3 ladders localize predominantly in planar non-bonding O 2pz (pπ) orbitals of rung oxygen sites rather than forming conventional ZRS states. Polarization-resolved RSXS uniquely identifies this orbital assignment, while lattice and magnetic excitations reveal its coupled consequences, establishing a unified microscopic picture that excludes the conventional σ-bonded singlet. This oxygen-sublattice charge order produces an anomalous diagonal stretching phonon and explains the absence of incommensurate magnetic fluctuations and anomalous magnon splitting. These findings motivate a reassessment of hole pairing in ladder cuprates and the sufficiency of copper-centric models for cuprate superconductors.

</details>


### [143] [Some features of high-temperature superconductivity on flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10231)
*V. R. Shaginyan,A. Z. Msezane,S. A. Artamonov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了平带如何在排斥配对相互作用下促成高温超导体的形成，并发现平带在超导态中会发生倾斜，使有效质量变为有限值，从而维持超流重量和超电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨平带在高温超导中的作用，尤其是在排斥相互作用下的超导机制。

Method: 理论分析平带在超导态中的行为及其对超流性质的影响。

Result: 发现平带在超导态中发生倾斜，有效质量变为有限，超流重量和超电流得以维持。

Conclusion: 平带的存在有助于形成高温超导态，即使在排斥相互作用下也能维持超导性质，结果与实验数据吻合良好。

Abstract: In this letter, we examine how the presence of flat band leads to the formation of a high-temperature superconductor even in the case of repulsive pairing interactions. We also show that in the case of flat bands, the high-temperature superconducting state deforms the flat band, tilting it and making the effective mass finite. As a result, neither the superfluid weight nor the supercurrent disappear. Our results are in good agreement with experimental data.

</details>


### [144] [Observation geometry for uncertainty-aware Hamiltonian inference and experimental design in quantum magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10350)
*Roy Liu,Venugopal Ranganathan,David Dahlbom,Shizhou Xu,Tianyu Zhang,Yuan Ni,Daniel M. Pajerowski,Garrett Granroth,Thomas Strohmer,Matthew B. Stone,Andrew F. May,Mark D. Lumsden,Joshua J. Turner,Yongqiang Cheng,Zhantao Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一种人工智能驱动的框架，用于不确定性感知的哈密顿量推断和自适应实验设计，结合神经网络代理与贝叶斯推断，实现了对量子材料中微观相互作用的可解释性推断与多模态实验优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从光谱和散射实验数据中准确确定微观相互作用是理解量子材料的关键，但现有方法难以判断哪些相互作用可被可靠识别，且缺乏对实验模态设计的指导，因此需要一种能量化不确定性和指导实验的通用框架。

Method: 结合哈密顿量条件下的神经网络代理、贝叶斯推断和观测几何，利用多模态中子散射数据（粉末和单晶）对NiPS$_{3}$进行分析，实现对哈密顿参数空间的后验不确定性传播和物理可解释推断。

Result: 成功实现了对NiPS$_{3}$微观相互作用的可解释哈密顿量推断，量化了不同实验模态下的参数可识别性，并展示了如何通过自适应实验设计减少不确定性。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子材料中的微观特性表征和多模态实验设计提供了通用且不确定性感知的解决方案，具有广泛的适用前景。

Abstract: Determining microscopic interactions from spectroscopic and scattering measurements is central to understanding quantum materials, yet it often remains unclear which interactions can be reliably revealed by the available experimental data and how additional experimental modalities should be designed to resolve the remaining ambiguities. Here we present an artificial intelligence-enabled framework for uncertainty-aware Hamiltonian inference and adaptive experimental design. By combining Hamiltonian-conditioned neural surrogates with Bayesian inference and observation geometry, the framework characterizes how measurements constrain Hamiltonian parameter space, quantifies the identifiability of microscopic interactions, and propagates posterior uncertainty directly in the physical Hamiltonian parameter space rather than an abstract learned representation. Using multimodal powder and single-crystal inelastic neutron scattering measurements of the quantum magnet NiPS$_{3}$, we demonstrate physically interpretable Hamiltonian inference, modality-aware uncertainty quantification, and adaptive experimental design. The framework provides a general strategy for uncertainty-aware microscopic characterization and multimodal experimental design across quantum materials.

</details>


### [145] [Role of Goldstone mode in nonequilibrium insulator under DC electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10547)
*Xi Chen,Jong E. Han*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过Goldstone模式激发来破坏有序绝缘体的新机制，该机制下玻色子的阈值场显著小于电子阈值场，可能解释实验中观察到的低阈值电场下的绝缘体-金属转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释在直流电场下电子系统中电阻击穿的阈值场远低于单电子激发理论（如Landau-Zener理论）预测值的现象。

Method: 利用Keldysh格林函数形式体系，数值模拟了非平衡稳态下Goldstone模式的行为及其对电场的响应。

Result: 发现Goldstone模式在非平衡稳态下仍保持良好定义，其非平衡激发对电场敏感，且其有效温度上升速度远超电子有效温度。

Conclusion: 集体相位动力学可能通过纯电子机制进一步降低绝缘体-金属转变所需的电场强度至实验可观测范围。

Abstract: Measurements of resistive breakdown in electronic systems under a DC electric field have shown that the threshold fields for the insulator-to-metal transition are significantly lower than predicted by single-electron excitation scenarios, such as the Landau-Zener theory. In this work, we propose an alternate mechanism of destabilizing ordered insulators under a DC electric field by fluctuations of the order parameters through the Goldstone mode excitation. The low-energy bosonic excitations receive energy from accelerated electrons and thus destroy the spontaneous symmetry breaking. Using the Keldysh Gree's function formalism, we numerically confirm that the Goldstone mode remains well-defined in the nonequilibrium steady state, while its nonequilibrium excitations are sensitive to the electric field. The effective temperature of the Goldstone mode increases much more rapidly than the electronic effective temperature, with the bosonic threshold field significantly smaller than the electronic one, which suggests that collective phase dynamics may further reduce the transition field to the experimental range via a purely electronic mechanism.

</details>


### [146] [Carrier-tunable RKKY magnetism in a crystalline magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10574)
*Xiang S. W. Huang,Bruno K. Saika,Satoshi Hamao,Yuki Majima,Yuki Settai,Hideki Matsuoka,Yuki M. Itahashi,Masato Sakano,Taro Nakajima,Shinichiro Seki,Yoshihiro Iwasa,Kyoko Ishizaka,Masaki Nakano*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过分子束外延和退火技术，在单一晶体宿主Cr-NbSe2中独立调控载流子密度和磁矩几何结构，实现了对RKKY相互作用的解耦调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于RKKY相互作用的巡游磁体中，交换耦合依赖于磁矩间距r和费米波矢kF，但体相合成中这两个参数通常耦合在一起，难以独立调控。本研究旨在通过薄膜方法在单一晶体宿主中实现两者的独立调节。

Method: 采用分子束外延（MBE）在NbSe2宿主中生长出两种不同结构的Cr掺杂相（2×2R0° Cr1/4NbSe2 和 √3×√3R30° Cr1/3NbSe2），并通过控制退火温度调节载流子密度，同时保持Cr超结构不变。

Result: 两种初始生长相在电子结构、磁基态和输运性质上存在差异；随着退火温度变化，霍尔响应系统性演化，而磁响应在结构不敏感的情况下发生变化，仅在超过临界退火温度时在同一√3×√3R30°结构类中出现铁磁序；霍尔效应的幅值和符号变化表明系统处于低载流子密度状态，kF对外部调控敏感。

Conclusion: 在单一晶体Cr-NbSe2中实现了载流子敏感的RKKY磁性，验证了通过MBE结合退火方法可独立调控载流子密度与磁矩排列，该策略可推广至插层过渡金属硫族化合物体系。

Abstract: In itinerant magnets governed by the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interaction, the exchange coupling depends on both the moment-moment distance $r$ and the Fermi wavevector $k_{\mathrm{F}}$, yet in bulk synthesis the two are tightly coupled: a change in composition typically alters both. Here, we use a thin-film approach to tune these two variables independently in a single crystalline host. Using molecular-beam epitaxy (MBE), we stabilize either $2\times2\mathrm{R}0^\circ$ Cr$_{1/4}$NbSe$_2$ or $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}\mathrm{R}30^\circ$ Cr$_{1/3}$NbSe$_2$ within the same NbSe$_2$ host through separate growth windows. Controlled post-growth annealing performed across a series of temperatures then modifies the carrier density while leaving the Cr superstructure intact below a structural-transition threshold. The two as-grown phases are distinct in electronic structure, magnetic ground state, and transport. Along this annealing series, the Hall response evolves systematically while the magnetic response changes in a structurally insensitive manner, with ferromagnetic order emerging within the same $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}\mathrm{R}30^\circ$ structural class only above a critical annealing temperature, experimentally disentangling carrier density and moment geometry. The Hall magnitude and sign evolution point to a low-carrier-density system in which $k_{\mathrm{F}}$ is susceptible to modest external tuning. Cr-NbSe$_2$ thus realizes carrier-sensitive RKKY magnetism in a single crystalline host, within an MBE-plus-annealing approach extensible across the intercalated transition-metal dichalcogenide family.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum Mechanism of Piezomagnetism in Higher-Spin Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10735)
*Daisuke Yamamoto,Makoto Naka*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了具有易面单离子各向异性的高自旋反铁磁体中的压磁性，发现量子涨落是压磁响应的微观来源，且整数自旋和半整数自旋系统在低能激发行为上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高自旋反铁磁体中压磁性的微观机制，并理解量子涨落在其中的作用。

Method: 采用味波方法研究高自旋集体模式的量子涨落对压磁响应的影响。

Result: 整数自旋系统中，相关激发模式软化并演变为类似希格斯的振幅模式，显著增强压磁性；而半整数系统中，高自旋分支与低能偶极部分离，抑制其对响应的贡献。

Conclusion: 宏观压磁性与多极激发的低能命运直接相关，压磁性可作为探测高自旋量子动力学的手段。

Abstract: We investigate piezomagnetism in higher-spin altermagnets with easy-plane single-ion anisotropy using a flavor-wave approach. We show that quantum fluctuations of higher-spin collective modes provide a microscopic origin of the piezomagnetic response. For integer spins, the relevant branch softens and evolves into a Higgs-like amplitude mode on approaching the large-$D$ transition, endowing the excitation with a sizable dipolar component and producing a pronounced enhancement of piezomagnetism. By contrast, in half-integer systems the higher-spin branches are progressively separated from the low-energy dipolar sector as the anisotropy increases, which suppresses their contribution to the response. This integer-half-integer contrast directly links macroscopic piezomagnetism to the low-energy fate of multipolar excitations. Our results establish piezomagnetism as a probe of higher-spin quantum dynamics and identify higher-spin altermagnets as a promising setting for quantum magnetoelastic responses.

</details>


### [148] [Spin Splitter without Spin-Split Bands: A Reconfigurable Altermagnetic Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10958)
*Bin Xi,Jie Lu,Qiang Luo,Ken Chen,Jia-Wei Mei,Hong-Gang Luo,Jize Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需自旋轨道耦合的新型自旋分流机制，基于阻挫蜂窝磁体中的非共面反螺旋基态，实现电场到纯横向自旋电流的转换，且无净磁化和电荷霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找不依赖自旋轨道耦合、且能灵活调控自旋流极化方向的自旋电子学材料与机制。

Method: 通过分析非共面反螺旋磁结构的对称性，特别是Q锁定的手性镜面g和反平移对称性Θ，揭示其对能带分裂与自旋分流响应的不同作用，并结合掺杂调控费米面附近的奇宇称残余分裂。

Result: 实现了无自旋分裂能带但具有显著自旋霍尔电导（σ_H^(s_y)=0.082 e²/h）的自旋分流效应，同时保持电荷霍尔为零；通过选择不同的Q取向可精确以120°步进旋转自旋极化方向。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了对称性冗余保护下的新型自旋分流机制，为设计无自旋轨道耦合、可调极化的自旋器件提供了新路径。

Abstract: The altermagnetic spin-splitter effect converts an electric field into a transverse pure spin current, with no net magnetization and no charge-Hall counterpart. In established materials this function is tied to crystal-fixed spin-split bands that lock the polarization axis to the lattice. We show that the noncoplanar counter-spiral ground state of a frustrated honeycomb magnet instead carries the altermagnetic operation through a $\mathbf Q$-locked helicity mirror $g$. The mirror selects the spin-current polarization and forbids the perpendicular one, while an antitranslation $Θ$ forbids even-parity spin splitting. Band splitting and spin-splitter response therefore rest on different symmetry elements. Either element alone enforces the charge-Hall zero---a redundancy absent from other spin--orbit-free noncollinear routes---and a charge Hall appears only when both elements are removed. Hole doping then realizes a \emph{spin splitter without spin-split bands}---the symmetry-allowed odd-parity residual below $2\times10^{-7}$ of the hopping $t$ at the Fermi level---with $σ_H^{(s_y)}=0.082\,e^2/h$ without spin--orbit coupling and with zero charge Hall response. Selecting among the three degenerate $\mathbf{Q}$ orientations rotates the polarization axis in exact $120^\circ$ steps at fixed magnitude and charge-Hall zero; the selection rules persist in a $32$-site cell accessible to programmable photonic and circuit lattices.

</details>


### [149] [Influence of interactions on the chiral effect in $1D$ Dirac semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11004)
*Maksim Ulybyshev,M. A. Zubkov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了1D SSH模型在局域Hubbard相互作用下的手性效应，发现即使在强相互作用导致的Mott绝缘相中，轴向电荷密度与电导率之间的比例关系仍保持不变，表明手性效应不受局域相互作用的重整化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在存在局域Hubbard相互作用的情况下，1D SSH模型中的手性效应是否会被重整化，特别是在系统进入Mott绝缘相时的表现。

Method: 使用数值量子蒙特卡洛模拟方法研究带有两种类型局域Hubbard相互作用的1D SSH模型。

Result: 发现在所研究的参数范围内，即使强相互作用使系统进入Mott绝缘相，诱导的轴向电荷密度与电导率之间的比例关系仍然保持不变。

Conclusion: 手性效应在局域Hubbard相互作用下不被重整化，显示出该效应在此类相互作用下的稳健性。

Abstract: We consider the 1D Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model. It was recently shown that, for the noninteracting model in its Dirac semimetal phase, the linear response of the axial charge density to an external electric field is proportional to the electrical conductivity in the presence of finite dissipation, with the proportionality factor determined by the coupling constants. This relation may be viewed as a manifestation of the chiral effect, which is a dimensional reduction of the 3D chiral magnetic effect. In the present work, we investigate the same model in the presence of two versions of local Hubbard-type interactions using numerical Quantum Monte Carlo simulations. We find, within the numerical resolution and for the parameters studied, that, even in the regime where sufficiently strong interactions drive the system into a Mott insulating phase, the proportionality between the induced axial charge density and the electrical conductivity remains unchanged. This result indicates that the chiral effect is not renormalized by local Hubbard interactions.

</details>


### [150] [Universal scaling of spatially extended zero modes in inhomogeneous SSH chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11021)
*Gilles Parez,Nicolas Crampé,Quentin Labriet,Lucia Morey,Luc Vinet*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平滑界面下的非均匀SSH模型中Jackiw-Rebbi零模的空间扩展特性，发现其晶格扩展普遍随系统尺寸的平方根缩放，形成介观临界区域，关联函数在此区域内代数衰减，并通过纠缠熵的对数增长验证了该临界区的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拓扑相变中平滑界面如何影响保护性零模的局域化特性，拓展对传统尖锐界面模型的理解。

Method: 结合精确晶格解与非均匀狄拉克描述，分析多种平滑跃迁剖面下的零模行为。

Result: Jackiw-Rebbi零模在平滑界面下空间扩展，其扩展长度普遍按系统尺寸平方根缩放；系统出现介观临界区，关联函数由代数衰减过渡到指数衰减，纠缠熵随 emergent 长度对数增长。

Conclusion: 平滑拓扑界面存在普适的临界长度尺度，主导低能物理行为，揭示了一类新的介观临界现象。

Abstract: Protected zero modes are a hallmark of topological phases of matter and are exponentially localized at sharp interfaces between distinct gapped phases. We investigate how this picture changes for smooth interfaces in a broad class of inhomogeneous Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) models. Combining an exact lattice solution with an inhomogeneous Dirac description, we show that the associated Jackiw-Rebbi zero mode becomes spatially extended. For arbitrary smooth hopping profiles, its lattice extension universally scales as the square root of the system size, independently of the microscopic details of the interface. This emergent length defines a mesoscopic critical region separating two gapped phases, within which correlations decay algebraically before crossing over to exponential decay. In addition, the entanglement entropy scales as the logarithm of the emergent length near the interface, confirming the interpretation of a mesoscopic critical region. Our results establish a universal critical length governing the low-energy physics of smooth topological interfaces.

</details>


### [151] [Competing collinear and non-collinear spin textures imaged by spatially-resolved REXS in Eu(Al0.4Ga0.6)4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11070)
*Fellipe B. Carneiro,Zéetény Bacsó,Kevin Allen,Aly H. Abdeldaim,Rebecca Scatena,Jaime M. Moya,Roger D. Johnson,Emilia Morosan,Alessandro Bombardi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用共振弹性X射线散射（REXS）揭示了Eu(Al$_{0.4}$Ga$_{0.6}$)$_4$在零场下磁自旋结构的非均匀性，发现两种磁相变源于几乎简并但正交的q矢量对，并在空间上分离为不同的自旋密度波和螺旋态区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Eu(Al$_{0.4}$Ga$_{0.6}$)$_4$中零场磁自旋结构的空间不均匀性及其起源，挑战传统全局探测手段对磁结构代表性的假设。

Method: 采用空间分辨的共振弹性X射线散射（REXS）技术，在微米尺度上分析样品表面不同区域的磁结构特征。

Result: 观测到两个分别对应T$_{N1}$ = 17 K和T$_{N2}$ = 14 K的磁转变，来源于两组正交且近乎简并的q矢量；样品中存在共存的正交自旋密度波域和螺旋域，并发现螺旋态具有反演域结构，表明反演对称性在磁相变前未被破坏；磁态为单q态，且在0.8–1 mm范围内表现出显著的空间变化。

Conclusion: 局部与全局探测的磁结构存在显著差异，说明空间分辨探针对于准确描述此类材料的磁行为至关重要。

Abstract: Here, we use resonant elastic x-ray scattering (REXS) to investigate the inhomogeneity of the zero-field magnetic spin texture of Eu(Al$_{0.4}$Ga$_{0.6}$)$_4$. By using spatially-resolved REXS, we show that the two magnetic transitions at T$_{N1}$ = 17 K and T$_{N2}$ = 14 K originate from two nearly degenerate, yet distinct, orthogonal pairs of q-vectors. The corresponding phases are segregated spatially, such that one fraction of the sample comprises coexisting orthogonal spin-density-wave domains, while another fraction hosts coexisting orthogonal helical domains. Additionally, the helical state forms inversion domains indicating that the inversion symmetry is not broken prior the magnetic transition. Our results suggest that the magnetic state is single-q and revealed a large variation of spin textures across a 0.8 - 1 mm area of the sample surface. These results demonstrate clear differences between the locally and globally probed magnetic textures, typically assumed to be representative of the system as a whole, highlighting the importance of spatially-resolved probes for accurately describing the magnetic behavior of this class of materials.

</details>


### [152] [Sum rules and density-wave modes in spin-singlet fractional quantum Hall fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11133)
*Ritajit Kundu,Rakesh K. Dora,Dung Xuan Nguyen,Ajit C. Balram*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过使用正交关联拉盖尔多项式基展开并结合大规模蒙特卡洛数据拟合，获得了双组分自旋单态Halperin和Jain分数量子霍尔流体的对关联函数及其静态结构因子的热力学拟合结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在准确描述分数量子霍尔态中的强相互作用与拓扑序，并精确计算低波数激发能隙，特别是GMP模和反对称密度波模式的能量间隙。

Method: 采用正交关联拉盖尔多项式基展开对关联函数和静态结构因子，结合蒙特卡洛模拟数据进行拟合，并施加短程行为约束和长波极限下的求和规则以提高数值稳定性与精度。

Result: 成功获得了稳定的GMP/对称密度波激发能隙；扩展方法至自旋分辨关联函数，计算了反对称密度波模式的能隙；利用密度关联函数计算变分能量并构建双层FQH系统的相图。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究双层分数量子霍尔系统提供了可靠的理论工具，有助于理解通过调节层间距和密度不平衡调控相图的实验结果。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states are prototypical examples of strongly interacting topologically ordered systems. In this work, we obtain thermodynamic fits on the plane for the pair correlation function, and its Fourier transform, the static structure factor, of two-component spin-singlet Halperin and Jain FQH fluids by expanding them in the recently introduced basis of the orthogonal associated Laguerre polynomials [Fulsebakke et al., SciPost Phys. 14, 149 (2023), https://doi.org/10.21468/SciPostPhys.14.6.149 ] and ascertaining the expansion coefficients by fitting them to large-system Monte Carlo data evaluated using their trial wavefunctions. In this fitting procedure, aside from constraining the exact short-distance behavior of the wavefunction, we also derive and enforce the sum rules that the long-wavelength expansion of the static structure factor must adhere to. We show that incorporating these constraints is crucial for obtaining numerically stable and accurate values of the long-wavelength Girvin-MacDonald-Platzman (GMP)/symmetric density-wave excitation gap. We further extend this approach to spin-resolved density-correlation functions, enabling the evaluation of the gap of the antisymmetric density-wave mode for these spin-singlet FQH states. Finally, we use the density-correlators to compute variational energies of the states and construct phase diagrams for bilayer FQH systems. These could be relevant for understanding recent bilayer FQH experiments that map out the phase diagram by tuning the interlayer separation and density-imbalance/layer-polarization.

</details>


### [153] [Floquet Green's functions for lattice electrons driven by Gaussian quantum light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11189)
*Atsushi Ono*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一个用于非相互作用单带晶格电子在外部规定的高斯量子光源驱动下的Floquet格林函数理论框架，揭示了量子光源的压缩参数和相位对能谱结构和占据分布的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子光源对凝聚态系统的影响，发展超越经典驱动的量子Floquet工程理论。

Method: 构建共享历史的四端点核与浴核卷积的格林函数形式，非微扰处理Peierls耦合，并通过数值计算分析一维模型中的谱重构和占据重分布。

Result: 发现有限耦合下量子源对经典驱动的修正，以及压缩真空和压缩相干光源导致的边带结构和占据权重变化。

Conclusion: 建立了可用于外部规定量子光源下凝聚态系统量子Floquet工程的理论框架，提供了新的调控自由度。

Abstract: We formulate Floquet Green's functions for noninteracting single-band lattice electrons driven by a reservoir-stabilized single-mode Gaussian quantum light source. The source is prescribed externally and is not updated by the many-electron polarization, while an active electronic probe still conditions the source evolution through the Peierls coupling. The two time arguments of a Green's function share one source history: the lesser and greater components are obtained by convolving a shared-history four-endpoint kernel with the continuous bath kernels before the final source trace, while the bath canonical anticommutation relation yields an equal-time covariance that seeds the retarded and advanced one-leg propagations. The Peierls coupling is treated nonperturbatively within the prescribed-source model, and classical Floquet theory is recovered in the appropriate limit. Numerical calculations on a minimal one-dimensional model show finite-coupling quantum-source corrections beyond a prescribed classical drive, together with spectral reconstruction and occupancy redistribution for squeezed vacuum and squeezed coherent sources. The squeezing parameter and phase provide additional control knobs, beyond classical amplitude modulation, for both sideband structure and occupied weight. This work provides a theoretical framework for quantum Floquet engineering of condensed matter with an externally prescribed quantum light source.

</details>
