### [154] [Unsupervised Detection of Groundwater Storage Anomalies in Ghana Using GRACE Satellite Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10233)
*George Yamoah Afrifa,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Marcellin Atemkeng*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本研究结合GRACE遥感数据与无监督机器学习方法，分析加纳2004–2024年地下水储量异常变化，揭示了显著时空变异特征，并提出适用于数据稀缺地区地下水监测的有效框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏长期实地观测，加纳的地下水变异性尚未得到充分刻画，亟需利用遥感和先进数据分析方法弥补数据空白，提升对地下水动态的理解与监测能力。

Method: 采用GRACE反演的地下水储存异常数据，结合Z-score标准化和基于集成的孤立森林（Isolation Forest）无监督机器学习模型进行异常检测，并通过统计分析和空间对比评估异常事件的时空分布特征。

Result: 发现2004–2009年持续地下水亏缺，2018年后正异常增加；共识别出12个异常月份（5次亏缺，7次盈余），最强异常为亏缺事件；北部频发亏缺，南部盈余更强；机器学习方法比传统阈值法检测到更多细微异常。

Conclusion: GRACE数据与无监督机器学习相结合，可有效监测数据稀缺地区的地下水异常变化，为加纳及其他类似地区提供了一种可行的地下水动态监测框架。

Abstract: Groundwater variability in Ghana remains poorly characterized due to limited long-term in-situ observations. This study investigates groundwater storage anomalies using GRACE-derived data from 2004-2024 combined with statistical analysis and unsupervised machine learning. Groundwater anomalies were standardized using Z-scores, while an ensemble-based Isolation Forest framework was applied for anomaly detection. The results revealed substantial temporal variability, with persistent groundwater deficits during 2004-2009 followed by increasing positive anomalies after 2018. A total of 12 anomalous months were identified, comprising 5 deficit and 7 surplus events, with the strongest anomalies associated with groundwater deficits. Spatial analysis showed more frequent deficit anomalies in northern Ghana and stronger surplus occurrence in southern regions. Comparison with statistical thresholds further indicated that the machine learning framework captured additional subtle deviations beyond conventional threshold-based methods. Overall, the integration of GRACE observations with unsupervised anomaly detection provides a practical framework for groundwater monitoring in data-scarce environments.

</details>

### [155] [Analytical Theory for Anomalous Diffusion in the Anderson Model with Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10868)
*Elizaveta Safonova,Aleksey Lunkin,Mikhail Feigel'man*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无相互作用安德森模型中由于重尾分布跃迁幅度导致反常输运的解析理论，揭示了非相互作用系统中亚扩散行为的单粒子机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无相互作用系统中是否存在类似多体局域化过渡附近的反常输运现象，探索仅由无序驱动的输运异常机制。

Method: 通过解析求解输运方程，分析平均平方位移的时间依赖性，研究从亚扩散到正常扩散的连续 crossover 行为。

Result: 发现中间时域存在亚扩散有效指数，且随着局域化相变接近，亚扩散空间范围发散，但向正常扩散的 crossover 时间保持有限。

Conclusion: 纯无序引起的罕见跃迁过程足以产生稳健的输运反常，为区分相互作用效应与无序驱动动力学提供了解析基准。

Abstract: We develop an analytical theory of anomalous transport in a noninteracting Anderson model with heavy-tailed hopping amplitudes. The broad distribution of hopping amplitudes gives rise to an extended intermediate-time regime with a subdiffusive effective exponent, despite the absence of interactions or genuine many-body effects. By solving the transport equations analytically, we derive the time dependence of the mean-square displacement and identify a continuous crossover from an intermediate anomalous regime to asymptotically diffusive transport. As the localization transition is approached, the spatial extent of the subdiffusive window diverges parametrically, while the crossover to conventional diffusion remains finite in units of $Γ_0^{-1}$. This produces an increasingly broad anomalous transport regime in space that can closely resemble Griffiths-type transport observed near the many-body localization transition. Our results demonstrate that rare hopping processes alone provide a microscopic single-particle mechanism for robust transport anomalies, establishing an analytical benchmark for distinguishing interaction-induced effects from disorder-driven dynamics.

</details>


### [156] [Skin-Anderson Localization Transition in Strongly Coupled Disordered Non-Hermitian Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11186)
*S Rahul*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究了无序与非厄米效应竞争下本征态的转变，发现通过调节非对称耦合可引发多次局域化转变，且非厄米皮肤效应可在线能隙拓扑下重新出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无序和非厄米非互易性在局域化现象中的竞争机制。

Method: 构建包含Hatano-Nelson链与厄米链耦合的双链模型，引入强无序和非对称链间跃迁，分析本征态演化。

Result: 发现调节链间耦合不对称性可导致皮肤局域-安德森局域-皮肤局域的连续转变；即使在具有线能隙拓扑的谱中，非厄米皮肤效应仍可恢复。

Conclusion: 非厄米皮肤效应可在超越传统点能隙的条件下稳健存在，揭示了其在强无序环境下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The interplay between disorder and non-Hermitian effects gives rise to a variety of intriguing localization phenomena. While disorder tends to localize the eigenstates through Anderson localization, non-Hermitian non-reciprocity promotes the formation of skin modes by driving the eigenstates toward the system boundaries giving rise to non-Hermitian skin effect (NHSE). In this work, we investigate the interplay between these competing mechanisms in a two-leg ladder consisting of a Hatano-Nelson chain coupled to a Hermitian chain via asymmetric inter-chain hopping, with strong disorder present in both chains. We show that tuning the asymmetry of the inter-chain coupling induces successive transitions in the nature of the eigenstates, from skin localization to Anderson localization and subsequently back to skin localization. Remarkably, the non-Hermitian skin effect re-emerges even though the energy spectrum exhibits a line-gap topology, demonstrating that robust skin localization can persist beyond the conventional point-gap regime.

</details>

### [157] [A Volumetrically Stabilized Mixed Formulation of the Finite Element Immersed Boundary Method for Fluid Structure Interaction with Fully Incompressible Hyperelastic Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10217)
*Abhishek Mishra,Paul T. Bauman*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合形式的分布拉格朗日乘子有限元浸入边界法（FEIBM），以恢复在模拟完全不可压缩超弹性材料时的体积稳定性，解决了直接有限元离散化导致的体积失稳和锁定问题，并在多个流固耦合基准测试中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统有限元浸入边界法在模拟完全不可压缩超弹性固体时出现的体积失稳和锁定问题，提升数值模拟的稳定性和准确性。

Method: 基于近似不可压缩超弹性理论，通过引入由体积应变能导出的体积应力分量，并增加一个弱施加的固体压力场作为拉格朗日乘子来强制满足J=1的不可压缩约束，构建混合形式的分布拉格朗日乘子FEIBM，并采用有限元空间离散和无条件稳定的半隐式时间格式进行求解。

Result: 该方法在三个流固耦合基准问题中得到验证，成功消除了未稳定格式中的体积失效现象，且对下落圆盘终端速度的计算结果与解析解误差在1%以内。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合形式FEIBM有效恢复了拉格朗日框架下的体积稳定性，显著提升了对不可压缩固体在流固耦合问题中模拟的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: The finite element immersed boundary method (FE IBM) with a distributed Lagrange multiplier is an attractive framework for fluid structure interaction (FSI) because it couples an Eulerian description of an incompressible fluid to a Lagrangian description of an immersed solid on two independent, nonconforming meshes, avoiding the costly remeshing required by body fitted arbitrary Lagrangian Eulerian methods. When the immersed solid is modelled as a fully incompressible hyperelastic material, however, a direct finite element discretization of the deviatoric solid stress fails to enforce the Lagrangian incompressibility constraint, and the computed structure exhibits spurious volumetric instabilities and locking. In this work we present a mixed formulation of the distributed Lagrange multiplier FEIBM that restores volumetric stability. Following the theory of nearly incompressible hyperelasticity, we augment the solid stress with a volumetric contribution derived from a dilatational strain energy and introduce an additional solid pressure field, enforced weakly, that plays the role of the Lagrange multiplier for the Lagrangian incompressibility constraint J = 1. The resulting formulation is discretized in space by finite elements and in time by an unconditionally stable semi implicit scheme, and is implemented as a reusable Immersed Boundary physics kernel within the GRINS multiphysics framework, built on the libMesh finite element library. The method is verified on three FSI benchmarks, an elliptically displaced thick ring returning to equilibrium, a radially stretched incompressible ring, and a disk falling under gravity in a viscous fluid, for which the mixed formulation removes the volumetric failure observed with the unstabilized formulation and reproduces the analytical terminal velocity of the falling disk to within 1%.

</details>


### [158] [Shape optimisation of nonlinear Naghdi shells on discrete geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10342)
*Ado Farsi,Alberto Paganini*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种将完全几何非线性壳体响应嵌入形状优化循环的自动化框架，能够在无需精确中面参数化的情况下实现高效优化，并通过多个基准测试验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性模型在有限挠度下无法准确反映薄壳结构的刚度变化，导致形状优化结果偏离真实最优解，因此需要引入几何非线性模型以提高优化精度。

Method: 采用五参数非线性Naghdi壳模型，结合部分选择性减缩积分抑制闭锁效应，直接在离散三角网格上运行并数值恢复法向场；利用Firedrake自动生成残差、切线刚度和伴随变量，通过算法微分计算形状导数，并驱动Fireshape/ROL信赖域优化器进行优化。

Result: 该框架成功复现了Sze/Abaqus和COMSOL的基准案例：在半圆柱壳点载荷问题中捕捉到渐进硬化现象，在钣金支架弯曲问题中实现了87%的应变能降低，并在曲面半圆柱壳上形成平滑加强褶皱，使平均挠度降低78%。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效集成了几何非线性壳体行为与形状优化，避免了对中面参数化的依赖，具备高精度和强鲁棒性，适用于复杂薄壳结构的设计优化。

Abstract: A thin shell carries load through its shape and, at finite deflections, its stiffness changes with the load itself, so a shape optimum found with a linear model can be far from optimal. We present an automated framework that embeds the fully geometrically nonlinear shell response in the shape-optimisation loop. The forward model, a five-parameter nonlinear Naghdi shell stabilised against locking by partial selective reduced integration, operates directly on a discrete (faceted) triangulation with a numerically recovered director field -- dispensing with the exact mid-surface parameterisation of isogeometric approaches, a chart that ceases to exist once the geometry itself is the design variable. Implemented in Firedrake, the model generates its residual, consistent tangent and adjoint automatically; shape derivatives, computed by algorithmic differentiation through the full load-continuation solve, drive the Fireshape/ROL trust-region optimiser. The forward solver reproduces the Sze/Abaqus benchmark for a clamped semi-cylindrical shell under a point load, capturing the progressive stiffening that a geometrically linear model cannot reproduce. The optimisation is validated against the COMSOL benchmark, a sheet-metal bracket under bending: the framework develops the same off-mid-plane corrugation mechanism and attains an 87% reduction of elastic strain energy within the prescribed displacement budget, matching the benchmark's magnitude and area change. Applied to the curved semi-cylinder, it forms a smooth stiffening crease that reduces the shell's average deflection under load by 78%.

</details>


### [159] [Efficient Weak-Entropy PINN for Solving Hyperbolic Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10389)
*Qi Gao,Kuang Huang,Xuan Di*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的Weak-Entropy PINN (WEPINN)框架，用于求解具有不连续解的双曲型守恒律，通过弱形式和熵条件有效捕捉间断和波的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络方法在求解不连续解偏微分方程时依赖强先验假设或引入影响精度的人工平滑项，难以准确预测间断的形成与传播。

Method: 采用控制方程的弱（积分）形式，结合熵条件选择物理可接受的解，并利用离散快速傅里叶变换（DFFT）实现高效数值积分。

Result: 在多种一维和二维标量及系统守恒律上进行实验，结果表明该方法能精确分辨尖锐间断，并有效捕捉多个激波与稀疏波之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: WEPINN为求解具有不连续解的双曲型守恒律提供了一种无需先验知识、精度高的神经网络方法。

Abstract: In recent years, neural networks have significantly advanced numerical solutions of partial differential equations (PDEs). However, solving PDEs with discontinuous solutions, such as hyperbolic conservation laws, remains challenging for neural network-based methods such as physics-informed neural networks (PINNs). Existing methods often rely on strong prior assumptions such as knowledge of discontinuity locations, or they introduce artificial smoothing terms that degrade accuracy. However, accurately solving these conservation laws and predicting the formation and propagation of discontinuities in solutions is crucial in many practical applications, including gas dynamics and traffic flow modeling. In this paper, we introduce a novel Weak-Entropy PINN (WEPINN) framework for hyperbolic conservation laws with discontinuous solutions. The method enforces the governing equations in their weak (integral) formulation and incorporates the entropy condition to select the physically admissible solution, while employing the discrete fast Fourier transform (DFFT) for efficient numerical integration. Our method is tested through extensive numerical experiments on a variety of scalar conservation laws and systems of conservation laws in one and two dimensional spaces. These experiments demonstrate that our method can accurately resolve sharp discontinuities while effectively capturing interactions between multiple shock and rarefaction waves.

</details>


### [160] [A fifth-order divergence-free finite difference Hermite WENO scheme for ideal magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10390)
*Peiwen Chen,Shi Jin,Jianxian Qiu,Zhuang Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种五阶有限差分无散度Hermite加权本质无振荡（HWENO）格式，用于求解理想磁流体动力学（MHD）方程，在保持高精度的同时有效维持磁场的无散度约束，显著提升了数值模拟的稳定性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁流体动力学模拟中的关键挑战是保持磁场的无散度约束，传统数值方法通常难以满足这一物理条件，导致模拟不稳定或失真，因此需要发展能同时保持高阶精度和无散度性质的新型数值方法。

Method: 采用HWENO框架求解MHD方程，并联合演化解及其空间偏导数；在时间推进的每一步中，先以高阶精度获得近似无散度的磁场，再通过一种简单的线性修正将散度误差均匀分配到各偏导数中，使磁场在离散意义上严格无散。

Result: 该方法在光滑区域可高阶保持磁场无散，在多种基准测试中表现出优异的稳定性、分辨率和计算效率，且修正过程仅引入高阶扰动，不破坏守恒性，计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 所提出的五阶HWENO格式结合偏导数修正策略，能够高效、精确且稳健地求解理想MHD方程，特别适用于要求严格无散度约束的复杂磁流体问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a fifth-order finite difference divergence-free Hermite weighted essentially non-oscillatory (HWENO) scheme for the ideal magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) equations. In this framework, both the solution and its spatial partial derivatives are evolved in time and jointly employed in the spatial reconstruction procedure. A major challenge in MHD simulations is preserving the divergence-free constraint of the magnetic field, which is generally violated by standard numerical methods designed solely for hyperbolic conservation laws. To address this issue, we first solve the MHD equations within the HWENO framework for hyperbolic conservation laws, yielding a magnetic field divergence that remains zero up to high-order accuracy in smooth regions. Subsequently, we apply a correction that evenly distributes the divergence error among the partial derivatives involved in the divergence-free constraint, thereby rendering the magnetic field discretely divergence-free at the new time level. This approach offers several advantages. First, the scheme retains the conservation property, as only the partial derivatives of the numerical solution are corrected, leaving the conserved variables unchanged. Second, the correction applied to the partial derivatives of the magnetic field components introduces only a high-order perturbation, thereby preserving the overall accuracy. Third, the divergence-free treatment significantly enhances robustness, as most benchmark test cases cannot be run stably without such a correction. Fourth, the correction is a simple linear operation applied at each stage of the time integration, incurring negligible additional computational cost. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the accuracy, resolution, efficiency, effectiveness, and robustness of the proposed scheme.

</details>


### [161] [High-order Energy-stable and Charge-conservative Lagrangian FEM for 3D Incompressible Inductionless MHD equations with Variable Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10487)
*Lingxiao Li,Bijie Song,Haiyan Su,Jie Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高阶、能量稳定且电荷守恒的混合有限元方法，用于求解变密度、不可压缩、无感应磁流体动力学方程中的移动界面问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决变密度、不可压缩MHD方程中移动界面的数值模拟难题，需要发展高精度、稳定且满足物理守恒律的数值方法。

Method: 采用拉格朗日框架下的高阶曲线网格技术来捕捉界面，结合BDF2时间离散化和AB2方法更新网格控制点；速度-压力对使用高阶等参Taylor-Hood元并加入散度梯度稳定项；电流密度采用高阶参数化H(div)-协调BDM元以保证离散电荷守恒。

Result: 所提方法具有高阶精度和无条件离散能量稳定性，数值实验验证了其准确性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效求解含移动界面的变密度MHD问题，兼具高阶精度、能量稳定性和电荷守恒特性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the solution of variable-density, incompressible inductionless magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) equations with moving interface. We propose a high-order, energy-stable and charge-conservative mixed finite element method in the Lagrangian framework. The method utilizes the moving high-order curved mesh techniques to resolve the interface, together with the second-order Backward Differentiation Formula (BDF2) for the temporal discretization of the velocity field and the second-order Adams--Bashforth method (AB2) for the update of control points of the meshes. High-order isoparametric Taylor-Hood element with grad-div stabilization are used for the velocity-pressure pair. Moreover, to ensure the discrete charge conservation, high-order parametric H(div)-conforming BDM element is adopted for the current density. We establish the unconditional discrete energy-stability for the fully discrete scheme. Numerical experiments validate the high-order accuracy and unconditional energy stability of the algorithm.

</details>


### [162] [A Quadratic $C^0$ Interior Penalty Method for the von Kármán Obstacle Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10507)
*Sharat Gaddam*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一种用于冯·卡门板位移障碍问题的二次C^0内罚方法，采用拉格朗日P2有限元并在顶点施加障碍约束，通过边上的修正项保证三线性形式在离散能量范数下的有界性，建立了离散问题的适定性，并证明了在离散能量范数下具有O(h^α)阶误差估计（1/2<α≤1），数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决冯·卡门板位移障碍问题中传统有限元方法难以处理非线性与约束耦合的问题，需要发展一种稳定且收敛的C^0连续有限元方法。

Method: 采用基于P2拉格朗日元的二次C^0内罚法，在顶点施加障碍约束，并对冯·卡门括号中的三线性形式在边上进行修正，使其在离散能量范数下保持有界；结合罚方法处理不可微约束，并分析其适定性与收敛性。

Result: 证明了离散问题解的存在性和数据较小时的唯一性，量化了离散能量范数下的索博列夫和弗里德里希常数对网格尺寸的依赖关系，获得了O(h^α)阶（1/2<α≤1）的误差估计，数值实验在方形和L形域上验证了收敛率，并观察到接触集的不同结构及罚参数对迭代求解的影响。

Conclusion: 该二次C^0内罚方法能有效求解冯·卡门板的位移障碍问题，具备良好的收敛性和稳定性，理论误差估计与数值结果一致，但罚参数的选择和障碍大小会影响求解性能。

Abstract: This article proposes and analyses a quadratic $C^0$ interior penalty method for the displacement obstacle problem of the von Kármán plate. The discrete space consists of Lagrange $P_2$ finite elements and the obstacle constraint is imposed at the vertices. The trilinear form of the von Kármán bracket is modified by terms on the edges so that it is bounded in the discrete energy norm. The well-posedness of the discrete problem, namely the existence of a discrete solution and its uniqueness under a smallness condition on the data, is established. The Sobolev and Friedrichs constants of the discrete energy norm are quantified with an explicit dependence on the mesh size. The main result is an error estimate of order $\mathcal{O}(h^α)$ in the discrete energy norm, where $1/2<α\le1$ is the index of elliptic regularity of the biharmonic operator on the polygonal domain. Numerical experiments on a square and on an L-shaped domain confirm the predicted rates. The coincidence set has positive measure in one example and empty interior in another. The experiments also show how the penalty parameter affects the rates and identify a threshold in the size of the obstacle beyond which the iterative solver fails on fine meshes.

</details>


### [163] [An efficient and robust fifth-order HWENO scheme with gradient reconstruction for compressible Navier--Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10569)
*Zijun Guo,Chuan Fan,Zhuang Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的五阶有限体积Hermite WENO格式，用于可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，具有高精度、鲁棒性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决在强激波或低密度等极端情况下传统方法可能出现的不稳定性和精度下降问题，需要发展一种既能保持高阶精度又能保证数值鲁棒性的新方法。

Method: 通过构造每个耗散变量的弱导数矩，并采用HWENO界面迹的算术平均值进行梯度重建；使用标准的非线性HWENO重构公式处理各分量，同时应用保正限制器以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法达到了五阶精度，具有高分辨率和良好的计算效率，能够在涉及强梯度的复杂流动中稳定运行。

Conclusion: 所提出的五阶HWENO格式在精度、稳定性与计算成本之间取得了良好平衡，适用于挑战性的有限雷诺数Navier-Stokes流动模拟。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an efficient and robust fifth-order finite-volume Hermite weighted essentially non-oscillatory (HWENO) scheme with gradient reconstruction for the compressible Navier--Stokes equations. The key idea is to construct weak-derivative moments for each dissipative variable by taking the arithmetic average of two HWENO interface traces, which are then processed componentwise through the standard scalar nonlinear HWENO reconstruction formula, with only the input moments differing from those used in the convective reconstruction, thereby supplying the gradients needed for the viscous fluxes. This reconstruction achieves fifth-order accuracy using only candidate polynomials of degree at most four, avoids the order reduction that would result from direct differentiation, and introduces negligible additional computational cost. To ensure robustness, especially in extreme test cases involving strong shocks or low densities, we apply a positivity-preserving limiter to the conservative states while retaining the nonlinear HWENO reconstruction for the viscous gradients. This combination is essential for stable computations in flows with strong gradients. Both the conservative and gradient reconstructions share the same compact stencils and candidate-polynomial structure, enabling a single implementation routine without introducing additional algorithmic complexity. Numerical results confirm that the proposed scheme delivers fifth-order accuracy and high resolution, offers competitive computational efficiency, and robustly resolves challenging finite-Reynolds-number Navier--Stokes flows in which the positivity-preserving limiter is actively engaged.

</details>


### [164] [A numerical approach for solving the time-Fractional Mobile-Immobile Transport Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10607)
*Sandip Maji*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了两种全离散数值格式，用于求解由Caputo导数描述的一维时间分数阶移动-非移动模型，并采用非对称内罚间断Galerkin方法进行空间离散，结合Crank-Nicolson L1和L2-1σ公式进行时间离散，实现了高阶精度。理论分析证明了格式的稳定性和最优收敛率，数值实验验证了方法的有效性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为准确模拟异质多孔介质中的反常溶质输运现象，特别是移动与非移动区域间质量传递引起的延迟传输行为，需要发展高效且稳定的数值方法来求解时间分数阶Mobile-Immobile模型。

Method: 采用非对称内罚间断Galerkin方法进行空间离散，结合Crank-Nicolson格式下的L1和L2-1σ公式对Caputo时间分数阶导数进行时间离散，构建了两个全离散数值格式，并进行了严格的稳定性与误差分析。

Result: 所提方法具有高阶时间精度，理论分析表明其收敛速率取决于精确解的正则性，数值实验结果与理论预测一致，验证了方法的效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的数值框架为求解分数阶移动-非移动模型提供了可靠、准确的工具，适用于复杂多孔介质中分数阶输运过程的模拟。

Abstract: Fractional diffusion models provide a powerful framework for describing anomalous transport phenomena in heterogeneous porous media. The Mobile-Immobile model is a fundamental approach for characterizing such anomalous diffusion, specifically addressing the delayed solute transport caused by mass transfer between mobile and immobile regions. In this study, we develop and analyze two fully discrete numerical schemes for the one-dimensional time-fractional Mobile-Immobile model governed by the Caputo derivative of order $α\in (0,1)$. The spatial discretization is carried out using a non-symmetric interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin method, while the temporal derivative is approximated by the Crank-Nicolson L1 and L2-$1_σ$ formulas, resulting in two distinct schemes with high-order accuracy. Rigorous stability and error analyses are established, showing that the methods achieve optimal convergence rates depending on the regularity of the exact solution. Numerical experiments verify the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the efficiency and robustness of the proposed schemes. The presented framework provides a reliable and accurate approach for simulating complex fractional transport processes in porous media and related fields.

</details>


### [165] [Uniform discrete Poincaré inequalities for Hybrid High-Order differential forms on polyhedral meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10713)
*Silvano Pitassi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于一般多面体网格上微分形式的混合高阶框架，通过局部Stokes公式重构外导数，并在任意空间维度下证明了在各种形式度下的统一离散Poincaré不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在一般多面体网格上构建适用于任意空间维度的微分形式的高效数值方法，提供一种统一的外微积分框架。

Method: 结合网格单元上的多项式微分形式与网格面上的多项式迹未知量，利用局部Stokes公式重构外导数，并采用混合L²范数分析稳定性与误差控制。

Result: 证明了在任意形式度k下离散Poincaré不等式的均匀性，该结果对网格尺寸和面空间的选择均具有一致性，并适用于任意拓扑结构的区域。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合高阶框架为微分形式提供了统一且稳定的数值方法，在任意维度和复杂网格下具有良好的理论保证和应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce a Hybrid High-Order framework for differential forms on general polyhedral meshes. For each form degree $k\in\{0,\ldots,n-1\}$, the discrete space combines polynomial differential forms on mesh cells with polynomial trace unknowns on mesh faces, and the exterior derivative is reconstructed through a local Stokes formula. In three dimensions, the corresponding vector proxies recover the usual hybrid gradient, curl, and divergence reconstructions, while the differential-form setting provides a unified exterior-calculus generalisation in arbitrary space dimension.
  Within this framework, we prove uniform discrete Poincaré inequalities at every form degree. The canonical broken--stabilised seminorm controls the distance, in the hybrid $L^2$ norm, to the full kernel of the prescribed reconstructed exterior derivative, uniformly with respect to the mesh size and to every stability-admissible choice of the face spaces. The result holds on domains with arbitrary topology.

</details>


### [166] [Successive Schur-Riesz Analysis for Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10757)
*Matthew Francis Dixon*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在希尔伯特空间中通过商化系数和控制正交创新来获得与层次无关的Riesz界的逼近方法，适用于包含因式分解算子的一般设定，并通过块Schur补识别内在新维度，实现了构造性增强过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统方法中由于精确冗余和强跨层相互作用导致的系数非唯一性问题，以及避免成对或对角优势检验过于悲观的问题。

Method: 在希尔伯特空间中，通过对表示相同函数的系数进行商化处理，并控制连续的正交创新，从而得到与m无关的Riesz界；使用块Schur补来识别内在的新维度并减少最佳逼近误差的平方。

Result: 获得了独立于层次m的Riesz界，能够准确识别新增的内在维度，并有效降低最佳逼近误差的平方；在非平稳和提升实例中给出了积极的内在边界，而这些情况下对角优势估计可能是负的或者标记的Gram矩阵是奇异的。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅克服了传统方法中的局限性，还为自适应计算和重用子空间提供了清晰的理论基础，突出了表示稳定性和特定应用效用的不同角色。

Abstract: Many approximation methods enlarge a trial space by adjoining function blocks generated by different operators. Exact redundancy and strong cross-level interaction can make coefficients nonunique and render pairwise or diagonal-dominance tests needlessly pessimistic. For \(V_m=\sum_{\ell\leq m}S_\ell(E_\ell)\) in a Hilbert space $\mathcal H$, we quotient coefficients representing the same function and control successive orthogonal innovations to obtain Riesz bounds independent of \(m\). The setting includes factored operators \(S_\ell=T_\ell\circ\cdots\circ T_1:E_\ell\to\mathcal H\), with compatible intermediate spaces, but the theorem allows arbitrary bounded \(S_\ell\). The same constants control approximation, truncation, perturbation, and levelwise error. A block Schur complement identifies the intrinsic new dimension and gives the exact reduction in squared best-approximation error, leading to a constructive enrichment procedure. Nonstationary and lifted examples give positive intrinsic bounds where diagonal-dominance estimates are negative or labelled Gram matrices are singular; adaptive and recycled-subspace calculations illustrate the distinct roles of representation stability and application-specific utility.

</details>


### [167] [A High-Order Galerkin Scheme for the Decoupled Potential Integral Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10799)
*David Scott Winterrose*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 证明了去耦势积分方程在Sobolev空间中的适定性，并通过高阶离散方案展示了良好的谱收敛性和条件数性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高电磁场积分方程求解的稳定性和效率，研究去耦势积分方程在Sobolev空间中的适定性及其数值方法。

Method: 使用基本的伪微分技术，分析L^2配对下的离散稳定性，并采用具有间断基函数或附加单元切向连续性的高阶格式进行数值求解。

Result: 获得了离散稳定性并推导出高阶格式的谱收敛性，且该方案相比成熟的商业EFIE/CFIE求解器具有更优的条件数，在多种情况下几乎与波数、网格密度和阶数无关。

Conclusion: 所提出的高阶方案在求解去耦势积分方程时表现出优异的数值性能和稳定性，适用于广泛的参数设置。

Abstract: We prove Sobolev space well-posedness of the decoupled potential integral equations. Using basic pseudo-differential techniques, discrete stability is obtained for the standard $L^2$-pairing, and we deduce spectral convergence for a high-order scheme employing discontinuous basis functions, or with additional element-wise tangential continuity imposed in the vectorial part of the system. The scheme is shown to be much better conditioned than a mature commercial EFIE/CFIE solver. In many cases appearing almost independent of wavenumber, mesh density, and the order.

</details>


### [168] [Locking analysis and high-order locking-free finite element method for three-dimensional electroporoelasticity equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10830)
*Xuan Liu,Yongkui Zou,Yanzhao Cao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维准静态电渗弹性方程在拉梅常数较大时的标准有限元离散中的泊松锁死现象，提出了一种五场公式和全离散高阶有限元方法，证明了该方法对大拉梅常数的均匀稳定性和无锁性，并通过数值实验验证了理论收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准协调有限元离散在拉梅常数较大时出现的严重泊松锁死问题，提高空间收敛阶。

Method: 引入五场公式和全离散高阶有限元方法，证明其对大拉梅常数的均匀稳定性，并推导关于拉梅系数的均匀误差估计。

Result: 建立了无锁方案，理论分析覆盖完全耦合的电磁-渗流弹性系统，适用于三维高阶单元，并通过大量数值实验验证了理论收敛率和对拉梅参数的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的五场高阶有限元方法能有效消除泊松锁死，实现对大拉梅常数的锁定自由计算，具有良好的理论保证和数值表现。

Abstract: Electroporoelasticity equations couple Maxwell's equations with Biot's poroelasticity model and admit severe Poisson locking in standard conforming finite element discretizations when the Lamé constant is large. In this paper, we provide a rigorous analysis of the Poisson locking phenomenon for three-dimensional quasi-static electroporoelasticity equations and show that the spatial convergence order of conforming finite element approximations is reduced in the nearly incompressible regime. To eliminate this locking effect, we introduce a five-field formulation and a fully discrete high-order finite element method. We prove uniform stability with respect to large Lamé constant. Based on this, we establish a locking-free scheme by deriving its uniform error estimates with respect to the Lamé coefficient. The analysis covers the fully coupled electromagnetic-poroelastic system and applies to high-order elements in three dimensions. Extensive numerical experiments are presented to verify the theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate robustness with respect to the Lamé parameter.

</details>


### [169] [Adaptive Time-Stepping Euler--Maruyama Scheme for SDEs with Non-Globally Lipschitz Coefficients: Uniform Convergence, Stability and Ergodicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10855)
*Xueqi Wen,Shan Huang,Xiaoyue Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应时间步长的Euler-Maruyama方法，用于求解具有非全局Lipschitz系数的随机微分方程，证明了其在有限时间和无限时间下的1/2阶强收敛性、矩稳定性及不变测度的多项式遍历性和最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非全局Lipschitz条件下传统固定步长方法易导致数值不稳定的问题，设计一种能动态调节步长的数值方法以提高稳定性和精度。

Method: 采用自适应时间步长策略，在每一步迭代中根据系统状态动态调整步长，结合Euler-Maruyama格式构造数值解，并通过严格数学分析证明其收敛性和稳定性。

Result: 证明了数值解的矩有界性和1/2阶强收敛率，保持原SDE的pth矩指数稳定性；对长时间遍历系统，建立了数值不变测度的多项式遍历性及其在Lq-Wasserstein距离下以最优1/2阶收敛到真实不变测度。

Conclusion: 该自适应方法有效避免了数值不稳定问题，在精度、稳定性和长期动力学保持方面优于现有方法，适用于非全局Lipschitz条件下的SDE模拟。

Abstract: This paper develops an adaptive time-stepping Euler--Maruyama scheme for stochastic differential equations (SDEs) with non-globally Lipschitz drift and diffusion coefficients. By dynamically adjusting the timestep at each iteration, the proposed scheme effectively prevents numerical instability. We prove the moment boundedness of the numerical solution and establish a $1/2$-order strong convergence rate both on finite-time intervals and uniformly in time. Furthermore, the scheme faithfully inherits the $p$th moment exponential stability of the underlying SDE. For long-time ergodic dynamics, we establish the polynomial ergodicity of the numerical invariant measure. Moreover, we show that the numerical invariant measure converges to the invariant measure of the underlying SDE at an optimal rate of $1/2$ in the $L^q$-Wasserstein distance. Numerical experiments confirm our theoretical results and indicate the superior accuracy and computational performance of the proposed scheme over several fixed-step and existing adaptive methods.

</details>


### [170] [Diffusion Quasi-Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11055)
*Jianlong Chen,Yifeng Yu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了在复杂目标测度下，利用基于扩散的传输映射和随机拟蒙特卡洛（RQMC）进行高维数值积分的方法。通过组合高斯基变换与欧拉离散化的概率流常微分方程，构建从单位立方体到目标分布的映射，并采用重要性采样保留无偏性。理论结果表明，在温和条件下，该方法可实现O(N^{-1+ε})的均方根误差，实验验证了其在高维问题中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数值积分在复杂目标分布下具有挑战性，传统蒙特卡洛方法收敛较慢。本文旨在结合扩散生成模型与RQMC，提升积分精度并提供理论保障。

Method: 使用得分-based扩散模型诱导的概率流ODE将简单先验映射到目标分布，构造从单位立方体到目标的传输映射；结合分量逆高斯CDF与欧拉离散化概率流ODE，并在单位立方体上采用重要性采样以保持无偏性。

Result: 提出了可验证条件，确保IS被积函数满足边界增长条件，从而对scrambled nets实现O(N^{-1+ε}) RMSE；在2D混合分布、784D图像及40,960D涡度同化任务中验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为结合扩散生成模型与高精度RQMC积分提供了理论与实验基础，展示了在高维复杂积分问题中显著提升采样效率与估计精度的潜力。

Abstract: We study high-dimensional numerical integration with respect to complex target measures using diffusion-based transport maps and randomized quasi-Monte Carlo (RQMC). Score-based diffusion models induce a deterministic probability flow ODE that transports a simple prior to the target, suggesting a principled way to transform low-discrepancy points on the unit cube into informative samples. We construct a cube-to-target map by composing a Gaussian base transformation (the component-wise inverse Gaussian CDF) with an Euler-discretized probability flow ODE. To retain unbiasedness under transport approximation, we formulate integration as importance sampling (IS) on the cube. Our main result provides verifiable conditions under which the resulting IS integrand satisfies the boundary growth condition, implying an $O(N^{-1+ε})$ RMSE for scrambled nets. We then establish these conditions for diffusion probability-flow transport under mild bounded-derivative assumptions on the learned vector field, explicitly controlling the boundary singularities introduced by the inverse Gaussian CDF. Experiments range from a 2D mixture to 784D images and a 40,960D conditional vorticity-assimilation task; in the latter, blocked scrambled Sobol' sampling reduces the randomization standard deviation of nonlinear accuracy metrics at essentially unchanged online denoising cost. Together, these results give a theoretical and empirical foundation for combining diffusion generative modeling with high-precision RQMC integration.

</details>


### [171] [Randomized Tucker-Sketched GMRES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11091)
*Alberto Bucci,Martina Iannacito,Mirjeta Pasha,Rudi Smith*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了两种基于随机化的GMRES算法（RHOSVD-Tucker sGMRES 和 MLN-Tucker sGMRES），用于高效求解大规模Tucker格式张量线性系统，克服了传统迭代法中Krylov基向量多线性秩增长导致的计算与内存瓶颈，并在对称与非对称问题中均优于标准低秩求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GMRES等迭代求解器在处理大规模Tucker格式张量系统时，因Krylov基向量的多线性秩随迭代增长，导致计算代价和内存需求迅速上升，难以扩展。

Method: 提出两种基于sketched GMRES框架的随机化算法：RHOSVD-Tucker sGMRES采用带迭代内秩选择的随机HOSVD，提升鲁棒性；MLN-Tucker sGMRES采用固定秩的多线性Nyström近似，支持流式计算，并可通过压缩的草图表示高效重构解。

Result: 两种方法在对称与非对称张量系统中均优于标准低秩Tucker求解器；结合自适应投影Tikhonov正则化与自动参数选择，能稳定求解逆问题。

Conclusion: 所提随机化算法有效缓解了大规模张量线性系统的计算与内存瓶颈，兼具高效性、稳定性与内存友好性，适用于复杂逆问题的低秩正则化解。

Abstract: We address the problem of solving large-scale tensor-structured linear systems in the Tucker format. In this setting, standard iterative solvers such as GMRES face a fundamental bottleneck: the multilinear ranks of the Krylov basis vectors grow with the iteration count, leading to rapidly increasing tensor operation costs and memory requirements. To overcome these challenges, we propose two randomized algorithms within the sketched GMRES framework that replace full Arnoldi orthogonalization with short recurrences. The first, RHOSVD-Tucker sGMRES, uses randomized HOSVD with per-iteration rank selection, providing robustness across a wide range of problems. The second method, MLN-Tucker sGMRES, leverages the multilinear Nyström approximation with a fixed rank, enabling streaming computations; the streamability of the approximation further allows, at no additional cost, a memory-efficient reconstruction of the solution from a compact sketched representation of the Krylov basis. Both methods outperform standard low-rank Tucker solvers in symmetric and non-symmetric settings. Applied to inverse problems, the low-rank Tucker constraint acts as an implicit regularizer; combined with adaptive projected Tikhonov penalization and automatic regularization parameter selection, the methods yield stable reconstructions.

</details>


### [172] [How different is rational approximation from piecewise polynomial approximation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11120)
*Daan Huybrechs*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了有理函数与分段多项式在逼近具有点奇异性的一元函数时的相似性与差异，指出两者虽能达到相近的收敛速度，但有理逼近具有更小的常数因子，并通过现代数值方法验证其在多变量情形下优势更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨有理函数与分段多项式在函数逼近中的相对优劣，特别是在存在奇异性的情况下，以澄清实践中对多项式更广泛使用的合理性。

Method: 回顾经典文献中关于两类方法收敛性的理论结果，并结合最新的实用有理逼近技术进行数值比较，扩展到多变量情形进行实验验证。

Result: 理论和数值结果表明，有理函数与分段多项式在逼近具有点奇异性的一元函数时收敛速度相近，但有理逼近的常数更小；在多变量情况下，该优势更加明显。

Conclusion: 尽管多项式更常用，但在特定函数类（如含奇异性函数）的逼近中，有理函数更具优势，尤其在现代高效算法支持下，其性能优于分段多项式。

Abstract: The first aim of this paper is to show that there is merit to the question posed in the title. Indeed, for certain function classes, approximation by rational functions and by piecewise polynomials are surprisingly similar. Between these two, polynomials are more widely studied and more widely used. One setting in which both approaches are equally well understood, and equally practical in use, is that of approximating univariate functions with point singularities. In this context we can fully address the question. We review classical literature on the topic which shows that both approaches do indeed achieve similar convergence rates. However, rational approximations come with significantly smaller constants. Owing to recent advances in practical rational approximation, we can augment the discussion with a comparison of modern numerical techniques that achieve the optimal rates. We end by showing numerically that the difference becomes even more pronounced in several variables.

</details>
